veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉai -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vittai ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. atai muḷaikkac ceytavar paritimāl kalaiñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟaimalaiyām maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭaiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟaiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkaiḷai nilai nāṭṭiya malaiyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavai eḻutiyavai aṉaittum meymmaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭait tam āyvu matukaiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuraiyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uraiyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavai alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkkai añciṟait tumpiyāka avaṟṟai yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉai akara niral tokaiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kālaiyil, tamiḻ moḻiyaiyum tamiḻ maṇṇaiyum tamiḻ iṉattaiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmaiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uraiyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉai vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉaivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkaruttai yāṉ uraikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷai amaittār.
tēvanēyar paṭaippukaḷ aṉaittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷait tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amaikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakaiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷaiyum kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimaiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭaikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉaivar kaikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya nilaiyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpatai nilaip paṭuttum vakaiyil avarkkup paṭaiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉaivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷaiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumaiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭaiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!							iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!						    irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



patippurai
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇaiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutaiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatait tam paṉmoḻip pulamaiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ aṉaittaiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatait tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talaippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakaiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭaitta vairaccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷai veḷiyiṭu vatil perumaip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlattai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

tamiḻ vaḷarcci
i. urainaṭai 
kiḷaviyākkam
1. coṟṟirivu muṟaikaḷ
kiḷaviyiṉ iyalviḷakkam : mūvakaikkiḷavi - ōreḻuttu, īreḻuttu, palaveḻuttu.
kiḷavi eṉpatu nālvakai ilakkaṇaviyaṟ colliṟkum potuvāṉa col. kiḷattal collutal. kiḷappatu kiḷavi.
māntaṉ nākarikam vaḷara vaḷarak karuttukkaḷ palkukiṉṟaṉa. aṭippaṭaik karuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ kiḷaikkumpōtu, muṉṉavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ palvēṟu vakaiyil tiripaṭaikiṉṟaṉa. allākkāl palaporuḷoru collum (Polysemy) pulacolloru vaṭivum (Homonymy) mikkup poruḷ mayakku ēṟpaṭum. atait taṭukkac col vaṭivai māṟṟa vēṇṭiyuḷatu. cuḷ eṉṉum aṭi poruntaṟ poruḷiṟ ceḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ cuṭutalaiyum uṟaittalai yum kuṟikkuñ colloṭu atai mayakka nērum. ceḷ eṉṉum vaṭiviṉiṉṟē ceṇṭu, ceṇṭai, ceṭi, ceṇṇu, ceru, ceṟu, ceṟi, ceṟṟai, ceḻi, cēr mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa. nuḷ eṉpatu neruṅkaṟporuḷil naḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ, kiḷḷu talaik kuṟikkum colloṭu atai mayakka nērum. mēlum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa karuttukkaṭku vevvēṟu collum col vaṭivum vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, vērccollum aṭiccollum palvēṟu tiripukoṇṭā loḻiya moḻivaḷarccikkiṭa millai.
(1) aṟuvakaittiripu
e.ṭu.
valittal 	:	ūṅku - ūkku, kuṇṭu - kuṇṭamkuṭṭam; 
		nantu - nattai, kuṟiñci - kuṟicci.
melittal	:	oppu - ompu, kuttu - kuntu. pōkku - pōṅku.
nīṭṭal 	: 	uṇ-ūṇ, kuṭa-kuṭā, nakarakam-nākarikam.

kuṟukkal 	: 	āṅku-aṅku, tēvu-teyvam, vaṇaṅku- vāṅku- vaṅku-
		vaṅki=vaḷaintakatti,  neḷivaḷaiyal.
tokuttal 	: 	turutti - tutti, peṭṭai - peṭai, 
			veymmai - vemmai, vēyntōṉ -
			vēntaṉ.
virittal 		: 	paravar - paratavar, māṭam-māṭakam.
(2) mukkuṟai
mūtaṟkuṟai		:	cippi-ippi, nīram-īram ukai-kai kaittal 
			= celuttutal.

kaṭaikkuṟai 	: 	cāy-cā, nal-na.
(3) mummikai
mutaṉmikai 	:	ēṇ-cēṇ, iḷai-ciḷai.
iṭaimikai 		:	ilaku-ilaṅku, piṟaku-piṟakku, mūcu-mūñcu, 
			metu-mettu.
kaṭaimikai 		: 	tirum-tirumpu, kaṭai-kaṭaici.
tokuttalum virittalum pakukiḷavikkum, iṭaik kuṟaiyum iṭaimikai yum pakākkiḷavikkum, uriyaveṉa vēṟupāṭaṟika. āyiṉum ivvēṟupāṭu mutaṟkālac coṉṉilaikku ēṟkātu.

mōṉaittiripu
paci-paccai, pāci, paitu.
kiḷ-kiṇṭu, kīḷ, keṇṭu, kēṇi.
uṭu-uṭaṉ, oṭu, ōṭu.
uyirttiripukaḷellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, u-o, u-a, u-i eṉṉum mūṉṟum palateṉcoṟkaḷiṉ mūlaṅkāṇavum, teṉ collā vaṭacollā eṉṉum aiyattiṟkiṭamāṉa cila coṟkaḷait teṉcolleṉṟu tuṇiyavum, peruntuṇaiyāyiruttalāl, mika mutaṉmai vāyntaṉavākum. ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē, muṉṉait tiripu, aḷḷaittiripu. piṉṉaittiripu eṉa aḻaikkalām. muṉṉaittiripum mōṉaittiripum oṉṟē.

palvakai īṟukaḷ
poruḷ māṟumpōtu collum māṟavēṇṭumeṉpatu, collākka aṭippaṭai neṟimuṟai, allākkāl, poruḷ atuvō ituvō eṉa mayaṅka nērum. maṇ eṉṉuñ col maṇalaik kuṟittaṟku allīṟu peṟṟu maṇal eṉṟāṉamaiyum, kampu eṉṉuñ col, marakkōlaik kuṟiyātu kaṉiya (ulōka)k kōlaik kuṟittaṟku ikara vīṟu peṟṟuk kampiyeṉ ṟāṉamaiyum, kāṇka. pakkattaiyum iṭuppaiyum kuṟikkum maruṅku eṉṉum col, maruṅkal eṉa ullīṟu peṟṟē taṉippaṭa iṭuppaik kuṟittatu.
mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya īṟukaḷ, cuṭṭukkaḷum cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷumākavēyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ, mutaṟkālattil peyareccamāka maṭṭumaṉṟip peyarākavum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. M = mJ (bg.), anta (pe.e.) ikkālattum atu pōtu eṉṟu, cuṭṭup peyar peyareccamāy vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
uyirolikaḷait taṉit tolikkum pōtu, kuṟiliṉum neṭilē iḷañciṟārkkum muntiyal māntarkkum eḷitām. kuṟilai viṭṭolikkum pōtu mūccuppai muyaṟci mikku vēṇṭiyirut taliṉ, kīḻvakuppu māṇavar kuṟil neṭil vēṟupāṭiṉṟiyē uyireḻuttukkaḷai olittup pōtal kāṇka. itaṉāl uyireḻuttukkaḷ mutalil neṭilākavē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum.
a i u am mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu  (31)
eṉṟu kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār, caṟṟup piṉpu,
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōrēḻut torumoḻi  (43)
kuṟṟeḻut taintum moḻiniṟai pilavē  (44)
eṉṟu atai orumaruṅku maṟuttiruppatum, viṉāveḻuttuk kaḷaik kuṟikkumiṭattu.
ā ē o am mūṉṟum viṉāa  (32)
eṉa ekarattai viṭṭiruppatum, tolkāppiyam neṭukalum āyiṭai eṉṉum toṭarē payiṉṟu varuvatum, āṇṭu īṇṭu eṉṉum coṟkaṭkuk kuṟukiya vaṭiviṉmaiyum, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku iṉakkuṟil pintit tōṉṟi yuḷḷamaiyum, itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum.
cuṭṭīṟukaḷ
ā-uṇā, nilā, irā, viḷā, kaḷā, nuṇā, kaṭā, miḻā.
ī-kurī (kurīi = kuruvi).
ū - koḷ - koḷum - koḷumū - koṇmū (mukil).
ciṟu paṟavai (kuruvi) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum kurī eṉṉum collaip piṟkālattār kurīi eṉa aḷapeṭaiyākki viṭṭaṉar.
koṇmū - kaṭalnīraik koḷḷum mukil. koḷḷutal = mukattal. koḷumutal = mukattal.
āvīṟu muṟaiyē a, avu eṉat tiriyum,
e.ṭu. nilā - nilavu, irā-ira-iravu, kaḷā-kaḷa-kaḷavu. avu īṟu avam eṉṟum tiriyum.
e.ṭu. arā - ara - aravu - aravam, kaḷā - kaḷa - kaḷavu - kaḷavam.
mutaṟkālattil neṭilākavēyirunta muccuṭṭukkaḷum piṟkālattil kuṟilākavum māṟiṉa.
e.ṭu.	ā - a, ātu - atu, āṅku - aṅku.
		ī - i, ītu - itu, īṅku - iṅku.
		ū - u, (ūtu) - utu, ū - uṅku.
eḻuttukkaḷ taṉinilaiyiṉṟip puṇarnilaippaṭṭuc coṟ kaḷākum pōtu, neṭil vaṭiviṉuṅ kuṟilvaṭivē palukka (uccarikka) eḷitām.
īkāra ūkārac cuṭṭīṟukaḷ ikkālattu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟil vaṭivukaḷē iṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
e.ṭu.	a - uṇa, nila, viḷa, ira, (ce. va.)
		i - kaṇṇi, kiḷi, nari, puli, uṟumi, koṭi, veḷḷi, 
	u - koḻu (kalappaik kāṟu), karu (cūl), urumu, varaku.
cila akaravīṟu avu eṉṟu tiriyum.
e.ṭu.	kuḻa - kuḻavu, maḻa - maḻavu.
cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ
ellā meykaḷuḷḷum iyalpāṉatum eḷitāṉatum makara mātaliṉ, mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu makaramey yīṟṟatē. atu piṉṉarp piṟa meyyīṟṟatākat tirintum, iṟutiyilum iṭaiyilum vēṟeḻuttup peṟṟu virintum, ēṉaiyīṟu kaḷōṭu kūṭiyum, palvēṟu taṉiyīṟukaḷaiyum kūṭṭīṟukaḷaiyum piṟappittuḷḷatu.
am ikkālattil taṉiyīṟu; mutaṟkālattil kūṭṭīṟu (a+m)
am - aṉ - al. e.ṭu. tiṟam - tiṟaṉ - tiṟal.
uyirum uyir meyyumākiya īṟukaḷellām coṟkaḷum coṟ citaivukaḷumē yātaliṉ, attakai īṟu peṟṟa collellām mutaṟkālattil iru collākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
e.ṭu. uṇā (uṇ + ā) = uṇṇum atu, uṇṇum poruḷ.
meyyīṟṟu muccuṭṭaṭi yīṟukaḷ

nālvakai yīṟukaḷ
taṉiyīṟu, kūṭṭīṟu, tiriyīṟu, viriyīṟu, eṉa īṟukaḷ nālvakaip paṭum. am pōṉṟatu taṉiyīṟu; ampu (am+pu), ampam (am+pu+am) pōṉṟavai kūṭṭīṟu. aṉ, al, aḷ, aḻ, ar, atu, aṟu eṉpaṉavum ivaṟṟiṟ kotta ikara vukara mutalīṟukaḷum tiriyīṟu; akku (aku-akku), attu (atu-attu) pōṉṟaṉa viriyīṟu.

aku, acu, aṭu, atu, apu, avu, aṟu, akku, accu, aṭṭu, attu, appu, aṟṟu, aṇ, am, ar, al, aḻ, aḷ, aṉ eṉṉum īṟukaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟkotta ikaravukara mutal vaṭivukaḷumē, iṉṟu muṟaiyē ku, cu, ṭu, tu, pu, vu, ṟu, kku, ccu, ṭṭu, ttu, ppu, ṟṟu, ṇ, m, r, l, ḻ, ḷ, ṉ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivaiyellām uṇmaiyiṟ cuṭṭaṭiyiṉavākum.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṉ, aḷ, ar, atu, avai, a eṉpaṉa pālīṟukaḷalla; aṇ, aḻ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟu iyalpāṉa potuvīṟukaḷē.
cila iṭappeyarkaḷ cilaviṭattuc cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ pōl tōṉṟum. avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
e.ṭu.	cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu : akam - kaḻakam eṉpatiṟpōl oru 
					  peyarīṟu (aku + am).
		iṭappeyar 	:	akam = maṉai, iṭam, kallakam = 
					kalviccālai (kaṟkumiṭam), kaṉ 
					maṉai, kalluḷḷa iṭam.
		cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu	:	iṭam-ōr īṟu. v.L.: kaṭṭiṭam =  maṉai
		iṭappeyar	:	iṭam-oru peyarccol.
				e.ṭu. iruppiṭam = irukkumiṭam.
				kaṭṭiṭam = kaṭṭukiṉṟa iṭam.
ōr īṟu pala vakaiyil tōṉṟalām.
e.ṭu.	avu    :  irā-ira-iravu, cel-celavu.
	aṉ   :  am-aṉ, al-aṉ.
	antu :  am-antu, atu-antu, attu-antu.
pala coṟkaḷil lakaram takaramākat tirivatālum, mel eṉṉuñ col metu eṉṟu tirintiruppatālum atu eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu al eṉpataṉ tiripākavē koḷḷap paṭṭatu. aḵtu eṉpatu atu eṉat tokkateṉṟu koḷvataṟ kiṭamuṇṭēṉum, atu eṉat tokkateṉṟu koḷvataṟkiṭamuṇṭēṉum, atu eṉpataṉ vali iraṭṭiya vaṭivamāṉa attu eṉpatu paḵtu eṉpa tōṭoppa āytamiṭaiyiṭṭa maṟuvaṭivu koṇṭateṉpatu poruttamām. metu metteṉṟāyatu pōl atu aḵteṉṟāyatu; patu patteṉṟāyatu. āytattai yaṭutta takaram, aḵṟiṇai, kaḵṟītu, paḵṟi, paḵṟuḷi eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl ṟakaramākat tirivatē puṇarcciyilakkaṇa mātalāl aḵtu, paḵtu, eṉpavaṟṟiluḷḷa āytam iyalpāṉa iṭaiyeḻuttaṉ ṟeṉṟum, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa iṭaikkālat tiripeṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
collīṟukaḷ tiriyumpōtu, meli valiyākalām, vali meliyākalām.
e.ṭu. nīṅku-nīkku, pōkku-pōṅku.
palvakaik kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ
accam, viraivu, ōcai, oḷi, vaṇṇam, acaivu, ūṟu, mutaliya palvēṟu poruḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ, ōroḻuṅku paṭṭa īṟukaḷaik koṇṭirā viṭiṉum, makkaḷ karuttait teḷivāyp pulappaṭuttaṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa 
vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
accakkuṟippu - kapīr. tiṭukku, tuṇukku, verukku.
viraivukkuṟippu - avakku, kupīl-kupīr, carēl-carēr, tiṭum, 
tiṭīr, paṭakku, pucukku, pocukku, vicukku, viruṭṭu, veṭukku.
ōcaikkuṟippu - kaṇīr, kiṟiccu, kīccu, caḷār, taṭāl - taṭār, turuṭṭu, puḷiccu, paṭār, molōr.
oḷikkuṟippu - paḷiccu, paḷīr, miṉukku.
vaṇṇakkuṟippu - civīr, cevēr, veḷēl - veḷēr.
acaivukkuṟippu - kiṇukku, kiṇukkaṭṭi, cavakku, totukkup putukku.
ūṟṟukkuṟippu - kaṭukku, cuṟukku, cuḷīr, parapara, potukku, noḷukku, metukku.
olikkuṟippillāta coṟkaḷellām iyaṟcoṟkaḷalla; īṟu peṟṟa tiricoṟkaḷē.
e.ṭu. miṉ-miṉukku, melu-melukku, paḷ-paḷiccu, veḷ-veḷēr.
valiyiraṭṭal
e.ṭu. paku - pakkam, pēcu - pēccu, neṭu - neṭṭai, karutu - karuttu, kuṟu - kuṟṟam, kuṟṟi, neṟu neṟṟi.
eḻuttiraṭṭal (Germination or Doubling of Consonants) eṉṉum collamaiti, āriya moḻik kuṭumpak kiḷaikaḷum airōppāviṉ vaṭamēlaik kōṭiyiluḷḷatum, āṅkilattai uḷḷiṭṭatumāṉa, tiyūttāṉiyattiṟkē ciṟappāṉateṉṟu (i)riccārṭu māricu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
taṉikkuṟilaiyaṭutta mey uyirōṭu puṇarumpōtu iraṭṭuvatu, tamiḻukkum āṅkilattiṟkum potuvām.
e.ṭu. tamiḻ - kaṇ + i = kaṇṇi, koḷ + ai = koḷḷai, il + am = illam, iṉ + um = iṉṉum.
E. thin + er = thinner, sit + ing = sitting.
palvakait tiri coṟkaḷ
viḷ
viḷ - viḷa, viḷattu, viḷampu.
viḷ - viṭu - viṭar, viṭai, viṭuti, viṭal.
viḷ - vir - viri, virivu, viral, virippu.
viḷ - vil - viyaṉ. (l)
viḷar - viḷā, viḷa, viḷavu, viḷavam, viḷatti

pōli (Interchange of Letters)
oru collil, ōriṭattil, oru taṉiyeḻuttiṟku allatu iṇaiyeḻuttiṟkup pakaramāka (patilāka) muṟaiyē maṟṟoru taṉiyeḻuttu allatu iṇaiyeḻuttu vantu, poruḷ māṟā tiruppatu pōliyām. pōliruppatu pōli. pōlutal - ottal. iṭam nōkki, mutaṟpōli, iṭaip pōli, kaṭaip pōli eṉap pōli mūvakaippaṭum.
e.ṭu.	     mutaṟpōli 	: nāyiṟu - ñāyiṟu, maṭali - vaṭali;
		iṭaippōli 	: kuṭavaṉ - kucavaṉ - kuyavaṉ;
			neyavu	: necavu, aracu - araicu, nīntu - nīñcu;
		kaṭaippōli	: kaṭam - kaṭaṉ, kūtal - kūtar.
poruḷ māṟātirukkum pōliyāl, ceyyuḷil etukai yamaippiṟkaṉṟi moḻivaḷarccikkiṭamillai. āyiṉ pōli muṟaiyait taḻuvip poruḷ vēṟupaṭum coṟkaḷ moḻiyai mika vaḷampaṭuttum.
e.ṭu. : tarukku - cerukku, paku-vaku, mīṟu-vīṟu, katali-kacaḷi, kuḻal- kuṭal, paḻam-payam-payaṉ, vāyil-vācal, mottai-moccai, iṭam-iṭaṉ, nāḷi-nāḻi, nāḷi-nāṭi.
ilakkaṇappōli (Metathesls)
oru taṉiccolliṉ eḻuttukkaḷum oru kūṭṭuc colliṉ uṟuppukkaḷ allatu uṟuppuccoṟkaḷum, muṉpiṉṉāka muṟai māṟi variṉ ilakkaṇap pōli eṉappaṭum.
e.ṭu. 	taṉiccol 	alari-araḷi,	koppuḷam -
		pokkuḷam,  	catai-tacai,  	ñimiṟu-miñiṟu
		viciṟi-civiṟi.
kūṭṭuccol
ilvāy - vāyil, ilmuṉṟu - mūṉṟil - muṟṟam, katuvāli - kavutāri tāṉaimuṉ - muṉṟāṉai.
mūvakaic coṟkiṭakkai
taṉiccol, kūṭṭuccol, toṭarccol eṉac coṟkiṭakkai mūvakaippaṭum. avai muṟaiyē oru collum iru collum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pala collumākum.
e.ṭu. nilam, taṇṇīr, ceṅkāl nārai, vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ.
kūṭṭuccol vakaikaḷ
1. iraṭṭaikkiḷavi	e.ṭu.	kalakala, viruviru
2. iraṭṭitta col	e.ṭu.	cekkac cevēr, ciṉṉañciṟu
3. aṭukkut toṭar	e.ṭu.	palapala, tirumpat tirumpa
4. mīmicaic col	e.ṭu.	kaṉṟukkuṭṭi, ōṅkiyuyarnta
5. iru peyaroṭṭu	e.ṭu	kumarimalai, makkaṭcelvam
6. mikavuccol	e.ṭu.	āṇmakaṉ, peṇ peṇṭāṭṭi
7. muraṇpaṭucol	e.ṭu.	peṇmakaḷ, peṭṭaip pacaṉkaḷ
8. iṇaimoḻi	e.ṭu.	kāykaṟi, vāḻvu tāḻvu
9. aṭaipeṟṟa col	e.ṭu.	naṉcey, talaikkaḻakam
10. kiḷaviyam
	(eccattoṭar)	e.ṭu.	āṭci māṟi, intiyoḻiyiṉ
11. toṭariyam 	e.ṭu.	tamiḻ vāḻum, tamiḻaṉ uyarvāṉ (muṟṟuttoṭar)
aṭukkuttoṭar oṟṟiṭai mikkum tirintum tokkum varum.
e.ṭu. palappala, paṟpala, meṉmēlum, nannāṉku, vevvēṟu, cila coṟkaḷ taṉiccoṟkum kūṭṭuccoṟkum potuvāyirukkum.
oru poruḷ tantu oru collāyum iruporuḷ tantu iru collāyum iruppatu potuccol.
aḷittēṉ = tantēṉ (orucol), vaṇṭuttēṉ (irucol).
ceyvōṉ = purivāṉ (orucol), ceyaṟkai maḻai (irucol).
toṭarccol vakaikaḷ
1. aṭukkukkiḷavi	e.ṭu.	kuṭukuṭukuṭu
2. mīmicaiccol	e.ṭu.	mutupaḻantoṉ (moḻi)
3. aṭukkuttoṭar	e.ṭu.	tīttīttī
4. mikavuccol	e.ṭu.	araiñāṇkayiṟu, peṇ 
				piḷḷaip piḷḷai
5. aṭaipeṟṟacol	e.ṭu.	tūṅkeyileṟinta, toṭittōṭ
				cempiyaṉ, maturaik 
				kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīrar
6. kiḷaviyam	e.ṭu.		tamiḻaṉ urimaipeṟa vēṇṭiṉ
7. toṭariyam	e.ṭu.		intiya araciyaṟ caṭṭattai 
				māṟṟiyamaittal vēṇṭum
poruṭkēṟṟa oliyamaippu
potuvāka, mellōcaic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ meṉmaiyaiyum  vallōcaic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ vaṉmaiyaiyum, uṇarttum.
e.ṭu. kuttutal = cataiyuḷḷa iṭattil muṭṭiyāl allatu kattiyāl tākkutal. kuṭṭutal = vaṉmaiyāṉa maṇṭaiyil muṭṭiyāl tākkutal.
muttutal = mukattōṭu allatu vēṟuṟuppoṭu mukam mellap poruntutal. muṭṭutal = maṇṭaiyoṭu maṇṭai iṭittal.
arital = ciṟitāy naṟukkutal. aṟuttal = peritāy naṟukkutal. arivatu arivāḷ; aṟuppatu aṟuvāḷ.
kaṟittal = kaṉṟu iḷampullai metuvāyk kaṭittal.
kaṭittal = peritāṉa vilaṅku mutirnta payirai valitākak kaṭittal.
caṉṉam = melliyatu. tiṇṇam = tiraṇṭatu.
kuñcu = mellitāṉa iḷampaṟavai. kuccu = vallitāṉa ciṟukōl.
tiṇṭu = melliya pañcaṇai. tiṭṭu = vaṉṉilamāṉa ciṟumēṭu.
karuppu = iyalpāṉa karuniṟam. kaṟuppu = ciṉattāl allatu mutircciyāl nērum iruṇṭa niṟam.
marūu
cila taṉiccoṟkaḷum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum toṭarc coṟkaḷum vaḻakkāṟṟil maruvi, atāvatu ilakkaṇiyarāl oppuk koḷḷap peṟum vakaiyil kuṟukiyum tirintum,  vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
taṉiccol
elumpu - eṉpu, teṉku - teṅku, naṭuvaṇ - nāppaṇ, nerutal, nerunaṟṟu - nēṟṟu, pakuti - pāti, peyar - pēr, perumakaṉ - perumāṉ - pemmāṉ, pirāṉ; poḻutu - pōḻtu, pōtu, yāṉai - āṉai, viyar - vēr.
kūṭṭuccol
araiyuḻakku - āḻākku, koṇṭuvā - koṇṭā - koṇā - koṇar, cumaiyaṭai - cumaṭu - cummāṭu, cōḻa nāṭu - cōṇāṭu, teṅkaṅkāy - tēṅkāy, pukaviṭu - pukaṭṭu - pōṭṭu - puḻaikkai - pūḻkkai - pūṭkai, pōkaviṭu - pōkaṭu - pōṭu, varaiyāṭu - varuṭai, māryāppu - mārāppu, mukakkūṭu - mukkāṭu.
toṭarccol
arumaruntaṉṉa - arumānta - arumanta.
karivalam vanta nallūr - karuvai.
pūṭkai, pōṭu eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟkaḷ, mūla nilaiyil kūṭṭuc collāyiṉum, marūu nilaiyil oru coṟpōl  niṉṟu  orēporuḷ tarutalāl, taṉic collākavē koḷḷappaṭum.

tamiḻakattil piṟappōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya āriyakkulap piriviṉai ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, poṉṉiṟa uyirikaḷ pārppāra eṉṟum, karuniṟa vuyirikaḷ paṟai eṉṟum, aṭai peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. pārppāra nākam, paṟai nākam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpaik kāṭṭum aṭaimoḻi, apporuḷkaḷaic ciṟappākak kaiyāḷum vakuppāriṉ peyarākum.
e.ṭu. kuṟavaṉ pōkaṇi = periya pōkaṇi araca maruttuvam = aracarukkuc ceyyum vilakkūṇillā maruttuvam.
cila karuvippeyarkaḷiṉ aṭaimoḻi, akkaruviyāṟ ceyyappaṭum allatu ceppañ ceyyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyarākum.
e.ṭu. kōṇiyūci = periyavūci.

puṇarcci
irucoṟ puṇarvatu puṇarcci. moḻi potumakkaḷāl ākkap peṟṟatākaliṉ, ataṉ puṇarcciyum avaratu amaippē, kavaṉit taṟiyappeṟātu ēṟkeṉavēyirunta coṟkaṭṭu neṟimuṟai kaḷaiyē, puṇarcci neṟimoḻikaḷ (vitikaḷ) eṉa eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar ilakkaṇiyar, iyalpup puṇarcci, tiripuppuṇarcci eṉap puṇarcci iru vakaippaṭum. oruvakai vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi iyalpā yiruppatu iyalpup puṇarcci, ētēṉumoru vakaiyil tirivatu tiripup puṇarcci, tirital vēṟupaṭutal. atu tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. muṉṉillāta eḻuttō acaiyō  tōṉṟuvatu tōṉṟal; ōreḻuttu maṟṟōreḻuttāka māṟuvatu tirital; muṉṉuḷḷa eḻuttō acaiyō maṟaivatu keṭutal.
puṇarcciyiṟ putitāyt tōṉṟum acai, iru coṟkaḷai allatu oru collaiyum oru col uṟuppaiyum cārntu niṉṟu iyaippatāl (icaippatāl), cāriyai eṉappaṭum.
iyalpuppuṇarcci
cāttaṉ + vantāṉ = cāttaṉ vantāṉ
nalla + paiyaṉ = nalla paiyaṉ
pāl + iṉitu = pāliṉitu
meyyōṭu uyir cēriṉ uyirmeyyām. itu iyalpē.
vēṟṟumai : 1,8
pe-e. - ka.kā. ni. kā. akaravīṟṟuk kuṟippu.
vi. e. - i. kā.
tiripup puṇarcci
tōṉṟal : (Insertion)
vāḻai + kāy = vāḻaikkāy
avarai + pantal = avaraippantal
kaḷā + paḻam = kaḷāppaḻam, kaḷāmpaḻam
pū + ceṭi = pūcceṭi (pūkkum ceṭi)
... ... ... = pūñceṭi (aḻakiya ceṭi)
paṇattai + koṭu = paṇattaik koṭu
ūrukku + pō = ūrukkup pō
vara + col = varaccol
i + nāḷ = innāḷ
veḷ + āṭu = veḷḷāṭu
nāṭu + āṇmai = nāṭṭāṇmai
vēṟṟumai - 2, 4
pe. e. - akara vīṟṟu
iraṭṭal
kuṟilaṭuttamey + uyir kuṟilaṭutta valliṉa meyyēṟiya ukām + uyir
e.ṭu. kuṟu, ciṟu putu, neṭu, uṟu, vēṟṟumai.
ivai eḻuttut tōṉṟal
cekkār + kuṭi = cekkārakkuṭi cāriyaiyum eḻuttum
puḷi + paḻam = puḷiyam paḻam
kaṇ + potti = kaṇṇāmpotti cāriyaiyum iraṭṭalum
ivai cāriyai tōṉṟal.
tirital (Mutation)
nal + cey = naṉcey
veḷ + kalam = veṇkalam
cem + tāmarai = centāmarai
vem + nīr = vennīr
vēmpu + ilai = vēppilai
taṇ + nīr = taṇṇīr
uḷ + nākku = uṇṇākku
kal + tāḻai = kaṟṟāḻai
nal + nilam = naṉṉilam
uḷ + tu = uṇṭu, īṉ + tu = īṟṟu.
ivai tirital,
keṭutal (Omission) or Elision 
makara vīṟṟuppeyar peyarait taḻuval
maram + vēr = maravēr
tolkāppiyam + nūl = tolkāppiya nūl ivai keṭutal.
aṉṟu + kūli = aṟṟaikkūli
maṇ + kaṭṭi = maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi
ivai tōṉṟalum tiritalum.
paṭṭiṉam = piḷḷaiyār = paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷaiyār
nakaram + āṉ = nakarattāṉ
ivai tōṉṟalum keṭutalum
vēmpu + kāy = vēppaṅkāy
paṉai + tōppu = paṉantōppu
ivai tōṉṟalum tiritalum keṭutalum.
marūuppuṇarcci
cila coṟkaḷ olineṟippaṭi oḻuṅkāyp puṇarātu maruvippuṇarum, appuṇarcci marūuppuṇarcciyām. maruvutal neṟi tiṟamputal.
e.ṭu. 	ātaṉ + tā = (āntā) - āntai
		akam + kai = aṅkai
		nāḻi + uri = nāḻuri - nāṭuri 
		maka + kaḷ = makkaḷ
uḷḷaṅkai (uḷ + aṅkai) eṉpatu aṅkaiyiṉ naṭuppakuti. itil am eṉpatu cāriyaiyaṉṟu.
entai, tantai eṉpaṉa marūuppuṇarcciyalla; ivai em + tā, tam + tā eṉap piriyum, tā eṉpatu tantaiyaik kuṟikkum peyarccol.
tā + tā = tātā - tātai (tantaiyiṉ tantai) - iyalpup puṇarcci
... ... ... = tāttā (tantaiyiṉ tantai) - tiripuppuṇarcci.
tātai eṉpatiṟ pōṉṟē, entai, tantai, āntai, pūntai eṉpavaṟṟilum tai eṉpatu tā eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
o.nō.	empi(ṉ)	eṅkai evvai emmuṉ entai
			numpi(ṉ)	nuṅkai nuvvai nummuṉ nuntai
			tampi(ṉ)	taṅkai tavvai tammuṉ tantai
empiṉ = emakkup piṉ piṟantāṉ. kai = taṅkai (piṅ.). iḷaṅkiḷai eṅkai = emakkuc ciṟiyavaḷ. em + avvai = evvai. itil avvai eṉṉum aṉṉaiyiṉ peyar aṉṉai pōlum akkaiyaik kuṟittatu. iccol cila paḻañceyyuṭ kaḷil taṅkaiyaik kuṟippatu. kātal paṟṟiya marapu vaḻuvamaiti yeṉa aṟika. eṉ nantai, eṉ tampi, eṉ taṅkai eṉpaṉa vellām vaḻakkup paṟṟiya iṭavaḻuvamaitiyē,
emappaṉ 	numappaṉ 	tamappaṉ - takappaṉ
emakkai	numakkai	tamakkai
emaiyaṉ	numaiyaṉ	tamaiyaṉ
kārkālam, vaṭakōṭu, kōvūrkiḻār, nāṭukiḻavōṉ eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum, kēṭucālam, vēṟukālam, viṟaku talaiyaṉ, viṟaku kāṭu, vaṭakarai, maḻaikālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkilum, valimikātu vaḻaṅkuvatālum; nāṉku, veṭku eṉṉum tiripu vaṭivukaḷuṭaṉ nālku, velku eṉṉum iyalpu vaṭivukaḷum vaḻaṅki varuvatālum; palku, palporuḷ, cilkāl, valci, valpu eṉac cila coṟkaḷ eṉṟum iyalpākavē yiruppataṉālum; mutaṟkālattil tōṉṟal tirital puṇarcci attuṇaik kaṇṭippāyirunta tillaiyeṉa uyttuṇaralām.
mūvakaip puṇarcciyum oliyicaivai maṭṭumaṉṟi, iru poruṭ kiṭaippaṭṭa neruṅkiya toṭarpaiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka.
avarai x tuvarai
vāḻai x tāḻai
puṉṉai x teṉṉai
viḷā x palā, taḷā
ōriyalamaippu (Uniformity of Words)
orutuṟaippaṭṭa coṟkaḷaiyellām, mutalō īṟō etukaiyō paṟṟi ottavaṭivākkuvatu ōriyalamaippām.
oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, toṇṭu (oṉpatu), pattu, nūṟu eṉṉum mutaṟkālattu eṇṇup peyar kaḷellām ukarattil iṟutal kāṇka.
teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku, mēṟku eṉṉum ticaippeyarkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē.
pētai, petumpai, maṅkai, maṭantai, arivai, terivai, eṉṉum peṇpāṟ paruvap peyarkaḷ aikārattil iṟukiṉṟaṉa.
atō, anta, avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avai, ampar, aṅku, avaṇ, atōḷ - atōḷi.
itō, inta, ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivai, impar, iṅku, ivaṇ, itōḷ - itōḷi.
utō, unta, uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvai, umpar, uṅku, uvaṇ, utōḷ - utōḷi.
etō, enta, evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, etu, evai, empar, eṅku, evaṇ, etōḷ - etōḷi.
ittakaiya ōriyaloḻuṅkupaṭṭa cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷ vēṟem moḻiyilum illai. atā, antā, aṉṉā, aṉṉa mutaliya coṟkaṭkum iṅṅaṉamē oṭṭikkoḷka.
oppumai yamaippu (Analogy)
oru colliṉ etukai vaṭivil iṉṉoru collai amaippatu oppumai yamaippām.
etirmaṟaic coṟkaḷ :
iyaṟkai eṉṉum collamaippaip piṉpaṟṟi, ceyal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarōṭu kai yīṟu cērttuc ceyaṟkai eṉṟamaittatu oppumai yamaippē. ēṉaiyeṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu :
aṟam, maṟam, iṟappu, piṟappu; itai (putukkollai), mutai (paḻaṅkollai); iṇaṅku, piṇaṅku; iṉpu (iṉpam), tuṉpu (tuṉpam); kuṟṟam, naṟṟam; ciṟu, peru; ciṟṟu, peṟṟu (periyatu), ciṉṉañciṟiya peṉṉamperiya; tāy, cēy, teruḷ, maruḷ; nalam; polam; naṉcey, puṉcey; nāṭu, kāṭu; pēraṭi, cīṟaṭi,  nāṭṭam, pāṭṭam; muntu, pintu.
iṉapporuṭ coṟkaḷ;
nāṟṟicaik kāṟṟup peyarkaḷuḷ, vāṭai kōṭai eṉpavum koṇṭal teṉṟal eṉpavum oppumai yamaippām.
oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ :
koṟṟam, veṟṟam.
toṭarpaṟṟa coṟkaḷ :
viṭai - viṭalai, kaṭai - kaṭalai, toṭai - toṭalai, muṭai - muṭalai; ai - (aintu) añcu, pai - pañcu, mai - mañcu, nai - nañcu.  (naṟṟiṇai 353).
nāṉilak karupporuṭ coṟkaḷ
nilaittiṇai (tāvaram), iyaṅkutiṇai (caṅkamam) eṉṉum iru vakuppuyirikaḷuḷ, cila pala ovvoru nilattiṟkum ciṟappāka uriyaṉavākum. muntiyal tamiḻar kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻntirunta pōtē, mūviṭac cuṭṭup peyar, viṉāp peyar, muṟaippeyar, vaṇṇamum vaṭivum aḷavum cuvaiyum ūṟum paṟṟiya kuṇappeyar, iṉṟiyamaiyāta viṉaiccoṟkaḷ mutaliya aṭippaṭait tamiḻc coṟkaḷ amaintuviṭṭaṉa. payir paccai, vilaṅku paṟavai, uṇavu vakai, toḻil muṟai, karuvi paṭaikkalam, paḻakka vaḻakkam, koḷkai kōṭpāṭu, kuṭiyiruppiṭam mutaliya palvēṟu karupporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya coṟkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. ataṉpiṉ kuṟiñciyaṭutta mullai nilattiṟkup paraviyapōtu, annilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karupporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ piṟantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ mullaiyaṭutta marutanilattiṟkup paraviya pōtu, annilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karup poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉpiṉ, marutattaiyaṭutta neytal nilattiṟku maruta nakar mīṉpiṭiyāḷar kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta pōtu, annilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karupporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya 
coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
kuṟiñciyum mullaiyum mutuvēṉilil vaṟaṇṭanilaiyē pālai yātaliṉ, annilattup piṟkālat tuṇṭāṉa coṟkaḷellām toḻilum karuviyum paḻakka vaḻakkamum koḷkaiyum paṟṟiyaṉavē.
kuṟiñciyaṭuttum mullaiyaṭuttum neytalirukkavuṅ kūṭumāyiṉum, kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar kuṟiñciyiṉiṉṟu āṟṟaiyoṭṭiyē paṭippaṭiyākap paraviyatākat terivatāl, maruta nilaikkup piṉpē neytal ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. mutaṟkāla māntar ceyaṟkai nīrnilai amaikkat 
teriyātirunta kālattil iyaṟkai nīrnilaiyāṉa āṟṟaiyē nampiyiruntaṉar. iṟṟait tamiḻakattiṟ pōṉṟē paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattilum, perumalaittoṭarkaḷ mēl kōṭiyilē iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu paḵṟuḷiyum kumariyum pōṉṟē āṟṟaiyaṭuttē makkaḷ paraviṉ, kuṟiñci mullai marutam neytal eṉṟa muṟaiyilēyē nilaṅkaḷai aṭaintirukka muṭiyum. innilaimai iṟṟait tamiḻakattiṟkum ēṟṟatātal kāṇka. muntiyal māntar munnilai nākarikattaiyum muṟaiyē aṭaitaṟkēṟṟavāṟu, kuṟiñci mullai marutam ākiya 
munnilamum aṭuttaṭuttiruttal tamiḻnāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅkaṇumillai.
veṟpaṉ, kuṟumpoṟaināṭaṉ, ūraṉ, tuṟaivaṉ, viṭalai eṉṉum aintiṇait taṉiyūrt talaivarāṭcikkup piṉ, palavūrk kiḻamai pūṇṭa vēḷiriṉ kuṟunila āṭci vantu, ataṉpiṉ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyariṉ perunila āṭci tōṉṟiyapōtu, tamiḻ aintiṇaic coṟkaḷum cērnta peruvaḷa moḻiyāyiṟṟu, maruta nilattil nakarkaḷ tōṉṟi nākarikam vaḷarntu palvēṟu toḻilkaḷum kalaikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, tamiḻ mēṉmēlum vaḷarntōṅkiṟṟu.
tamiḻ aintiṇai makkaṭkum potuvāyirukkum iḵtoṉṟē, tamiḻarellām ōriṉameṉṟum, toṉṟu toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭi lēyē vāḻntu varupavareṉṟum, toḻiltaḻuvātu piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyak kulappiriviṉaiyāl tūya māperun tamiḻiṉam ciṉṉa piṉṉamākac citaivuṇṭateṉṟum, terivikkap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
poruṭṭirivu muṟaikaḷ (Semantic Changes)
1. uvamaiyāku peyar 
cila peyarkaḷ uvamai yaṭippapaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. māṅkāy - māṅkāy pōṉṟa neñcāṅkulai (Heart).
takkaṭṭi - takkaṭṭikkāy allatu kaṉipōṉṟa kaṇ paṭṭaik kaṭṭi (Sty).
erumai nākku - erumai nākkup pōṉṟa mīṉ.
kiḷimūkku - kiḷimūkku vaṭivāṉa māmpaḻam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar, paṇpiyākupeyar muṟaiyil ap poruḷkaḷiṉ niṟattaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. : cāmpal vāḻai, kākkaip piciṉ, pavaḻac cōḷam, kurutikkāntaḷ, poṟkoṉṟai, mayiṟkaḻuttuc cēlai.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamait tokaiyāy amaintuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. ārālmī ṉavarai, kōḻikkāl taiyal, pāmpuk kaṟṟāḻai, vauvāl mīṉ.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamai yaṭippaṭaiyil amaintu, teḷivupaṟṟi īṟu peṟṟum tirintum uḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu.	āṉaikkompaṉ - oruvakaic campānel.
		kāṭaikkaṇṇi - kāṭaikkaṇ pōṉṟa oruvakaic ciṟu 
	tavacam (tāṉiyam), ataṉ payir.
	kutiraivāli - kutiraivālpōṟ cutirtaḷḷum oru vakaic 
	ciṟu tavacap payirum attavacamum.
	nuṇā - nuṇal (maṇal tavaḷai) pōṉṟa kāy kāykkum 
	maravakai.
mūvakait takuti vaḻakku
iṭakkaraṭakkal (Euphemism) maṅkalam (Euphemism) kuḻūuk kuṟi (Coventional terms) eṉṉum mūvakait takuti vaḻakku muṟaiyilum, cila coṟkaḷum vaḻakkāṟukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. 
iṭakkaraṭakkal	oṉṟiṟku iraṇṭiṟkup pōtal, pavvī, 
				mutaṟkuṟai kamalam.
maṅkalam 		koṭittaṭṭutal = pāmpu kaṭittal; 
				perumpiṟitu = cāvu.
kuḻuukkuṟi 		centalai = araikkāl,
				karuntalai = kāl, taṅkāṉ = arai.
				arumpu = arici.
ōriṉappaṭuttalum (Generalisation) vēṟiṉappaṭuttalum (Discrimination).
ōriṉappaṭuttal	palvēṟu vakaipporuḷkaḷai, oru 
				potut taṉmai paṟṟi oru collāṟ 
				kuṟittal ōriṉap paṭuttalām.
e.ṭu. māṭu - peṟṟamum (kāḷaiyum āvum) erumaiyum.
māṉ - uḻai, pulvāy, navvi, pukar (puḷḷimāṉ), marai, varuṭai, kaṭamai, miḻā, kācaṟai, (kattūri) kavari, kutirai mutaliyaṉa.
tumpi - muṉtūmpu (Proboscis) uṭaiya yāṉaiyum kuḷaviyum.
vilaṅku, paṟavai, maram, mīṉ eṉpaṉa mikavirivāka ōriṉappaṭuttum coṟkaḷ.
perunelli, arinelli, kīḻvāynelli eṉpavaṟṟai nelliyiṉappaṭuttiyatum ōriṉappaṭuttalē.
vēṟiṉappaṭuttal
orē iṉamāṉa allatu otta tōṟṟamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷai, nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭu paṟṟi vevvēṟu collāṟ kuṟittal vēṟiṉappaṭuttalām.
e.ṭu. ilai - ilai, tāḷ, tōkai, ōlai, piñcu, kāy.
nilaittiṇai - pūñcaṇam, pācam, pāci, kāḷāṉ, pul, pūṇṭu, kōrai, payir, taṭṭai, koṭi, tūṟu, ceṭi, maram.
nuḷampu - nuḷḷāṉ, ulaṅku, mutalai, iṭaṅkar, aravam.
camaitta tavacavuṇavu - cōṟu, kañci, kūḻ, kaḷi.
iṉi, ciṟappuccoṟkaḷaṉṟip potupcoṟkaḷē  vevvēṟaṭai peṟṟup poruḷkaḷai vēṟiṉappaṭuttalummuṇṭu.
e.ṭu. ciṟṟeṟumpu, poṭiyeṟumpu, āyeṟumpu, kuṟuveṟumpu, (cevveṟumpu), maḻaiyeṟumpu, maṇṭaiyeṟumpu, piḷḷaiyār eṟumpu, kaṭṭeṟumpu.



aivakaip poruḷtiripu
1. vēṟupāṭu (Variation)
coṟkaḷ kuṟittaṟkuriya poruḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟoṉṟaik kuṟippatu vēṟupāṭām. atu uyarpu, iḻipu, tolviyalpu eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.

oru col, tāṉ kuṟikkakkūṭiya pala poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkē allatu ataṉ oru kūṟṟikē, ciṟappāka āḷappeṟutal varaiyaṟaiyām.
col	kuṟikkumporuḷkaḷ	varaiyaṟaipporuḷ

	taṉmaippaṉmai
nām	iruvakait 		uḷappāṭṭut 
	taṉmaippaṉmai		taṉmaip paṉmai

			peṇ (pāṇṭi nāṭu)
maṇam, kuṇam, oḻukkam eṉṉum potuccoṟkaḷai nalvakaikkum, nāṟṟam, vīccam, viṉai eṉṉum potuc coṟkaḷait tīyavakaikkum, vaḻaṅkuvatum varaiyaṟaiyē.
ciṟappikkai (Specialisation)
pala poruḷkaṭkup potuvāṉa collai avaṟṟuḷ talai ciṟantataṟku āḷvatu ciṟappikkaiyām.
col		potupporuḷ	ciṟappupporuḷ

ney		palvakai ney	āviṉ ney
malar (aṭi) 		palvēṟu malar	tāmarai malar
makkaṭpēṟu illaṟa vāḻkkaiyiṟ ciṟanta pēṟākak karutap paṭṭamai, 
peṟumavaṟṟuḷ yāmaṟiva tillai yaṟivaṟinta
makkaṭpē ṟalla piṟa.
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷālaṟika, peṟṟōr = ciṟanta pēṟu peṟṟa tāytantaiyar.
pulāluṇpār kaṟi eṉṉum collāṟ pulālaik kuṟittalum ciṟappikkaiyē.
potuppikkai (Generalisation)
ciṟappup poruṭcollaip potupporuḷil āḷvatu potuppikkai yām. itu ciṟappikkaiyiṉ maṟutalai.
col 	ciṟappupporuḷ	potupporuḷ
eli	veḷḷeli	eli
tāmarai	centāmarai	tāmarai
payaṟu 	paccaippayaṟu	payaṟu
tūṅkutal	toṅkukaṭṭilil	uṟaṅkutal
		allatu toṭṭilil
		uṟaṅkutal
poruṭperukkamum poruḷiḻappum
oru karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟoru karuttut tōṉṟuvatāl, potuvākac coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ iraṇṭum palavumākap perukuvatuṇṭu. ētēṉumoru ciṟappupporuḷ kuṟitta aṭaipeṟṟa collaip potupporuḷil, vaḻaṅkiṉ, acciṟappup poruḷaṭai taṉ poruḷai iḻantuviṭum.
e.ṭu.
poruṭperukkam
kāḷai = erutu, maṟavōṉ.
tiru = celvam, aḻaku, teyvattaṉmai,
paḷḷi = paṭukkai, paṭukkaiyaṟai, vīṭu, kōyil, kalviccālai.
poruḷiḻappu.
aṭaipeṟṟacol	ciṟappupporuḷ	potupporuḷ
eṇṇey  		eḷḷiṉ ney 		neypporuḷ
tamappaṉ (takappaṉ) 	tam appaṉ 	appaṉ
taṇṇīr  	kuḷirnta nīr  	nīr
ākupeyarum ākuviṉaiyum aivakaittiripu iruvakai :
1. mutaṟporuḷ taḻuviyatu
2. mutaṟporuḷ taḻuvātatu.
coṟṟoṭarākkam (tōrā. ki.mu. 25,000)
toṭariyam eṉṉum muṟṟuc coṟṟoṭar, ikkālattiṟ pōṉṟē mutaṟkālattilum (eḻuvāy payaṉilaiyeṉṉum) īruṟuppukkaḷaiyō, (avaṟṟoṭu ceyappaṭuporuḷum cērnta) mūvuṟuppukkaḷaiyō koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, avai īṟum iṭainilaiyumaṟṟa pakāccoṟkaḷākavē yiruntirukkum. aṟi eṉpatu aṟivu eṉṟum, keṭu eṉpatu kēṭu eṉṟum, cey eṉpatu ceykaiyeṉṟum, iṉṟum poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
aṟikoṉ ṟaṟiyā ṉeṉiṉum  (kuṟaḷ. 638)
keṭuviṉṟi maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar  (puṟam. 39, 7)
kaḷiṟu kaḷampaṭutta peruñcey yāṭavar  (neṭu. 171)
aṭi, viḻi, taḷ, col, mutaliya ettuṇaiyō viṉaiyaṭikaḷ, iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peyarākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. cila malaiyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil nal (nallu) eṉṉum paṇpaṭi naṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭukiṉṟatu. uṇṭu illai eṉpaṉavum, ivai pōṉṟa piṟavum, iṉṟum mukkālattiṟkum potuvām. kuṟippu viṉaikaḷ muṉpiṉ varum coṟṟuṇai koṇṭu ikkālattil oru kālattai uṇarttutal pōṉṟē, terinilai viṉaiyaṭikaḷum mutaṟkālattil uṇarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivākavē iruppiṉum, potumakkaḷ pēccu eṉṟum urainaṭaiyē yeṉpatai maṟantuviṭal kūṭātu.
ceyyuḷ naṭai (tōrā. ki.mu. 15,000)
tamiḻa māntar nīṇṭa kālamāka urainaṭaiyilēyē pēci vantapiṉ, mati viḷakkattālum uṇarveḻucciyālum tūṇṭap paṭṭu, teyvappaṟṟu, kātal, pōr, vāḻttu, katai, varalāṟu mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷ paṟṟi, eḷiya naṭaiyil teṉmāṅkum cintum pōṉṟa icaippāṭṭukkaḷum, akavalum veṇkalip pāvum irāmappayyaṉ ammāṉaiyum pōṉṟa ceyyuṭkaḷum, iyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. mōṉaiyetukai yamaippum coṟ curukkamum poruṭceṟivum avar uḷḷattaik kavarntatiṉāl, palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya paḻamoḻikaḷum ovvoṉṟā yeḻuntaṉa.
nālvakai eḻuttu (nōrā. ki.mu. 12,000)
aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaip pēccum paṇṭamāṟṟuraiyāṭṭum kātalaṟivippum, vāymoḻiyāka nikaḻttuvatu nērilaṉṟit tolaiviṟ kūṭāmaiyiṉ, nāḷaṭaivil nālvakaiyeḻuttu muṟaikaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiṉa. avai,
1. paṭaveḻuttu (Pictograph)
2. karutteḻuttu (Ideograph)
3. acaiyeḻuttu (Syllabary)
4. oliyeḻuttu (Phoretic characters)
eṉpaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpataṉ mutaṟporuḷ paṭam allatu ōviyam eṉpatē. eḻututal varaital. iṉṟum paṭameḻututal eṉṉum vaḻakkiruttal kāṇka.
eḻutappeṟṟa ilakkiyam (tōrā : ki.mu. 11,000)
oliyeḻuttut tōṉṟiyapiṉ, katai, varalāṟu, kaṇakku, aṟam, araciyal, maṇaviṉpam, pōr, maruttuvam, kaṇiyam.
icai, nāṭakam, camayam, pukaḻcci, vaḻuttu mutaliya pala tuṟaikaḷ paṟṟip paṉuvalkaḷum eḻuntaṉa.
ilakku eṉpatu kuṟi allatu kuṟikkōḷ, makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyiṉ ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷai eṭuttukkūṟi, avarait tiruttuvatum uyarttuvatumē paṉuvaliṉ allatu nūliṉ nōkkamātaliṉ, avaiyiraṇṭum ilakkiyam eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa. ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam.
paṇṭai viṟpayiṟciyāḷar, muṟaiyē, parupporuḷ, nuṇporuḷ, cuḻalāp poruḷ, cuḻalporuḷ ākiya nālvakai ilakkiṉūṭu eytu payiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟaiyē,
peruvaṇmai ciṟu nuṇmai calam niccalam
eṉṟu pāratam (vāraṇā. 56) kūṟum, peruvaṇmai eṉpatu marattiṉ aṭi pōlvatu; ciṟu nuṇmai eṉpatu marattiṉ mēluḷḷa ciṟṟilai pōlvatu; calam niccalam eṉpaṉa marattucciyil amaikkappeṟum tiripaṉṟiyum cuḻalāc cakkaramum pōlvaṉa.
ilaiyai ilakkeṉpatu iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakkākum. ciṟunuṇmaikkup perumpālum ilaiyaiyē kuṟiyākak koṇṭamaiyāl, ilakku eṉṉum coṟkuk kuṟi eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ilakkaṇam (tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000)
ilakkaṇamum, moḻiyiṉ allatu ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟanta amaippaiyum naṭaiyaiyum eṭuttukkūṟuvataiyē kuṟik kōḷākak koṇṭamaiyiṉ, appeyar peṟṟatu. ilakku + aṇam = ilakkaṇam.
uḷḷuṟai teyvam oḻintatai nilaṉeṉak
koḷḷu meṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil (993), kuṟi eṉṉuñcol ilakkaṇattaik kuṟittal kāṇka. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉṉum irucollum orē mutaṉilaiyē. poruḷ vēṟupāṭu paṟṟi īṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. ivaiyiraṇṭum mutaṉilaiyiṉālum īṟṟiṉālum tūya teṉcollēyeṉṟu teḷika. ivai vaṭamoḻiyil muṟaiyē lakṣaya lakṣaṇa eṉṟu tiriyum. āyiṉ, aṅku ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷaik kuṟippavai vyākaraṇa, sāhitya eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē. lakṣya lakṣaṇa eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāka vaṭamoḻiyil kāṭṭappeṟum lakṣ eṉṉum coṟku, ilakku (kuṟi) eṉpatē poruḷ. ākavē, ivviru vaṭacollum teṉ colliṉ tiripeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām. mēlum, tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl āriya varukaikku 8000 āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭateṉṉum uṇmaiyum, itai valiyuṟuttuvatu kāṇka.
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟoru peyar kuṟikkiṉṟatu cintāmaṇi (103). katiraivēṟpiḷḷai avarkaḷ tokutta tamiḻmoḻiyakarātiyil, añcaṇaṅkam-pañca vilakkaṇam. añcaṇaṅkiyam-pañcavilakkiya eṉak kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum irucoṟkum, aṇaṅku eṉpatu mutaṉilaiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. aṇaṅkutal olittal.
mikaiyaṇaṅku meynniṟīi mīḷi maṟavar
pukaiyaṇaṅkap pūmāri cinti
eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai (potuviyaṟ paṭalam, 8). aṇaṅku eṉpatu eḻuttaiyuṅ kuṟikkumāyiṉ, aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, muṟaiyē, ilakkaṇattaiyum ilakkiyattaiyuṅ kuṟippatu poruttamē, āyiṉ, iccoṟkaḷ tamiḻakattil eṉṟum vaḻakkil iruntatākat teriyavillai. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉavē toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉa.
tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ, (1) mutal, (2) cārpu eṉa iru vakaippaṭum. mutalāvatu iyalpākat tōṉṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ mutaleḻut tukkaḷ, piṉpu avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpākat tirinta eḻuttukkaḷ cārpeḻuttukkaḷ. uyireḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum meyyeḻuttup patiṉeṭṭumāka mutal eḻuttukkaḷ muppatām. cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟām. ākavē, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ allatu olikaḷ mottam muppattu mūṉṟām.
uyireḻuttukkaḷ, kuṟilaintum neṭilēḻumāka iru vakaip paṭum. meyyeḻuttukkaḷum, valliṉam āṟum melliṉam āṟum iṭaiyiṉam āṟumāka mūvakaippaṭum.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷ (Phonemes)
māntaṉ taṉ vāyait tiṟantavuṭaṉ iyalpāka olikkum oli akaram allatu ākāram. kuḻantai piṟanta uṭaṉ aḻum oliyum muḻaittal muṟaippaṭṭa akara ākāramē. ākavē, mutalāvatu māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiyatu uyiroliyē; atu akaramē.
vāy tiṟavāta nilaiyil, mūkkuvaḷiyāl olikkum oli makaram. ākavē, meyyolikaḷuḷ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyatu, itaḻ maṭṭum porutti eḷitāka volikkum makaramākum.
iṅṅaṉam mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya akaramum makaramuñcēriṉ, am eṉṉum acai piṟakkum. orukāl itaṉiṉṟu. amma muṟaippeyar kuḻavivaḷarp poliyāyt tōṉṟiyirukkalām. pāluṟuppaik kuṟikkum ammam eṉṉum col marumam eṉpataṉ tiripākum. marumam - mammam - ammam.
māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya moḻiyolikaḷuḷ, uyirellām muṉpum meyyellām piṉpumākat tōṉṟavillai. uyirum meyyum oḻuṅkiṉṟi māṟimāṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. ammuṟaiyai iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. āyiṉum, uyiriṉattaiyum meyyiṉat taiyum vēṟu pirittu ovvōr iṉattaiyuñ cērnta olikaḷ tōṉṟiya muṟaiyai oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
uyir tōṉṟiya muṟai
mutal tōṉṟiyavai : neṭil - ā, ī, ū
iṭaittōṉṟiyavai : neṭil - ē, ō kuṟil - a, i, u
kaṭaittōṉṟiyavai : neṭil - ai, au kuṟil - e, o
uyirkaḷuḷ, neṭil muṉpum kuṟil piṉpum tōṉṟiṉa.
mutalil tōṉṟiya mūvuyirum, muṟaiyē, cēymai yaṇmai muṉmaic cuṭṭukkaḷākavē tōṉṟiṉa.
aṇmaic cuṭṭiṉum muṉmaic cuṭṭu pintiyatātalālum, caṟṟu mikunta muyaṟci vēṇṭutalāṉum, īkāram ūkārattiṟku muntiya tātal vēṇṭum.
ikara īkārattil mōṉaiyāka ekara ēkāramum, ukara ūkārattiṉ mōṉaiyāka okara okāramum tōṉṟutalāṉum; koccai vaḻakkil iṭam eṉpatu eṭam eṉṟum, uṉakku eṉpatu oṉakku eṉṟum, ikaram ekaramākavum ukaram okaramākavum olippatālum, aṇmaic cuṭṭiliruntē ekara ēkāramum, muṉmaic cuṭṭiliruntē okara ōkāramum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
aḷapu vakaiyil,
ā ī ū ē ai ō au e(ṉ)ṉum
appāl ēḻum
īraḷa picaikkum neṭṭeḻut teṉpa  (4)
eṉṟu, amaippu vakaiyil,
akara ikaram aikāra mākum  (54)
akara ukaram aukāra mākum  (55)
eṉṟum, tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, akara ikaram cērntu aikāramum akaravukaram cērntu aukāramum puṇarolikaḷāyt (Diphthongs) tōṉṟiṉaveṉa aṟiyalām. ikaram ukarattiṉum muntiyatātaliṉ, ikarak kūṟuḷḷa aikāram ukarak kūṟuḷḷa aukārattiṉum muntit tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭanūlār puṇarccit tiripaip paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷaiyum puṇarolikaḷākak koḷvar. akkoḷkai tamiḻukku ēṟkātu. avai mōṉait tiripāy eḻuntavaiyē.
ōriru coṉ mutalil varum akara ikaram aikāramākavum eḻutap peṟum.
e.ṭu. vaḷ-vay-vayir-vayiram-vairam.
ōriru coṉ mutalil varum akara ukaram aukāramākavum eḻutappeṟum, 
e.ṭu. katuvāli (= kuṟukiya vālaiyuṭaiya paṟavai iṉam - kavutāri (ilakkaṇappōli - kautāri).
iṉi, akarattaiyaṭutta yakarameyyum ellāviṭattum aikāra mākavē eḻutappeṟum.
akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
ai eṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟattōṉṟum  (tol. 56)
e.ṭu. vaḷ (= kūrmai) - (vay) - vai = kūrmai. ḷakara mey yakara meyyākat tirivatu peruvaḻakku.
e.ṭu. koḷ - kōḷ - kōy, toḷ - toy, noḷ - noy, piḷ - piy, māḷ - māy, veḷ - vey - veyyōṉ = virumpukiṉṟōṉ. vey - veymmai - vemmai = viruppam.
vemmai vēṇṭal  (tol. uri, 36)
yakara meyyīṟṟu okara mutaṟcol, īreḻuttuccollāyiruntu akara mutalāy māṟiṉ, aikāra vaṭivu peṟum.
e.ṭu. poḷ - poy (uṭṭuḷai) - (pay) - pai.
akarattai yaṭutta vakaramey, oru cila coṟkaḷil aukāramākavum eḻutappeṟum.
e.ṭu. avviyam - auviyam, kavvu - kauvu, vavvāl = vauvāl.
auvai eṉṉum col avvai eṉpataṉ tiripēyāyiṉum, oru pulattiyāriṉ iyaṟpeyarātaliṉ, aukāra mutalatākavē eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum.
ammai - avvai - auvai.
av, avvāṟu, cavvu, tavvu mutaliya cila coṟkaḷil, akaramaṭutta vakaramey aukāravīṟāka eḻutappaṭuvatēyillai. āyiṉ, ay eṉṉum vaṭivam ay eṉṉum acaiyaik kuṟittālaṉṟi, eḻuttuttamiḻil nikaḻtaṟku iṭamēyillai. itai nōkkip pōlum,
akarat timpar vakarap puḷḷiyum
au eṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟātuviṭṭatu! uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ, 
ai, au iraṇṭum puṇaroliyaṉkaḷ (Dipthongs or Compound Phonemes); ēṉaiya taṉiyoliyaṉkaḷ (Monophthongs or Simple Phonemes).
uyir tōṉṟiya vakaikaḷ
1. aṅkāttal (vāytiṟattal) - a, ā
2. cuṭṭal - ā, ī, ū
3. mōṉaittiripu - i - e, u - o
4. uṇarcciyoli - ai!
5. vāycceykai - av - au
6. kuṟukkam - ā - a, ē - e
7. oppoli - ū (kū)
aṅkāttal eṉpatu maṟṟellā vakaikaṭkum aṭippaṭait tuṇaiyē yaṉṟi, taṉivakai yaṉṟu.

mey tōṉṟiya muṟai
ma
pa, ka, ṅa
la, ra, ta, na
ca, ña, ya, va
ṭa, ṇa, ḷa, ḻa
ṟa, ṉa
kuṟippu : (1) uyirum meyyum tōṉṟiya muṟaiyāka iṅkuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa īroḻuṅkum, tōrāya (uttēca) muṟaiyē yaṉṟi immiyum vaḻuvāta aṟiviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭa tulliya muṟaiyalla.
2. uyirum meyyum vevvēṟākak kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppiṉum, uyirellān tōṉṟiyapiṉ mey tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟākātu. iruvakaiyum ovvoṉṟāy allatu oṉṟum palavumāy māṟi māṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa.
mutalil ākāravuyirum ataiyaṭuttu makarameyyum tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.
yakaram tōṉṟumuṉ īkāra ikaramum, vakaram tōṉṟumuṉ ūkāra ukaramum, tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa tōṉṟumuṉ ellā vuyirkaḷum tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
3. melliṉa meykaḷuḷ, makaram oṉṟē taṉiyē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ēṉaiyavellām ṅka, ñc, ṇṭa, nta, ṉṟa eṉat taṉtaṉ valliṉa meyyuṭaṉ iṇaintē tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum. 
kuḻavikaḷiṉ toṇṭaiyil ṅka eṉṉum oli piṟattal kāṇka.
valliṉameykaḷ taṉittaṉit tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
4. māntaṉ allatu muntiyal tamiḻaṉ pēciya iyaṟkai moḻi, uyir, mey eṉṉum pakup puṇarcciyiṉṟi, ōracaiyum, palavacai yumākiya olikaḷaiyum ōcaikaḷaiyumē koṇṭiruntatu.
meytōṉṟiya vakaikaḷ
1. oppoli - k (kākkā), ṇ (kiṇ)
2. vāycceykai - v (av), m (am)
3. iṉattōṉṟal - ṅ (ṅka), ṉ (ṉṟa)
4. puṇarcci - ñ (pū + cōlai = pūñcōlai)
5. tiripu - n (porum - porun), r (l-r)
6. mutircci - ḻ (ḷa-ḻa), ṟ (ra-ṟa)
7. iyaṟkai - t, p
aḷavu (māttirai)
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ oliyaḷavu aḷapu allatu māttirai yeṉappaṭum. māttal aḷattal, itu oru vaḻakkaṟṟa viṉai. māttiram eṉṉum collum itaṉaṭip piṟantatē. mā eṉṉum aḷavup peyar mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyarām.
ōr imai allatu noṭiyaḷavu oru māttirai. uyirāyiṉum uyirmeyyāyiṉum, kuṟilukku oṉṟum neṭilukku iraṇṭum māttiraiyām. uyirmeyyil uyirum meyyum ākiya īreḻut tiruppiṉum, ammeykku māttiraiyillai. taṉimeykku māttirai arai.
ai, au eṉṉum iru neṭilum taṉittu nillātum aḷapeṭuk kātum colluṟuppāy variṉ, oṉṟarai māttiraiyāyk kuṟukiyolikkum. aikāram colliṭaiyil oru māttirai yākavum kuṟukum.
2. cārpeḻuttukkaḷ
uyirum meyyum ākiya mutaleḻuttukkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpiṉāl tōṉṟuvaṉa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ, cārtal oṉṟai yoṉṟu aṭuttal. uyiriṉattaic cērntavai iraṇṭum meyyiṉattaic cērntatu oṉṟum āka, cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mottam mūṉṟām. avai kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉpaṉavākum. ivaṟṟuḷ ku, u iyaṟkai, ēṉaiyiraṇṭum puṇarcci viṉai.
tolkāppiyar kūṟiyavāṟu immūṉṟē cārpeḻuttukkaḷ. naṉṉūlār uyirmeyyaiyum vēṟu cila eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅ kaḷaiyum aḷapeṭaiyaiyum cērttut tavaṟākap patteṉa virittu viṭṭār. uyirmey uyirummeyyum cērnta kūṭṭeḻuttē yaṉṟi vēṟeḻuttākātu. eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅkaḷai yellām cārpeḻutteṉṟu koḷḷiṉ, āytak kuṟukkattaic cārpiṟ cārpeṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. aḷapeṭai eṉpatu eḻut toli nīṭṭamē yaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttākātu. ivvuṇmaikaḷai yellām nōkkātu, cāppeḻuttut tokaiyaip perukki yataṟku, māṇavarai mayakkutalaṉṟi vēṟoru payaṉumiṉṟām.
kuṟṟiyalikaram
araiyaḷapāyk kuṟukiyolikkum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaram, atu mūvakaiyil tōṉṟum.
1.	yakarattōṭu puṇarnta kuṟṟiyalukarattiripu.
	e.ṭu. varaku + yātu = varakiyātu.
2.	puṇarcciyāl amainta miyā eṉṉum muṉṉilaiyacaic col.
	kēḷum + aiyā = kēḷumaiyā - kēḷumiyā - kēṇmiyā. o.nō: maruḷum - maruṇm.
3.	melliṉa iṭaiyiṉa meyyīṟu yakarattōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcci viḷaivu.
	maṇ + yāṉai = maṇṇiyāṉai
	vēḷ + yāvaṉ = vēḷiyāvaṉ
ivvakaip puṇarcci ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. 
kuṟṟiyalukaram
taṉineṭiṟkum iru allatu palaveḻuttiṟkup piṉ, valliṉa meyyōṭu kūṭic collīṟāy varum ukaram, itaḻ kuviyātu, ikarattiṟkum ukarattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭu, arai māttiraiyāyk kuṟukiyolikkum atu kuṟṟiyalukaram.
e.ṭu. māṭu, cekku, paruppu, piṇṇākku, vantataṟku.
āytam
taṉiccollilum puṇarccollilum, kuṟilukkum valliṉa uyirmeykkum iṭaiyil, nuṇṇiya kakaramāka olittu niṟkum eḻuttu āytamām. āyntatu āytam. āytal nuṇukutal.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cā ay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (813) kūṟutal kāṇka.
āytam piṉvarumāṟu nālvakaiyil tōṉṟum
(1) puṇarccittiripu
colluṟuppup puṇarcci
e.ṭu. al + ku = alku - aḵku
veḷ + ku = veḵku
alkutal = curuṅkutal. veḷḷutal = virumputal, veḷ - veṇṭu (ācaippaṭu). veḷ - vey - veyyōṉ = virumpiyōṉ.
coṟpuṇarcci
e.ṭu. pal + tuḷi = paḵṟuḷi
kal + tītu = kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu
muḷ + tītu = muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu
al + kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya
2. taṉiccol tirippu

pakuti - paḵti
3. cāriyaippuṇarppu
e.ṭu. aḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ

ivvakai ceyyuṭkē yuriyatām.
āytam ōruyirōṭuṅ kūṭātu taṉittu niṟpatāl taṉinilai eṉṟum, puḷḷivaṭivāyiruppatāl puḷḷi eṉṟum, muppuḷḷi eṉṟum, taṉakkuppiṉ varum valliṉa meyyai melivippatāl nalipu eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum.
pattu, paḵtu; kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu; eṉṉum colliṇaikaḷai olittuk kāṇka.
iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻukkuriya āytam, tirimoḻi kaṭkuriya polivolikaḷaip (Voiced Sounds) piṟappikkum eṉṉum kūṟṟu, tiripuṇarcciyiṉ viḷaiveṉa aṟika. āyta voliyiṉ kakaraviṉat taṉmaiyai aḵkēṉam, maḵkāṉ eṉṉum cāriyaip puṇarppālum kaṇṭukoḷka.
cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ, kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum tām niṟkumiṭattu vēṟoṉṟaṟkum iṭantārāmaiyāl, avviraṇ ṭaiyum maṟuvoliyaṉkaḷ (Allophones) eṉṉalām. āytam attuṇai oḻuṅkum yāppuṟavum peṟāmaiyum maṟuvoli yaṉākātu.

eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. (kāñci taḻuva. 244)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya muppateḻuttuk kaḷai maṭṭum koṇṭa tokutikkuk kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, ataṉoṭu 216 uyirmeyyeḻuttuṅ koṇṭa tokutikku neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, peyar. paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṇ meyyoṭuṅ kūṭa, uyirmeymottam iru 
nūṟṟuppatiṉāṟām.
ellā moḻikaḷilum uyirmeykaḷ kalantutāṉ olikkiṉṟaṉa.
āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuk kūṭṭu vaṭivam mutaṉmutal amaittavar tamiḻarē, tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyē vaṭamoḻiyilum piṟa intiya moḻikaḷilum uyirmey vaṭivukaḷ amaintaṉa. iṉṉum mēlai āriyattilum cēmiyattilum uyirum meyyum 
taṉittaṉi niṟuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. amerikku, cappāṉ mutaliya oru cila moḻikaḷil maṭṭum, taṉivarikkum kūṭṭu varikkum iṭaip paṭṭa acaivarikaḷ (Syllabaries) ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaiyum kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭavaiyē.
āytam uyirēṟappeṟāta meyyāyiṉum, ataṟkut taṉi vaṭiva muṇmai paṟṟi ataiyum ōr eḻuttākak koḷḷiṉ, tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ mottam iru nūṟṟu nāṟpattēḻām.
peyar mutal, cārpu
cevippulaṉāṉa moḻiyolikaḷ kaṭpulaṉāṉa varivaṭivil eḻutappeṟṟataṉāl, eḻutteṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpatu varivaṭivaik kuṟippiṉ toḻilākupeyar; olivaṭivaik kuṟippiṉ kuṟiyākupeyar.
viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum  (1594)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyuḷḷapaṭi, tamiḻil mutalilakkaṇa nūliyaṟṟiyavaṉ, muṟṟat tuṟantavaṉum meypporuḷaṟivu nirampiyavaṉumāṉa muṉivaṉāvāṉ.
muṉital = (ulaka vāḻvai) veṟuttal, veṟuttut tuṟattal, muṉi, muṉai eṉṉum iraṇṭum orē colliṉ vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivamām. muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ, muṉaintavaṉ muṉaivaṉ.
muṉaivu muṉivākum  (tol. col. 386)
antaṇar nūṟkum  (543)
iṉṟīm pālai muṉaiyiṉ  (perumpāṇ. 180)
ulakiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ ellām, uyir, mey, uyir mey eṉa mūvakai. uyir tāṉē iyaṅkum kāṟṟu vaṭiviṉatu; mey uyiriṉ utaviyiṉṟi iyaṅkāta uṭampu allatu kaṉapporuḷ; uyirmey uyiroṭu kūṭiya uṭampu. pirāṇi (pirāṇaṉaiyuṭaiyatu) eṉṟa vaṭacol tamiḻil vaḻakkūṉṟiyapiṉ, ataṟku nērāṉa uyirmey eṉṉum tamiḻc col vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉatuṭaṉ, taṉ poruḷaiyum iḻantatu. tamiḻil mutaṉūl kaṇṭa muṉaivaṉ, mūvakaiyeḻuttukkaḷum oliyiyakkattil mūvakaip poruḷ kaḷai ottiruppatu kaṇṭu, apporuḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyē avveḻuttukkaṭkum uvamaiyāku peyarāka iṭṭāṉ. tāṉē olikkum eḻuttu uyireḻuttu; uyiroṭu kūṭiyallatu olikkāta eḻuttu meyyeḻuttu; uyirmeyyaip pōṉṟa eḻuttu uyir meyyeḻuttu. ittakaiya amaippu vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇpataṟkaritām.
kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉam eṉpaṉa veḷippaṭai.
uyir, āvi eṉpaṉavum; mey, uṭal, uṭampu, uṟuppu, oṟṟu puḷḷi eṉpaṉavum; oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
muṟaiyum piṟappum
tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭuḷ, muṟaiyum oṉṟām. itu mēlai moḻikaṭkillai. vaṭamoḻiyuṭpaṭa ēṉaiyintiya moḻikaḷellām neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaiyil tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavē.
uyir, muntittōṉṟiyatu paṟṟiyum, tāṉāy olippatu paṟṟiyum, neṭuṅkaṇakkil muṉvaikkappeṟṟatu. kuṟumai paṟṟik kuṟil muṉṉum, neṭumai paṟṟi neṭil piṉṉum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ā, ī, ū mūṉṟum, muṟaiyē cēymaic cuṭṭākavum aṇmaic cuṭṭākavum muṉmaic cuṭṭākavum iyalpākat tōṉṟiṉa maiyāl, piṟavuyirkaḷukku muṉ vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ākāram vāytiṟanta maṭṭil olippatāl mutalilum, īkāram vāyiṉ piṉpakkattil aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallaip poruntiyolippatāl iṭaiyilum, ūkāram vāyiṉ muṉpakkattil itaḻ (utaṭu) kuvintolippatāl kaṭaiyilum, vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu, vāyiṉ piṉpuṟattiliruntu muṉpuṟam nōkkiya varicaiyil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ amaikkap paṭṭatai aṟiyalām.
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ muṟaiyē īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷiṉ mōṉait tiripātalāl, iyalpākat tōṉṟiya mutal mūṉṟuyirkaṭkum piṉ vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ēkāram aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallaip poruntiyolippatāl muṉṉum, ōkāram itaḻ kuvintolippatāl piṉṉum, vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ai, au iraṇṭum muṟaiyē ai, au eṉṉum puṇaroli kaḷātalāl, aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallaip porunti yolikkum aikāram ēkārattiṉ piṉṉum, itaḻ kuvintolikkum aukāram ōkārattiṉ piṉṉum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āytam nuṇṇiya kakaramātalāl, kakarattiṉ muṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu.
meykaḷuḷ valliṉamum melliṉamum etiriṉaṅkaḷātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiyavaiyellām valiyum meliyumāka iv viraṇṭāy muṉ amaikkappeṟṟaṉa. iṭaiyiṉam avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum piṟameykaḷ oḻuṅku paṭuttappaṭṭataṟkup pintit tōṉṟiyamaiyāl, iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
meykaḷuḷ, ka, ṅa aṭiyaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, ca, ña iṭai yaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, ṭa, ṇa muṉṉaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum ta, na pallaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, pa, ma itaḻakap piṟappiṉavum, ātalāl ammuṟaiyē vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
makaramoḻinta melliṉa meykaḷellām taṉtaṉ valliṉa meykkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyamaiyālum, valiyolitta piṉ, meliyolippatē muyaṟcik keḷimaiyātalālum, valiyum meliyum aṭuttaṭuttu niṟpatu olivēṟṟumai yait teḷivāyk kāṭṭuvatālum, valimuṉṉum melipiṉṉumāka iṇaiyiṇai yāy niṟuttappeṟṟaṉaveṉa aṟika.
iṭaiyiṉa voliyiṉ iṭaittaṉmai valloliyum mel loliyum aṟinta piṉṉarē aṟiyak kiṭattaliṉ, valimeli kaḷiṉ piṉ iṭaiyiṉam vaikkappaṭṭatu. iṭaiyiṉa meykaḷuḷ, yakaram iṭaiyaṇṇattilum, rakaram muṉṉaṇṇattilum, lakaram pallaṇṇattilum, vakaram pallitaḻilum, piṟappatāl, avai ammuṟaiyē vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kumarikkaṇṭattil neṭuṅkaṇakkut toṭaṅkiyapōtu, atil amaintirunta eḻuttukkaḷ ittaṉaiyē. piṉṉar, nāḷaṭaivil ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum tōṉṟiṉa, ataṉālēyē avai iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
annāṉkaṉuḷ ḷa ḻa muṉtōṉṟiyamaiyāl muṉṉum; ṟa ṉa piṉtōṉṟiyamaiyāṟ piṉṉum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum muṉṉaṇṇa varuṭolikaḷāyiṉum (Linguals or Cerebals), ḻakaram ḷakarattiṉum caṟṟup piṉṉiruntu varuṭap peṟuvatāl, muṉvaikkappeṟṟatu.
iṟutimey nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiṉaveṉpataṟku ētuk kaḷāvaṉa :
1. 	ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum iṭaiyiṉattuḷḷum lakarattiṟku muṉṉum vaikkap peṟāmai.
2. 	ṟa, ṉa iraṇṭum valimeli yiṇaikaḷuḷ ta na iṇaikku muṉ vaikkap peṟāmai.
3. 	lakaram tiraṇṭu ḷakaramum ḷakaram tiraṇṭu ḻakaramum ātal.
	e.ṭu. kal-karumai. kal + tu = kaḵtu. kaḵṟeṉal = karumaik kuṟippu. kal - kāl = karumai. kāl - kāḷ = karumai. kāḷ - kāḻ = karumai.
4. 	rakaram tiraṇṭu ṟakaram ātal.
	e.ṭu. ar-aṟu, oḷir-oḷiṟu, muri-muṟi :
5.	ṉakarantōṉṟumuṉ nakaramē colliṟutiyilum vaḻaṅkiyamai
	e.ṭu. verin, paḻunu
6.	tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyum ēṉaiyolikaḷiṉ meṉmaiyum.
ṟakaram tōṉṟiyapōtu ataṟkiṉamākavē ṉakaramum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. tannakaram ṟakarattiṟku iṉamākāmai kāṇka.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmai, teṉmai muṉmai, meṉmai mutaliya taṉmaikaḷai aṟiyāmaiyāl, kālṭuvel uḷḷiṭṭa mēlai yārāycciyāḷar tamiḻaic camaṟkiruta aṭippaṭaiyilāyntu, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum muṟaiyil,tamiḻ 
neṭuṅ kaṇakku camaṟkirutattaip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉṟu muṭivu koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl cila koṇṭāṉa mārum tamiḻppaṟṟaṟṟa tiraviṭat tamiḻp pulavarum, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum vaṭamoḻi yilillāmaiyāl neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu, pitaṟṟalāyiṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiliṉ maiyē iṟutivaip piṟkuk karaṇiyamāyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyi lillāta ekara okaramum iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumē! aṅṅaṉamiṉmai yiṉ, atupōliyuraiyeṉa maṟukka.
uruvam
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyarē, tamiḻukku ilakkaṇan tōṉṟumuṉṉarē eḻuntiruntamaiyai uṇarttum, eḻutuvatu eḻuttu. eḻututal varaital.
eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōl  (kuṟaḷ, 1285)
iṉṉa palapala eḻuttunilai maṇṭapam  (paripā. 19,53)
eḻuvutal eṉpatu icaikkaruviyiṉiṉṟu oliyeḻuppu talaik kuṟikkumēyaṉṟi eḻututalaik kuṟikkātu.
paṇṭait tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷellām pirāmiyiṉ tiripāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttuk kaḷeṉṟum, kirantaveḻuttu teṉṉāṭṭil piṟkālattil vaṭamoḻik keṉṟēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉṟum, potuvākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, eḻuttuvaṭivam paṟṟiya cila tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷaiyum naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷaiyum, paṇṭaiyēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷaiyum nōkkumiṭattu, ikkaruttup poruttamuṭaiyatāyt tōṉṟavillai. iṟṟai vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟaiyē paṇṭai vaḻakkilum iruntateṉṟum, paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṭṭeḻuttu veṭ ṭeḻuttu eṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalāmeṉṟum, kirantaleḻuttu paṇṭait tamiḻeḻuttiṉ tiripēyeṉṟum, atu ēṭṭiṟkuriya āṇiyeḻutteṉṟum koḷvatē poruttamākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum ētukkaḷumāvaṉa :
1.	mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa tamiḻēṭṭilum iṟṟaiyeḻuttu vaṭivēyiruttal.
2.	ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iṟṟaiyeḻuttu vaṭivait toṉṟu toṭṭateṉak kūṟal.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.  (98)
eṉṟu 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟiṉār.
ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa tolkāppiyattiṉ nūṉmarapu eṉṉum mutaliyalil,
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram  (2)
āytam eṉṟa - muppāṟ puḷḷiyum.
uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvā kummē  (14)
meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal  (15)
ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē  (16)
puḷḷi yillā ... uyirtta lāṟē  (17)
eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivviyalpukaḷiṟ cila paṭṭaya veḻuttiṟkup poruntavillai.
(3)	paṭṭayaveḻuttu vaṭṭamāyirāmai. vaṭṭeḻuttu eṉpatu vaṭivu paṟṟiyatāyiṉ, atu ēṭṭeḻuttiṟkē (kiranta vaṭiveḻuttiṟkē) poruntu vatākum
(4)	ēṭṭiṟku vaḷaikōṭṭeḻuttum paṭṭayattiṟku nēr kōṭṭeḻuttumē icaivāyiruttal.
(5)	tamiḻeḻuttu acōkak kalveṭṭiṟkum vaṭamoḻi varaviṟkum muntiyatātal.
(6)	vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakku tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkaip piṉpaṟṟiyē amaittiruttal.
(7)	mēṉāṭukaḷilum karuvikkēṟpa eḻuttu vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭirun tamai.
intiya nākarikam āriyarateṉṟum, tamiḻ ellā vakaiyilum vaṭamoḻi yaip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟum mēlaiyaṟiñar kuruṭṭut taṉamākak koṇṭirunta aṭippaṭait tavaṟukaḷē, tamiḻeḻuttu pirāmiyiṉiṉṟu tirintateṉṟu koḷḷaṟ kiṭantantaṉa.
paṇṭaiyeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ cila iṉṟu māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avai varumāṟu :
eḻuttiṉ peyar	paṇṭaivaṭivam 		iṟṟaiyavaṭivam

	 puḷḷiyiruntaṉa)
ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ neṭilkaṭkup pintit tōṉṟiya maiyāl, avaṟṟai vēṟupaṭuttikkāṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ mēṟ puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷaip pēcuvōriṉ muṉṉōr, ekara okarakkuṟilkaḷ tamiḻil tōṉṟumuṉ kumarik kaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟatiṉālēyē, ammoḻikaḷil iṉṟum akkuṟilkaḷ illai. ammoḻikaḷiṉ mūlamākiya pirākirutaṅkaḷai vēta āciriyar pēciṉatiṉālēyē, avar vētattilum avai illai.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum āṇ(ṭu)
eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi
eṉṟu 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattiṉatāṉa naṉṉūl (98) kūṟuvatāl, tamiḻ ēṭṭeḻuttu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvateṉat tuṇiyalām. āṇṭu eṉpatu akkālattu (muṟ kālattil) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatākum. ekara okara uyirum uyir meyyum puḷḷipeṟṟa oṉṟē, paṇṭaiyeḻuttiṉ vēṟṟumai yeṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār pavaṇanti muṉivar. avar kālattilum avai puḷḷi peṟṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atai ēṉ vitantu kūṟal vēṇṭum? tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum eṉpati lēyē atu aṭaṅkiviṭumē! ātalāl, atu uraiyaṉṟeṉka.
eytum eṉpatu vaḻakkam paṟṟiya kālavaḻuvamaiti.
piṟkālattil vēṟṟaracu vantuviṭṭataṉālum, tamiḻaik kākkum poṟuppu centamiḻppulavar kaiyiliṉmaiyālum, ilakkaṇa maṟiyā māntar ēṭṭilum kallilum tappuntavaṟumāy eḻuti vantamaiyē, vīramā muṉivar muṉṉai muṟaippaṭi tiruttiṉār eṉa aṟika.
māttirai
eḻuttukaṭku māttirai iṉṉiṉṉa aḷapeṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭiruppiṉum, kūppīṭu (viḷi), oppāri (pulampal), icaip pāṭṭu, teruviṟpaṉai (paṇṭamāṟṟu) mutaliya ulakiyaṟ ceytikaḷil, avai cevippāṭu, tuyaram, iṉimai mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi, vēṇṭumaḷavu allatu iyaṉṟa aḷavu nīṇṭolikkum. atu aḷapeṭai eṉappaṭum. aḷapeṭuttal māttirai kaṭan toluttal. uyiraḷapeṭai, oṟṟaḷapeṭai eṉa aḷapeṭai iruvakai. oṟṟumey.
uyiraḷapeṭaiyai varivaṭiviṟ kuṟikkumpōtu neṭiliṉ piṉṉum, kuṟiliṉ piṉṉum iyalpākavuriya aḷaviṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ovvoru māttiraikkum ovvoru muṟaiyāka, avvakkuṟil allatu iṉakkuṟil vaṭivu cērtteḻutappaṭum. icaiyil aḷapeṭaikkuṟilkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi neṭilkaḷum toṭarntum viṭṭum olikkum; piṟavaṟṟil toṭarntē olikkum.
oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyāyiṉ, ellāviṭattum, kūṭiya ovvōr araimāttiraik kum oru muṟaiyāka avvam meyvaṭivē cērtteḻutappaṭum.
meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ valliṉam aḷapeṭukkā.
icai kuṟaintaviṭattu atai niṟaikkac ceyyuḷilum aḷapeṭai nikaḻum.
ulakavaḻak kaḷapeṭaiyum ceyyuḷvaḻak kaḷapeṭaiyum piṉvarumāṟu peyarpeṟum.
ulaka vaḻakku 			ceyyuḷ vaḻakku
iyaṟkai yaḷapeṭai	ceyaṟkai yaḷapeṭai
ulakiya laḷapeṭai		ceyyuḷiya laḷapeṭai
ceyyuḷiṟ kuṟil aḷapeṭuppiṉum, neṭilvaṭiviṉ piṉṉarē kuṟil vaṭivu kuṟikkappaṭum. valliṉattuṭaṉ ḷakara ḻakaramum ceyyuḷil aḷapeṭukkā.
kuṟṟiyalukara mēṟiya kakaram kakarattōṭu puṇariṉ, akkuṟṟiyalu karam kālmāttiraiyāyk kuṟukum.
e.ṭu. kokkukkāl.

palukkoliyam (Phonetics)
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ, mūkkoliyuyir allatu melliṉa uyir (Nasal vowel) eṉa oṉṟu tamiḻil illavē yillai.
avaṉ, vantēṉ, maram, pōṉōm mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa melliṉa meykaḷē, cōmpalāṟ cevvaiyāyp palukkap peṟāmal mūkkoliyaḷavāy olikkiṉṟaṉa. ivvoliyai īṟṟayaluyirmēlēṟṟi, ataṉai mūkkoli uyir eṉpatu tiripuṇarcciyē. avar, tāy, makaḷ, vantāl mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa iṭaiyiṉamey, ōriṭattum mūkkoliyāy oliyāmaiyai olittuk kāṇka.
moḻimutal ukaram itaḻkuvittē olikkappeṟuvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram coṉmutaṟkaṇ varavē varātu. tiraviṭap pulavar cilar karutukiṟapaṭi kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟumaṉṟu; olikkuṟuk kattai allatu tiripaik kāṭṭutaṟkē, ataṉmītu puḷḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.

kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa  (105)
eṉa māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar, āyiṉ, itai yuṇarātu kuṟṟiya lukaramum meyyīṟeṉap piṟaḻak koṇṭaṉar āriyavaḻit tamiḻāciriyar cilar.
ai, au iraṇṭum tamittu niṟkumpōtu, ai, au eṉa iru māttiraiyē olikkum; colluṟuppāy varumpōtē ay, av eṉa oṉṟarai māttiraiyāyk kuṟukum.
āytam kakaravoliyaic cārnta teṉpatai, atu toṉṟu toṭṭu olikkappaṭṭuvarum muṟaimaiyiṉālum, ēṉaccāriyai ēṟkumpōtu aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattaip paṟṟuk kōṭākak koḷvatālum, kuṟuṅkaṇakkilum neṭuṅkaṇakkilum kakara meykku muṉ vaikkappaṭṭiruppatālum, paṇṭai neṭuṅ kaṇakkēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyil ka - ḵ eṉa varaiyap peṟṟamaiyālum, aṟiyalām.
āytam varum coṟkaḷaiyellām āyntu pārppiṉ, atu perumpālum lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itai nōkkumiṭattu, kumarikkaṇṭattil ōriṭattu olittiri pākavō puṇarccit tiripākavō āytam tōṉṟi iruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
cilar āytattai vaṭamoḻi vicarkkameṉak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, atu akaramēṟiyum olikkumeṉak kūṟi,
aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu
peṟṟāṉ neṭituykku māṟu.  (943)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar aḷavaṟin tuṇṇuka eṉṉum pāṭattil taḷai cariyā yiruttaliṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷa vēṇṭiya tiṉṟām. nayaṉila colliṉuñ colluka eṉṉum kuṟaḷil (197). colka eṉpatu taḷaikkēṟpac colluka eṉa vantiruttal kāṇka.
iṉi, lakara ḷakara vakaram eṅṅaṉam āytamākat tiriya muṭiyumeṉṟu palar mayaṅkalām. pala olittiripukaḷ ellār vāykkum ēṟṟaṉavākavum, cila olittiripukaḷ ōriṭattu oru cilar vāykkē icaintaṉavākavum, amaintuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāṟ piṉṉavai piṟar mayaṅkutaṟku iṭamākiṉṟaṉa.
ḻakaram pala coṟkaḷil kakaramākat tirikiṉṟatu.

av + kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya
iṉi, ikkālattuc cilar āytaveḻuttaip piṟa tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum iṭṭu ja, ṣa F, Z mutaliya  piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷaik kuṟippatu, tamiḻiyl piṟkum marapiṟkum
muṟṟum māṟāṉatum tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷaikku muraṇāṉatum ākumeṉṟum, tamiḻaip piṟa moḻikaṭkup pintiyatākak kāṭṭumeṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷai yellām tamiḻeḻut tilēyē tiritteḻututal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, allākkāl tamiḻ nāḷaṭaivil vēṟu moḻiyāka māṟiviṭumeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. āytavolikkut tamiḻolikaḷai ayalolikaḷāka māṟṟum mantira vāṟṟal illai. oliyaip poṟuttamaṭṭil, āytavarivaṭiveḻutuvatum ayaṉmoḻi varivaṭiveḻutu vatum oṉṟē. eḻutteṉpatu uṇmaiyil oliyēyaṉṟi varivaṭivaṉṟu.
tamiḻ mikattoṉmaiyum muṉmaiyum meṉmaiyum vāynta moḻiyātaliṉ, ataṉ valliṉameykaḷum piṟamoḻi valliṉa meykaḷai nōkka melvalliṉamē. itaik kālṭuvelārum nōkkātu, tamiḻ valliṉattai vaṭamoḻi aivakuppiṉ mutal varicaik koppākak koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ valliṉameykaḷ iraṭṭittālaṉṟi āriya valliṉameykku īṭākā.

ṟṟa, ṉṟa eṉṉum iṇai meyyolikaḷai, muṟaiyē, tt, nd eṉṉum āṅkila meyyiṇai pōlap piḷaviṉṟi yolippatē tamiḻmarapām, aṅṅaṉa maṉṟip pirāmaṇaraip piṉpaṟṟi, ttr, ntr eṉa vitiroliyuṭaṉ (trill) piḷavupaṭap palukkuvatu vaḻuvām.
pirāṉmēyar (Fronhmeyer) eḻutiya paṭimuṟai malaiyāḷa ilakkaṇam (A Progressive Grammar of the Malayalam Language) eṉṉum nūliṉ 9 ām pakkattil kūṟiyiruppataik kavaṉikka. paṇṭaic cēranāṭṭut tamiḻē āriyattōṭu cērntu malaiyāḷamākat tirittiruppatāl, malaiyāḷa ṟakarattiṟkuc coṉṉatu tamiḻ ṟakarattiṟkum okkum.
ṟakaram taṉittum iraṭṭiyum ṉakarameyyaṭuttum varum mūvēṟu nilaimai paṟṟi, mūvēṟu olikaḷai aṭaiyum.
1. 	ṟa
	itu valiya allatu muraṭṭu rakaram. itaṟkoppāṉatu āṅkilatti lillai.
2. 	ṟṟa
	itu sit eṉpatilumuḷḷa t pōl, allatu sitting eṉpatiluḷḷa tt pōl olippatu.
3. 	ṉṟa
	itu send eṉpatiluḷḷa nd pōl olippatu.
ṅka, ñca, ṇṭa, nta, mpa, ṉṟa eṉṉum melivaliyiṇaikaḷē tamiḻiluṇṭu, ṅkka (nk), ñcca (nc), ṇṭṭa (nt), ntta (nth) mppa (mp) ṉṟṟa (nt) eṉṉum vaṉmelivaliyiṇaikaḷ tamiḻil nikaḻa immiyum iṭamillai.
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, tamiḻ iyalpai aṟiyātu, āṅkila varipeyarppil (Transliteration) koṭu eṉpataik koṭṭu eṉṟum, pāmpu eṉpataip pāmpu eṉṟum olikkumāṟu tavaṟākak kuṟittiruppatiṉāl, ataip piṉpaṟṟiya parō-emeṉō tiraviṭa oppiyal akaramutaliyum aṅṅaṉam vaḻuppaṭak kuṟittuḷḷatu.
munnilai yeḻuttukkaḷ
tamiḻ ulakap peru moḻikaṭkuḷ mika muntit tōṉṟiya melliya iyaṉmoḻiyātalāl. atiṟ piṟamoḻikaḷiṟ pōl ellā eḻuttukkaḷum coṉ mutaliṭai kaṭaiyākiya mūviṭattum varuvatillai. 
mutaṉilaiyeḻuttukkaḷ 
coṉmutalil uyirum uyirmeyyuntāṉ varum; āytamum meyyum varavē varā.
ṅa, ṭa, ṇa, ra, la, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum oṉpatumeyyum uyirōṭu kūṭi uyirmeyyākavum coṉmutal varā. uyirōṭu kūṭic coṉmutal varum meykaḷuḷḷum, ka ta na pa ma eṉṉum aintē paṉṉīruyiroṭuṅ kūṭivarum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟuḷ, ñakaram nāluyiroṭum, yakaram īruyiroṭum, vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum, kūṭivarum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟuḷ, ñakaram nāluyiroṭum, yakaram īruyiroṭum, vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum, kūṭivarum, avaiyāvaṉa:
ña, ñā, ñe, ño, ya (yavaṉar), yā : va, vā, vi, vī, ve, vē, vai, vau.
iṭainilaiyeḻuttukkaḷ
colliṭaiyil āytamum meyyum uyirmeyyumē varum; taṉiyuyir varātu. uyiruḷ, kuṟilmaṭṭum aḷapeṭaik kuṟiyāka varum.
k, c, t, p ākiya nāl valliṉa meykaṭkuppiṉ, taṉṉuyir meyyēyaṉṟi vēṟṟuyirmey varātu. itu uṭaṉilai meym mayakkam eṉappaṭum. piṟa meykaṭkuppiṉ piṟavuyirmeykaḷ cilavum palavum varum. itu vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkam eṉappaṭum. mayakkam kūṭṭam.
r, ḻ ākiya irumeyyum iraṭṭā; ēṉaiya iraṭṭum.
y, r, ḻ; ākiya mummeykkuppiṉ taṉimey varalām. piṟavaṟṟiṉ piṉ varā.
e.ṭu. vāyppu, tērcci, cīḻkkai.
mra, mla; vra, vla eṉṉum kūṭṭukkaḷ tamiḻil evvakaiyilum nikaḻā.
ṇña, ṉña; ñya, ṇya, nya, mya, vya, ṉya; mva eṉṉuṅ kūṭṭukkaḷ kūṭṭuc colliṭaiyilaṉṟi varā.
iṟutinilaiyeḻuttukkaḷ
āytamum valliṉameyyum colliṟutiyil varavē varā. piṟa meykaḷum uyirmeyyumtāṉ varum.
nāi (nāy) eṉṟu eḻutap peṟṟa paṇṭai vaḻakku iṉṟillai. uyirkkuṟil colliṭaiyiṟpōṉṟē colliṟutiyilum aḷapeṭaik kuṟiyāy varum.
r, ḻ, eṉpaṉa taṉikkuṟilukkuppiṉ collīṟāy varuvatillai.
tamiḻil eccollilum evviṭattilum piṟamoḻi yeḻuttum eḻuttumuṟaiyum varutal kūṭātu. piṟamoḻic col iṉṟiyamaiyātatum moḻipeyarkka muṭiyātatumāyiṉ, tamiḻ muṟaikkēṟpat tiritteḻutap peṟum. iṅṅaṉam varampīṭu ceyyākkāl, tamiḻ nāḷaṭaivil vēṟu moḻiyāy 

māṟiviṭum. itaṉālēyē vaṭacollaic ceyyuṭ colvakai nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟākak kuṟitta tolkāppiyarum, muṉṉōriṭṭa varampai mīṟātu.

viḻuttam (Accent)
oru colliṉ ōr acaiyil viḻum oliyuṟaippu viḻuttam eṉappaṭum. tamiḻil viḻuttam (1) iyalpuviḻuttam, (2) coṟpiri viḻuttam (3) colvakai viḻuttam eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
(1) iyalpuviḻuttam
iyalpu viḻuttam iyalpāka nikaḻvatu; atu eṉṟum colliṉ mutalil viḻum.
e.ṭu. koṟṟaṉ, vantāṉ
oliyuṟaippu varavarak kuṟaiyumātalāl, coṉmutalil eṭuttalum (Acute), iṭaiyil nalitalum (Circumflex), iṟutiyiṟ (Grave), paṭuttalum  nikaḻum.

aḻuttam (Stress or Emphasis)

mūvakaiccol
mutaṟkālattil ilakkaṇavakaiccol peyar, viṉai, iṭai eṉa mūṉṟākavē yiruntaṉa. ituvē ellāmoḻi kaṭkum potuvāṉa aṟiviyaṉ muṟaip pākupāṭām.
nālvakaiccol
āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻ kaṟṟut tamiḻāciriyarāṉa piṉ, iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticaiccol eṉṉum moḻi iyalvakaic coṟkaḷoṭu vaṭacollaiyum cērttuk koṇṭatu pōṉṟē, peyarccol, viṉaiccol, iṭaiccol eṉṉum ilakkaṇavakaic coṟkaḷoṭum uriccol eṉpataic cērttu, iru vakaiyilum coṟkaḷai nannāṉ kākkiṉar.
uriccol eṉpatu ceyyuṭkēyuriya col. col eṉpatu colvaṭivaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. āriyar ayalārāṉatiṉāl, tamakkut teriyāta aruñcoṟkaḷait tokuttup poruḷ uraittuk koṇṭaṉar.
1. peyarccol
oru poruḷiṉ peyarē peyarccol, peyvatupeyar, peytal iṭutal peyariṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
peyarccol, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉai, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṉum aṟuporuḷ paṟṟi, aṟuvakaippaṭum. irutiṇai paṟṟi, uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai, viravuttiṇai eṉa mūvakaippaṭum; mūviṭam paṟṟi, taṉmai, muṉṉilai, paṭarkkai eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. ciṉai = uṟuppu.
irutiṇaikkum potuvāṉa peyar viravuttiṇaip peyar allatu viravuppeyar eṉappaṭum.
v.L.: āṇ, peṇ, tāy, piḷḷai.
taṉmai muṉṉilaippeyarkaḷ, eṇ paṟṟi orumai paṉmai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. paṭarkkaip peyarkaḷ, pāl paṟṟi āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aivakaippaṭum.

3 ām nilai	nāṉ	nām		nāṅkaḷ
ilakkaṇavakaiccol mūṉṟum iyalpākat tōṉṟiya muṟai, potuvāka nōkkiṉ, iṭai, viṉai, peyar eṉpatākum.
uṇarcciyolikaḷum viḷiyolikaḷum kuṟippolikaḷum, māntaṉ vāyil muntit tōṉṟiyavaiyākum. ivai iṭaic col eṉpatu teḷivu. cappu, tuppu, vikku, mukku. vimmu, tummu, ici, ciri mutaliya muntiyaṟcoṟkaḷ, viṉaiyākat tōṉṟip piṉpu peyarumāyiṉa.
cila iṭaiccoṟkaḷ viṉaiyākip piṉpu peyarākiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. ol (iṭai) - oli (peyarum viṉaiyum).
mutaṟkālattil ol eṉpatē viṉaiyākavumiruntatu. iṭai-viṉai peyar eṉṉum immuṟai ellāccoṟkum taṉippaṭṭa nilaiyil ottataṉṟu. cila iṭaiccoṟkaḷ nēraṭiyāyp peyarākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. ella (ellā) - eluva - eluvaṉ (tōḻaṉ), eluvai (tōḻi).
ellā eṉṉum viḷiyoli iṉṟu ēla, ēlā eṉṟu vaṭivilum poruḷilum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cila coṟkaḷ mutalaṭiyilēyē peyarākavō viṉaiyākavō tōṉṟi viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. kākā- kākam, kākkā - kākkai (peyar) kara - karai (viṉai).
ivvirucollum āṅkilattil nērmāṟāy āḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nām kākam karaikiṟatu eṉkiṟōm, āṅkilarō ‘The crow caws’ eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
cila viṉaiccoṟkaḷ peyariṉiṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. 	uḷ - uṇ
		kaḷ - kaḷi, kātal - kātali, tēṉ - tēṉi
		uḷ = uḷḷiṭam. uṇṇutal = uḷḷiṭutal.
		kaḷittal = kaṭkuṭittal. tēṉittal - iṉittal.
ēṉ, ēm eṉpavai iṉṟu tamiḻiṟ peyarāka vaḻaṅkāviṭiṉum viṉaimuṟṟīṟāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu. vantēṉ, vantēm.
taṉmaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumai yaṭikaḷ
ēṉ		ēm		ēṅkaḷ 	
eṉ		em		eṅkaḷ
yāṉ		yām		yāṅkaḷ
nāṉ-naṉ	nām-nam	nāṅkaḷ-naṅkaḷ
naṉ eṉpatu iṟṟaittamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉālum teluṅkilum kaṉṉaṭattilum vaḻaṅki varukiṟatu.
naṅkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟum tamiḻil ceyyuḷ vaḻakkāyiṟṟu; 
naṅkaḷ itaṉ vantēṉ
yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum taṉittaṉmaip paṉmaip peyar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatiṉāl, iṉṟu ataṉ iṭattil nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmaippeyar taṉ uḷappāṭṭup poruḷaiyiḻantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ē eṉpatu uyarcci kuṟittalāl, iyalpāka māntaṉukkuḷḷa nāṉ eṉṉum cerukku allatu taṉṉalam paṟṟi, atai aṭiyākak koṇṭu taṉmaip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukkalām. vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇiyar taṉmaiyiṭattai uttama puruṭaṉ eṉṟum, āṅkilar ataṉai First Person eṉṟum, kuṟittataiyum nōkkuka.
ēpeṟ ṟākum.  (tol. uri. 7)
ekku, eṭṭu, eṇ, empu, evvu, eḻu, ēṇ, ēntu, ēr, ēṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷil ekara ēkāram uyarcci kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉṟu vaḻakkilillāta vaṭivaṅkaḷellām paṇṭait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉa aṟika.
muṉṉilaip peyar
	orumai paṉmai		iraṭṭaippaṉmai
mutalnilai	(ūṉ)	(ūm)		(ūṅkaḷ)
2-ām nilai 	(nūṉ) 	(nūm) 		(nūṅkaḷ)
3-ām nilai 	nīṉ 	nīm 		nīṅkaḷ
4-ām nilai 	nī 	nīyi (nī+ir)	nīṅkaḷ
piṟaikkōṭṭiluḷḷavai vaḻakku vīḻntaṉa.
nī eṉpatu nīṉ eṉpataṉ kaṭaikkuṟai. atu ir īṟupeṟṟa nīyir eṉṟākip piṉpu nīvir eṉat tirintu, nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nīṉ, nīm eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ iṉṟum nellai vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumai yaṭikaḷ :
(ūṉ) - uṉ 		(ūm) - um 	(ūṅkaḷ) - uṅkaḷ
(nūṉ) - (nuṉ) 		(nūm) - num 	(nūṅkaḷ) - nuṅkaḷ
nīṉ-niṉ 		nīm - (nim) 	nīṅkaḷ - (niṅkaḷ)
iṉṟu 4-ām nilai eḻuvāy vaṭivaṅkaṭkum mutal nilai vēṟṟumaiyaṭikaḷē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilaip peyarkaḷ ū eṉṉum muṉmaic cuṭṭaṭiyāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
mutaṟkālap paṭarkkaic cuṭṭuppeyar
		orumai 		paṉmai		iraṭṭaippaṉmai
mutalnilai (āṉ)  		(ām)  		(āṅkaḷ)
2 ām nilai tāṉ 		tām 		tāṅkaḷ
paṭarkkaip pakarappeyariṉ vēṟṟumaiyaṭikaḷ
tāṉ-taṉ 			tām-tam 		tāṅkaḷ-taṅkaḷ
ā eṉṉum cēymaic cuṭṭaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantuḷḷa tāṉ, tām eṉṉum paṭarkkaip peyarkaḷ, avaṉ, avaḷ mutaliya aimpāṟ cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyapiṉ, taṟcuṭṭup peyarkaḷāy (Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiviṭṭaṉa. (Auto) eṉṉum kirēkkaccollum, mutaliṟ cuṭṭup peyarāyiruntu piṉpu taṟcuṭṭup peyarāy māṟiyuḷḷamai iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ikkālap paṭarkkaic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ (Demonstrative Pronouns)
cēymaic cuṭṭup peyar : avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avai
aṇmaic cuṭṭup peyar : ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivai
muṉmaic cuṭṭup peyar : uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvai
ivai av, iv, uv; al, il, ul; eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭikaḷi ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavaiyākum.
al-atu, il-itu, ul-utu.
ukarac cuṭṭaṭip peyarkaḷ iṟṟait tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa; āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil vaḻaṅkuvatākat terikiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷ, ivarkaḷ uvarkaḷ eṉpaṉa iraṭṭaip paṉmai vaṭivaṅkaḷākum.
makkaḷ nākarikamaṭaintapiṉ, akavai aṟivu atikāram mutaliyavaṟṟil iḻintōṉaik kuṟikka orumaippeyaraiyum, ottōṉaik kuṟikkap paṉmaip peyaraiyum, uyarntōṉaik kuṟikka iraṭṭaip paṉmaippeyaraiyum, muṉṉilaiyilum paṭarkkaiyilum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. itu ulakavaḻakkēyaṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṉṟu. taṉmaiyiṭattiṟku iraṭṭaippaṉmai vēṇṭāviṭiṉum, oppumai paṟṟi atilum amaintuḷḷatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya mūviṭappeyarkaḷuḷ, taṉmaiyum muṉṉilaiyum (aimpāṟ cuṭṭumpeyar tavira ēṉaiyavellām), ṉakara meyyīṟṟāl orumaiyaiyum makara meyyīṟṟāṟ paṉmaiyaiyum, oḻuṅkāy uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ivvīṟukaḷ tōṉṟiya vakai varumāṟu:
ollutal = poruntutal, oṉṟātal.
ol - oṉ = oṉṟu, oṉ - aṉ, o. nō : E one-an.
oṉ eṉṉuñ collē aṉ eṉṟu tirintu yāṉ (nāṉ), nīṉ, tāṉ, eṉṉum peyarkaḷil ṉakara meyyaḷavāka niṉṟu orumai yuṇarttukiṉṟatu. aṉṉīṟu uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē + aṉ = ēṉ, ū - aṉ = ūṉ, ā + aṉ = āṉ.
o.nō: maka + aṉ = makaṉ. kō + aṉ = kōṉ (kōvaṉ)
ummutal kūṭutal, um eṉṉum viṉai iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟu, ataṉ aḷḷait tiripāṉa am eṉṉum collum, ataṉ vaḻiyaṭiyāṉa kum eṉṉuñ collumē vaḻakkiluḷḷaṉa.
am-amal - amalai = tiraḷai. amal-amar. amartal = poruntutal. am-amai. amaital = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal, am-ampu-ampal = kuvital, kuviyum mukiḻ. ampal - ampalam = avai, kūṭṭam, maṉṟam.
ma. ampalam, ka. ampala, tu. ampila.
ampalam - va. ampara. ciṟṟampalam - va. citampara (Cidambara)
um - kum. kummutal = kūṭutal. kum - kummal. kum - kumpu - kum pal.
um eṉṉum col kūṭutalaik kuṟittataṉālēyē, atu eṇṇupporuḷ allatu kūṭṭupporuḷiṭaic collākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
e.ṭu. eḻuttum collum poruḷum.
nāṉum varuvēṉ = nāṉ kūṭa varuvēṉ, itai oru ciṟu piḷḷaiyum ceytuviṭum = itai oru ciṟu piḷḷai kūṭac ceytuviṭum.
ivvaḻakkukkaḷil, ummaic collum kūṭaṟcollum muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.
ivvummaic collē, yām, nīm, tām eṉṉum peyarkaḷil makarameyyaḷavāka niṉṟu paṉmaiyuṇarttukiṉṟatu. kālṭuvelār karuttum ituvē. ammīṟu pōṉṟē ummīṟum, uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē + am = ēm, ū + am = ūm, ā + am = ām
o.nō : maṇa + am = maṇam, kā + am = kām (ācai)
mūvakaic cuṭṭuppeyar muṟaiyilēyē, e, ē, yā, eṉṉum viṉāvaṭi kaḷiṉiṉṟu aimpāl viṉāppeyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
viṉāppeyarkaḷ (Interrogative Pronouns)
e : evaṉ, evaḷ, evar (evarkaḷ), etu, evai
ē :  ēvaṉ, ēvaḷ, ēvar (ēvarkaḷ), ētu, ēvai
yā : yāvaṉ,. yāvaḷ, yāvar (yāvarkaḷ), yātu, yāvai
ē-e : ē-yā, e-ev, e-el-etu-ettu-entu. ē-ēl-ētu.
uyartiṇai āṇpālaik kuṟikkum evaṉ eṉṉum viṉāppeyarum, aḵṟiṇai yirupālaiyum uṇarttum evaṉ eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, vevvēṟām.
ētu eṉṉum col, ‘eppaṭik kiṭaittatu?  eṉṉum poruḷil kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟākavum varum.
e.ṭu. uṉakku itu ētu?
etu eṉṉum col ciṟitu aṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷ paṟṟiyum, yātu eṉṉum col ciṟitum aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷ paṟṟiyum viṉāvāka varum.
e.ṭu. kutiraivāli yātu? - ciṟitum aṟiyappaṭātatu. varaku ikkūlaṅkaḷuḷ etu? - ciṟitu aṟiyap paṭṭatu.
yāvar eṉṉuñ col, yār eṉat tokku uyartiṇai muppālaiyuṅ kuṟippatuṭaṉ, ulaka vaḻakkil ār eṉṟum maruvum.
yā eṉṉum viṉāvaṭi, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟ palaviṉpāl viṉāp peyarākavum vaḻaṅkum.
el-eṉ-eṉṉatu (orumai), eṉṉa (paṉmai).
ēl-ēṉ.
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (32) kūṟuvatāl, viṉāveḻuttu mutaṟkālattil neṭilākavē yiruntateṉpatum, atu ēkāramē yeṉpatum, uyttuṇarappaṭum.
ē-yā-ā-ō
yā colliṉ mutalilum, ā-ō colliṉ īṟṟilum, ē avvīriṭattum, viṉāvāka varum.
oru poruḷaip paṟṟi etu eṉṟu viṉavuvatu, ōriṭattiluḷḷa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟai eṭuttukkāṭṭac colvatu pōliruttalāl, eḻucciyai allatu uyarcciyai uṇarttum ēkāram viṉāveḻuttākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
paṭarkkaippeyarp pākupāṭukaḷ
taṉmaiyum muṉṉilaiyumaṉṟip paṭarvatu paṭarkkai paṭartal = parantu cellutal.
aṟuporuṭpeyar
ellāp poruḷkaḷum, poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉai, kuṇam, toḻil eṉa aṟuvakaippaṭum. ātalāl avaṟṟiṉ peyarum aṟuvakaiyām.
poruḷ eṉṟatu. kāṭcipporuḷākavō karuttup poruḷākavō uḷḷa oru taṉipporuḷai.
ētēṉum oru poruḷiruppiṉ, atu irukka iṭamum kālamum vēṇṭum. poruḷkaḷ, iyaṟkaiyāyiṉum ceyaṟkaiyāyiṉum, perumpālum pala vuṟuppukaḷai yuṭaiyaṉa. ovvoru poruṭkum cila taṉmaikaḷuṇṭu. attaṉmaikaḷē uṟuppaiyum uṭalaiyum tuṇaikkoṇṭu toḻilāka veḷippaṭum. iṅṅaṉam āyntu poruḷkaḷai āṟāka vakuttatum avaṟṟai muṟaippaṭuttiyatum, viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉaivaṉāṉa mutaṉūlā ciriyaṉiṉ, meypporuḷaṟivaiyum tarukkat tiṟaṉaiyum teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭum.
poruṭpeyar iyalvaraiyaṟai
pol-poru-poruḷ.
kuṟiyīṭṭaip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kuṇam eṉiṉum paṇpu eṉiṉum okkum.
muttiṇaip peyar
uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai, avviraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa viravuttiṇai eṉat tiṇai mūvakaippaṭum.
e.ṭu. 	uyartiṇaippeyar - avaṉ, māntaṉ, murukaṉ 
		aḵṟiṇaippeyar - atu, maram, kal
		viravuttiṇaippeyar - tāy, piḷḷai, āṇ, peṇ
		tiṇ - tiṇai = tiraṭci, tokuti, vakuppu.
aimpāṟpeyar
āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl eṉa uyartiṇaippāl mūṉṟum, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aḵṟiṇaippāl iraṇṭum ākap pāl aintām.
paku-pakal-pāl = pirivu.
āṇpāl, peṇpāl eṉṉum iraṇṭum orumaip pālkaḷ, āṇpeṇ vēṟu aḵṟiṇaiyuyirikaḷuḷḷum iruppiṉum, mutuṉūlāciriyaṉ ataṟku ilakkaṇattil iṭantaravillai. ēṉeṉiṉ, moḻiyai amaitta potu makkaḷē aṅṅaṉam amaittuviṭṭaṉar. ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyamputalē nūlāciriyaṉ ceyal.
kumarikkaṇṭap potumakkaḷ kūrmatiyum uyariya oḻukkamum uṭaiyarātaliṉ, nallataik kaṭaippiṭittut tīyatai nīkkum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta pakuttaṟivai aḷavai yākak koṇṭu, ataiyuṭaiya makkaḷai uyartiṇaiyeṉṟum, aḵtillātavaṟṟai yellām uyiriruppiṉum illāviṭiṉum aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟum vakuttu, ataṟkēṟpa, makaṉ varukiṟāṉ, makaḷ varukiṟāḷ eṉṟu uyartiṇaiyiṟ pāl pirittum, kāḷai varukiṟatu, āvu varukiṟatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇaiyiṟ pāl pirikkātum, ellāp peyarc coṟkaḷaiyum viṉaic coṟkaḷaiyum amaittuviṭṭamai, eṉṟum tamiḻaṉ eṇṇi makiḻtaṟkum taṉ muṉṉōraip paṟṟip 
perumai pārāṭṭutaṟkum uriyatoṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam vēṟēm moḻiyilu millāta ciṟanta ilakkaṇa vamaippu, iṟṟaip pula makkaḷiṉum aṟṟaip potumakkaḷ aṟivāṟṟalilum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭilum cālac ciṟantava reṉpataṟkut takka moḻiccāṉṟām.
