turuvutal eṉṉum poruḷil, uruvu, kuruvu,
curuvu, turuvu, - puruvu, eṉṟum; iṅṅaṉamē piṟavāṟum,
ēḻaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka.  iṅṅaṉam coṟkaḷ amaintiruttalai vēṟem moḻiyiluṅ kāṇa muṭiyātu. paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭāṉa paṇṭait tamiḻakam muḻuvatum muḻukip pōṉamaiyālum, āriyar varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyamaṉaittum aḻivuṇṭamaiyālum, iruvakai vaḻak kiṉiṉṟum pala iṇaippaṇṭuc coṟkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭa iyalavillai eṉa aṟika.
iṟṟai nilaiyatē paṇṭaittamiḻum eṉṟu karutik koṇṭum, camaṟkirutattai uṇmaiyil tēvamoḻi yeṉṟu nampik koṇṭum, tiraviṭamoḻiyāḷarellām tamiḻiṉ talaimaiyai oppukkoḷvatillai, āriya mayakkum itaṟkoru kāraṇam, poruḷilakkaṇamum eḻunilatteḻunta paṇṭaic ceyyuḷilak kiyamum muttamiḻppuṇarppum tiraviṭa mūlaṅkāṇṭaṟkēṟṟa varalāṟṟuk kuṟippum tirukkuṟaḷum, tamiḻiṉ talaimaiyai nāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷām.  iṉi, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ cemmaiyum tiraviṭac coṟkaḷiṉ koṭumaiyum, tuṇaic cāṉṟukaḷām.
tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci nilaikaḷ.
(1)	acainilai - Isolating or Monosyllabie Stage
(2)	puṇarnilai - Compounding Stage
(3)	pakucoṉṉilai - Terminational State
(4)	koḷuvunilai - Agglutinetive Stage
(5)	piriyānilai - Inflexional Stage
(6)	tokunilai - Synthetic Stage
(7)	paltokutinilai - Polysynthetic Stage
(8)	pirinilai - Analytical Stage
eṉa eṇvakaip paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
āppirikkarum āttirēliyarum cīṉarum, acainilaik kālattil kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu pirintupōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
turēṉiyar allatu cittiyar eṉappaṭum makkaḷ, koḷuvu nilaik kālattiṟ pirintu pōyirukkalām.
avarukkuc caṟṟē piṟpaṭṭa tamiḻaruḷ orucārār vaṭakkē ceṉṟu pirākirutarāka māṟiṉar. avarukkap piṟpaṭṭavar tiraviṭarākat tirintaṉar.
pirākirutarun tiraviṭaruṅ kalanta vakuppār orucārār vaṭa mēṟkākac ceṉṟu airōppāvil āriyarāka māṟiṉar.  avaruḷ oru piriviṉarē intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu vēta āriyarāyiṉar.
āriyaṉ eṉṟa peyar tōṉṟiyatu intiya nilattilēyē.  airōppiyarai āriyar eṉṟatu moḻiyiṉam paṟṟiyē.
āri = mēṉmai.  āriyaṉ = mēlōṉ. itteṉcollaiyē āriyar taṅkaṭku iṭṭukkoṇṭaṉar. intiya varalāṟṟu nūlil, āriyar eṉpatu, pūcakarōṭu kūṭiya āriya āyar kūṭṭattaiyē kuṟikkumēṉum, tamiḻāriyap pōrāṭṭattil āriyar eṉpatu, āriyap pūcāriyaraiyē eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum.
airōppā ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟiya pirākiruta tiraviṭar, mutaṟkaṇ kāṇṭiṉēviyam (Scandinavia) ceṉṟu taṅkiyataṉāl, ataiyaṭuttu tiyūttāṉiya moḻi tamiḻukkuc caṟṟu neruṅkiya tākavum, ataṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa ilattīṉam atiṉuñ caṟṟu vilakiyatākavum, ataṟkut teṉkiḻakkiluḷḷa kirēkkam mika vilakiṉatākavum, intiyā vanta āriyam mikamika vilakiṉatākavum, uḷḷaṉa.

vaṭamoḻiyiṉ ainnilaikaḷil tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa.
avaiyāvaṉa:-
(1) vaṭukanilai (2) airōppiya nilai. (3) pirākiruta nilai. (4) vēta nilai. (5) camaṟkiruta nilai.
ivvain nilaikaḷilum kalantuḷḷa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyil aintiliru pakutiyākum. tamiḻttuṇaiyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻi iyaṅka iyalātu. vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu kūṟi, teṉcoṟkaṭkellām poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum, valintum nalintum poruḷum poruṭkāraṇamuṅ kūṟum vaḻakkam, iṉip payaṉpaṭātu.  atai aṭiyōṭu viṭṭu viṭal vēṇṭum.
palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum tamiḻ talaimai yākavum (Main) vaṭamoḻi kīḻttuṇaiyākavum (Subsidiary) iruttal vēṇṭum.  potut tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭellām tamiḻilēyē naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇarē tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭai naṭatti vaikkalām. pirāmaṇak kuṭiyiruppukaḷiṟ pirāmaṇarukkeṉṟu kaṭṭiya kōvilkaḷil camaṟkiruta vaḻipāṭu naṭattikkoḷḷalām.
mutalamaiccar, kalviyamaiccar, palkalaikkaḻakat tuṇai vēntar, tamiḻttuṟait talaivar, kalvittuṟai iyakkunarkaḷ, kalvinilai yaṅkaḷil tamiḻāciriyaṉmār, varalāṟṟuttuṟai tol poruṭ ṭuṟai āciriyarum ārāycciyāḷarum talaivarum ākiya aṉaivar patavikaṭkum, nalla tamiḻarē amarttap peṟal vēṇṭum.  pariti māṟkalaiñaṉ pōṉṟavarāyiṉ pirāmaṇarum amarttap peṟa lām.
āṅkilappaṭṭam peṟṟa piṟapāṭa āciriyarkkuriya campaḷamē, pulavar paṭṭam peṟṟa tamiḻāciriyarkkum aḷikkappaṭal vēṇṭum.
ārāyccippaṭṭaṅkaṭkuriya iṭunūlkaḷai (Thesis)¤ tamiḻilum eḻuti viṭukka, aracum palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum icaivutaral vēṇṭum.
evvakaiyilum tamiḻukkut tāḻviruttal kūṭātu.
iravil matiyoḷi ettuṇai viḷakkamāyiruppiṉum kati roḷiyiṉ maṟuniḻalākavē yiruppatu pōṉṟu, iṟṟaic camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam ettuṇai virivupaṭṭiruppiṉum tamiḻ mūlattiṉ perukkamē eṉpatai, uṇartal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻiṉ taṉiyiyalpukaḷ
tamiḻ, varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta mutupaḻan toṉmoḻiyāta lālum, atu tōṉṟiya paḻam pāṇṭināṭu teṉmāvāriyil muḻukiyum, an nilatteḻunta mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum tamiḻp pakaivarāl aḻiyuṇṭum pōṉataṉālum, ataṉ taṉiyiyal pukaḷ neṭuṅkālat tiṭamāṉa naṭunilai ārāycciyiṉṟit talaimait tamiḻp pērāciriyarālum aṟiyavoṇṇātaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avviyalpu kaḷāvaṉa:-
1.  toṉmai
ōṅka liṭaivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ 
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉaiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ
eṉpatu, taṇṭiyalaṅkāra uraiyil mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa paḻam pā.
tamiḻai ulakat takaviruḷai yakaṟṟum cuṭarākac coṉṉatiṉ karuttu.  atu ulaka mutaṟṟāy uyartaṉic cemmoḻi yeṉpatē.  ataṉālēyē, ap pāvaip pāṭiyavar, tamiḻai oppuyarvaṟṟa moḻiyeṉṟuṅ kūṟi muṭittār.
eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatākac collappaṭum puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaiyiṉ āciriyarākiya aiyaṉāritaṉār, kuṟiñciyum mullaiyuṅ kalanta pālai nilattu maṟavar kuṭiyiṉ paḻaimai yaik kuṟikkumiṭattu.
poyyakala nāḷum pukaḻviḷaitta leṉviyappām
vaiyakam pōrtta vayaṅkolinīr - kaiyakalak
kaṟṟōṉṟi maṇṭōṉṟāk kālattē vāḷōṭu
muṟṟōṉṟi mūtta kuṭi  (pu.ve. 35)
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu,  kuṟiñci mullaivāṇar mikap paḻamaiyāṉa tamiḻ vakuppār. avar kuṭiyiṉ toṉmai kūṟavē.  tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyum uṭaṉ kūṟiyavāṟām.
iṉi, muttamiḻt tuṟaipōki muṟṟat tuṟantu mūvēntaraiyum muttamiḻ nāṭṭaiyum oppap pukaḻnta cēramuṉivar iḷaṅ kōvaṭikaḷ, ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil tāmiyaṟṟiya cilappatikārattuḷ.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmalai yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyu mimayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟicai yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi  (11:19-22)
eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatum: aṭiyārkkunallār, toṭiyōḷ pauvamum eṉṉum cilappatikāra vēṉiṟkātait toṭarukku,
akkālattu, avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavellai yākiya paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum kumariyeṉṉu māṟṟiṟku miṭaiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmali vāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturai nāṭum, ēḻ muṉpālai nāṭum, ēḻ piṉpālai nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟa nāṭum, ēḻkuṇakārai nāṭum, ēḻkuṟumpaṉai nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum, kumari kollam mutaliya paṉmalai nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟum, kaṭal koṇṭoḻitalāṟ kumariyākiya pauvameṉṟāreṉṟuṇarka eṉṟu viḷakkavurai varaintiruppatum: teṉmāvāriyil mūḻkippōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭaiyum ataṉ parampaiyu muṇarttum.
paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa tolaiviṉ aḷavum, palvēṟu nilappakutikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa muntu nūlkaḷuḷ allatu uraikaḷuḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  aṭiyārkkunallār avaṟṟaik kaṭṭik kūṟiyirukka muṭiyātu.
iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷuraiyiṟ kāṇum mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa, teṉmaturait talaikkaḻakamum kapāṭa (katava?) pura iṭaikkaḻakamum, muḻukippōṉa paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭuṭpaṭṭaṉavē.
oru kālattil, teṉṉintiyā, muḻukippōṉa teṉṉila vāyilāka āttirēliyāvuṭaṉum, teṉṉāppirikkāvuṭaṉum iṇaikkap paṭṭirunta taṉālum, tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyātalālum, māntaṉ piṟantakam maṭakācukkarp (Madagascar) pakkam eṉṟu (Haeckel) ekkēl pērāciriyarum, taṅkaṉiyikkāp (Tanganyika) pakkam eṉṟu ilīkki (Leakey) eṉṉum māntaṉūl ārāycci yāḷarum, kūṟiyiruppavai, paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭai oru pakuti yākak koṇṭa kumarikkaṇṭam eṉṉum oru perun teṉṉilap parappu, oru kālattirunta maikkup 
puṟaccāṉṟukaḷām.
tamiḻariṉ muṉṉōr teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷātalāl, tamiḻ varalāṟṟiṟkuk kumarināṭṭuk koḷkai iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṭippaṭaic ceytiyākum.
mūvēṟu kaṭalkōḷkaḷāṟ paṭippaṭiyāka muḻukippōṉa kumari nāṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭiyiliruntatu kumarimalai yeṉṟum, vaṭa kōṭiyiliruntatu kumariyāṟu eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
2.  meṉmai
māntaṉ piṟantakamum tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭināṭum ākiya kumarināṭu, nākarika nāṭāyiṉum, ulaka mutal nāṭātalāl, atil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻariṉ vāyil, uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumāka muppateḻuttukaḷē tōṉṟiṉa.  avaṟṟuḷḷum, ḻa,ḷa,ṟa,ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiyavaiyākum.
tamiḻccoṉ mutalil taṉimeyyum cila uyirmeyyum, col liṭaiyil, cila meykaṭkup piṉ cila vēṟṟumeyyum, col liṟutiyil valliṉa meyyum, varuvatēyillai.  ik kaṭṭup pāṭum vilakkum vēṇṭumeṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭavai yalla. māntaṉ vāyoli vaḷarcci nōkkiṉ, akkālat tamiḻar ciṟu piḷḷai nilaiyiliruntataṉāl, avar vāyil eḷiya muṟaiyil olikkak kūṭiya taṉiyolikaḷum kūṭṭolikaḷumē piṟantaṉa. ik kālattum, nellai māvaṭṭa nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷ, cākṣi eṉṉum vaṭacollaic cākki eṉṟum, jāti eṉṉum vaṭacollaic cāti eṉṟum olippataik kāṇalām. 
iṅṅaṉam eḷiya volikaḷaik koṇṭiruntum, tamiḻiṉ ōcaiyiṉimaikku eḷḷaḷavuṅ kuṟaivillai.
taṇṭalai mayilka ḷāṭat tāmarai viḷakkan tāṅkak
koṇṭalkaḷ muḻavi ṉēṅkak kuvaḷaikaṇ viḻittu nōkkat
teṇṭirai yeḻiṉi kāṭṭat tēmpiḻi makara yāḻiṉ
vaṇṭuka ḷiṉitu pāṭa marutamvīṟ ṟirukku mātō  (nāṭṭup. 41)
eṉṉuṅ kamparāmāyaṇac ceyyuḷaip pāṭik kāṇka.
oru moḻikku vēṇṭiyavai coṟkaḷē yaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttu kaḷalla.  kumarināṭṭup potumakkaḷ, muppatolikaḷaik koṇṭē, akkālattil maṭṭumaṉṟi iṉivaruṅ kālattum māntaṉ maṉattil tōṉṟakkūṭiya ellāk karuttukkaḷaiyum kuṟikkattakka, vērccoṟkaḷaip piṟappittiruppatāl, tamiḻukkup piṟamoḻi yolikaḷē tēvaiyillai.
urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉaittum olikkum mūvakaic ceyaṟkai vallolikaḷum, mūccoliyum, tamiḻiliṉmaiyāl atu ciṟu piḷḷaikaḷum nōyāḷiyarum kaḻiperu mūppiṉarum eḷitāy olikkat takkatāyiruppatuṭaṉ, ēṉai moḻikaḷi liyalāta nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tiruppukaḻ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum: valliṉap pāṭṭu, iṭaiyiṉap pāṭṭu, aṭimaṭakku, iru poru ḷiraṭṭuṟal, palporu ḷiraṭṭuṟal, naṭuveḻuttaṇi, muppāki, patiṉpaṅki, patiṟṟup patiṉpaṅki mutaliya collaṇikaḷum: kōmūttiri, cuḻikuḷam, tāmaraik kaṭṭu, cakkarakkaṭṭu, tērkkaṭṭu, irunākap piṇaiyal, eṇṇākap piṇaiyal mutaliya miṟaip pākkaḷum (cittirak kavikaḷum): pāṭaṟkēṟṟatāyu mirukkiṉṟatu.
3.  tāymai
makkaṭ perukkam, vaṇikam, koḷḷaikkum pōrukkum paḻikkum tappal, putunāṭu kāṇal, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya pala karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, tamiḻar vaṭakku nōkkic ceṉṟu, nāḷaṭaivil tamiḻ tirāviṭamākat tirintatāl tāmum tirāviṭarāyiṉar.  piṉṉart tirāviṭaruḷ oru cārār, mēṟkūṟiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟu, airōppāvai yaṭaintu āriyarāka māṟiṉar.
ātalāl, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumāyiṟṟu.
tamiḻ eṅṅaṉan tiraviṭamākat tirintateṉpataṟku, oru teluṅku viṉaiccolliṉ kūṟukaḷē pōtumāṉavai.

eṭuppolikaḷ (voiced sounds) āriyattiṟ pōṉṟē tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻt tōṟṟattai aṟiyātavarum oppukkoḷḷātavarum, tamiḻilum 
muṟkālattil paya, taya, ṉaya, ṉaya,ye eṉṉum eṭuppolikaḷ vaḻaṅkip piṉṉark ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa eṉṉum eṭuppila (unvoiced) volikaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpar. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷpavar ayalnāṭṭārum tamiḻp pakaivarum āvar.  piñcu muṟṟik kāyāṉa piṉ mīḷap piñcākātatupōl, eṭuppilā voli polintu eṭuppoli yāṉapiṉ, mīḷa eṭuppilā voliyāka māṟāteṉa aṟika.  teṉ ṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷai mēṉāṭ ṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷākat talaikīḻāyk koṇṭataṉālēyē, iṅṅaṉam talaikīḻāṉa muṭipiṟku vara nērntatu. itu iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa teṉpatai, kuḻantai vāyolikaḷaiyum vaḷarntōṉ vāyolikaḷaiyum oppunōkkik kāṇka.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉpatai, palvēṟu tamiḻ vērccoṟ kaḷiṉiṉṟum tirintu kuṭumpaṅ kuṭumpamākavum kulaṅkulamākavum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷuṭ ciṟcila.  āriyattiṟ kuṭumpat toṭarpuṅ kulattoṭarpumiṉṟit taṉittaṉiyāy vaḻaṅkuvataṉāl aṟiyalām.

4.  tūymai
ulaka moḻikaḷuḷ, tūymaiyai vēṇṭuvatum, ataik kāttuk koḷḷa vallatum tamiḻ oṉṟē.
5.  cemmai
tamiḻar pēriṉamāyp perukip perunilattiṟ paraviyapiṉ, coṟ kaḷiṉ vaṭivu potumakkaḷ vāyil iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭu vataiyum, avvēṟu pāṭṭiṟku ellaiyiṉmaiyaiyum, kaṇṭa ilakkaṇa nūlār, tiruttamāṉa vaṭivaiyē aḷavaippaṭutti ataṟkuc centamiḻ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar.
irukkutu, irukku, ikkutu, ikku, kītu eṉa vevvēṟiṭattil vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻaṅkiṉālum, ēṭeṭutteḻutum pōtum mēṭaiyēṟip pēcum pōtum, irukkiṉṟatu allatu irukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaṭivaiyē āḷavēṇṭumeṉpatu, tollāciriyar kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭa cemmaiyeṉṉum varampām.
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē. uyartiṇai yeṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē eṉṟu makkaḷ iyal varaiyaṟai ceyyappaṭṭirup patāl ataṟkēṟpa ellā viṉaikaḷaiyum paṇpaṭṭa muṟai yil iyaṟṟuvatē vilaṅkiṉum uyarnta nilaimaiyaik kāṭṭuva tākum.
āyiṉum, ic cemmaiyait tamiḻaḷavu ēṉai moḻikaḷ pēṇa iyalātu. tamiḻil vaḻunilaiyāyiruppaṉa pala ēṉaimoḻikaḷil vaḻānilaiyām. ātalāl, teṉmoḻikaḷ tamiḻum tirāviṭa mum eṉa iraṇṭākap pirittaṟippaṭum. tūymaiyuñ cemmai yum uṇmaiyiṉmaikaḷē itaṟkuk karaṇiyam.
6.  mummai
ilakkiya nilaiyil, icaiyum nāṭakamum iyaloṭu cērkkap paṭuvatāl, tamiḻ muttamiḻ eṉappaṭum.
7.  iyaṉmai
ēṉai yilakkiyap perumoḻikaḷilum, ilakkaṇam eḻuttum collum yāppum aṇiyum eṉa nāṟkūṟē koṇṭatu.  tamiḻilō ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum aṟiviyal muṟaiyiṟ pākupaṭuttum poruḷilakkaṇamum uṇṭu.
cilar, kātalum pōrum ākiya iraṇṭaiyē poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟumeṉpar. avar aṟiyār. kātalallāta ellāp poruḷ kaḷum puṟapporuḷāka vākait tiṇaiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
8.  viyaṉmai
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ulakeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: ammai appaṉ mutaliya peṟṟōr peyarkaḷ.
9.  vaḷamai
teṟkil īrāyiraṅ kal tolaivu nīṇṭirunta paḻam pāṇṭināṭu nīruḷ mūḻkiviṭṭataṉāṟ pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭa taṉāṟ pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭu mīḷā nilaiyaṭaintaṉa. 
āyiṉum, ikkālattum, ilai, tāḷ, tōkai, ōlai eṉṉum nālvakai ilaip peyarkaḷum: arumpu, pōtu, malar, vī, cemmal eṉṉum ainnilaip pūppeyarkaḷum: kaccal (vāḻai), vaṭu (mā), mūcu (palā), kurumpai (teṉṉai, paṉai) eṉṉum palvēṟu piñcukaḷiṉ ciṟappup peyarkaḷum, uḷḷaṉa.
yāṉaiyaik kuṟikka irupattaintiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
tottunōy (Infectious disease), oṭṭuvāroṭṭi (Contagious disease), tikkutal (to stammer), koṉṉutal (to stutter) eṉa nuṭpa vēṟupāṭu kuṟikkum oru poruṭ palacoṟkaḷum nirampavuḷḷaṉa. 
ivviyalpai yaṟiyātār vaṭacoṟ kaṭaṉkōḷ tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉpar.  vaṭacoṟ kalappāl, teṉcoṟkaḷ vaḻakkiṟantum vaḻakkuk kuṉṟiyum poruḷ māṟiyum vērp poruḷ maṟaintum iṟantupaṭṭumē pōyiṉa. ataṉāl tīmaiyēyaṉṟi naṉmaiyillai.
e-ṭu:
teṉcol	vaṭacol
vaḻakkiṟattal:
cūḷ, kaḻuvāy	āṇai, pirāyaccittam
vaḻakkuk kuṉṟal:
āṇṭu, makiḻcci	varuṣam, cantōṣam
poruḷmāṟal:
uyirmey, tōḷ	pirāṇi, pujam
vērpporuḷ maṟaital
akaṅkari, pattiriṉi 	ahaṅkāra, patnī
mēlum vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutamē āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷait tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇ ṭuḷḷatu.  ammoḻic coṟkaḷuḷ, ainti lirupakuti tamiḻ: ainti lorupakuti mēlaiyāriyam: ainti lorupakuti vaṭanāṭṭup pirākirutam: ainti loru pakuti putitākap puṉaiyappaṭṭavai.  vaṭa moḻittuṇaiyiṉṟit tamiḻ vaḻaṅka muṭiyum: āyiṉ tamiḻt tuṇaiyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkamuṭiyātu.
10.  iḷamai
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṟṟāy moḻiyāyiruntu patiṉeṇ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷaip piṟappittum, ēṟattāḻa aimpāṉ āriya moḻikaḷait tōṟṟuvittum, iṉṟum iḷamaiyāyiruppatu, ataṉ aḻiyāt taṉmaiyaik kāṭṭum.  eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻi yampiyicai koṇṭāṉ eṉṟār kampar. āriyampō lulakavaḻak kaḻintoḻintu citaiyāvuṉ, cīriḷamait tiṟamviyantu ceyaṉmaṟantu vāḻttutumē eṉṟār cuntarampiḷḷai. kāṇka: tamiḻiṉ taṉipperuntaṉmaikaḷ kaḻakap poṉviḻāmalar 1970.  (urimai vēṭkai poṅkal malar 1974)
tamiḻiṉ toṉmai
nilanūliyal ūḻikaḷ (Geological Eras)
1. toṭakkanilaiyuyir ūḻi (Archezoic Era)
2. muntunilaiyuyir ūḻi (Proterozoic Era)
3. paṇṭainilaiyuyir ūḻi (Paleozoie Era-Primary Period)
4. iṭainilaiyuyir ūḻi (Mezozoic Era-Secondary Period)
5. aṇmai nilaiyuyir ūḻi (Cainozoic Era)
aṇmai nilaiyuyir ūḻip pirivukaḷ
1.  mūṉṟām maṇṭalam (Tertiary Period):-
(1)	viṭiyaṇmai (Eocene)
(2)	cillaṇmai (Miocene)
(3)	pallaṇmai (Pliocene)
2.  nāṉkām maṇṭalam (Quarternary Period):-
(1)	kaḻipallaṇmai (Pleistocene)
(2)	aṇṇaṇimai (Recent)
nāṉkām maṇṭalap pirivum kālamum
1.  kaḻipallaṇmai
1. 	mutaṟ paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (1st Glacial Advance) - ki.mu. 450,000 - 475,000.
2.	mutaṟ paṉikkaṭṭip paṭala iṭaikkālam (1st Inter-Glacial Epoch) - ki.mu. 400,000 - 450,000.
3.	iraṇṭām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (2nd Glacial Advance) ki.mu. 375,000-400,000
4.	iraṇṭām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala iṭaikkālam (2nd Inter Glacial Epoch) - ki.mu.175,000-375,000.
5.	mūṉṟām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (3rd Glacial Advance) - kimu. 150,000 - 175,000.
6.	mūṉṟām paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala iṭaikkālam (3rd Inter Glacial Epoch) - ki.mu. 50,000 - 150,000.
7.	nālām paṉikkaṭṭip paṭala muṟpaṭarcci (4th Glacial Advance) - kimu. 25,000 - 50,000.
2. aṇṇaṇimai
paṉikkaṭṭip paṭalap piṉṉaik kālam (Post Glacial Advance) - tōrā. 25,000 āṇṭukaḷ.
māntak kuraṅkiṉ vaḷarccik kālam.
1.	neṟṟuṭaippāṉ kurakku māntaṉ (Nut-cracker man or Sinjanthropos Boisi) kālam - tōrā. ki.mu. 600,000.
2. 	nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropos erectus) kālam - tōrā. ki.mu. 500,000.
māntaṉ tōṟṟa vaḷarccik kālam
1.	māntaṉ tōṟṟam - tōrā. ki.mu. 100,000.
2. 	tamiḻaṉ (nākarika māntaṉ) tōṟṟam - tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000.
3. 	muḻu nākarikat tamiḻaṉ tōṟṟam - tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000
mēlaināṭukaḷuḷ mutaṉmutalāka nākarikamaṭaintatu ekipatu.  ataṉ nākarikat tōṟṟam ki.mu. 6000 eṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ekipatiya nākarikattiṟku muntiya kumarināṭṭut tamiḻ nākarikat tōṟṟam ki.mu. 10,000 eṉṟu kūṟuvatil, oru ciṟitum uyarvu naviṟciyillai. paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅ karait teṉmaturait talaikkaḻakat tōṟṟak kālamum atuvē.
māntaṉūṟ pirivukaḷ
1.	uṭaliyal māntaṉūl (Physical Anthropology)
2.	kumukavil māntanūl (Social Anthropology)
3.	paṇpāṭṭiyal māntaṉūl (Cultural Anthropology)
tamiḻiṉ toṉmai
ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ epirēyattil eḻutappaṭṭa yūta aracar varalāṟṟilum nāṭpottakattilum (Chronicles) uḷḷa tuki (tōkai) eṉṉuñ collai, tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyaik kuṟikkum mutalilakkiyac cāṉṟāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār kālṭuvelār.
āyiṉ, ki.mu. 1200 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭa āriya vētattil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ uḷḷamaiyē, tamiḻiṉ toṉmaikku atiṉum ciṟanta ilakkiyac cāṉṟām.
(1) cāyam
e.ṭu.	cāyam (sāyam) = cāyuṅkālam.
		cāy - cāyum (etirkālap peyareccam).
		cāyuṅkālam = poḻutu cāyum vēḷai.
poḻutu cāykiṟatu, poḻutu cāya vantāṉ, eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻakkam.
cāyuṅkālam eṉpatu, cāyaṅkālam, cāyṅkālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkil tiriyum.
iṅṅaṉamē, cāyuntaram eṉṉum collum cāyantaram, cāyntaram eṉat tiriyum. cāytal eṉṉum col taṉittum poḻutu cāytalaik kuṟikkum. ceykaiyilē eṉṉum 7-ām vēṟṟumai yurupēṟṟu ēkāra vīṟu peṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar oru nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccattuṭaṉ cērntu, avviṉaic collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum ceyal nikaḻum nilaiyai uṇarttuvatu marapu.
e.ṭu.	varacceykaiyilē (varumpōtu), pōkac ceykaiyilē 
	(pōkum pōtu)
ic ceykaiyilē eṉṉum col, cilē, cē eṉṟu koccai vaṭivuṅkoḷḷum.
e.ṭu.	varaccilē, pōkaccilē
		varaccē, pōkaccē
ik koccai vaṭivu koṇṭa cāyaccē eṉṉum col, cāykiṟa vēḷai eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu pōl cāykiṟaccē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa vaṭivu koṇṭa piṉ, cāyaraṭcai eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.
cāyaṅkālam eṉṉum vaṭiviluḷḷa cāyam eṉṉum nilai moḻiyē, cāyuṅkālam (eṟpāṭu) eṉṉum poruḷil muṉpu vaṭa tirāviṭattil vaḻaṅkip piṉpu āriya vētattiṟ pukuntu ataṉpiṉ camaṟkirutattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ivvuṇmaiyai maṟuttum maṟaittum talaikīḻāy māṟṟiyum sāyam eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟē cāyaṅkālam eṉṉum teṉcol vantateṉṟu tuṇintu ēmāṟṟuvar.  ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻakara mutaliyilum, itai vaṭa colleṉṟu kuṟittatuṭaṉ ivvēmāṟṟuk koḷkaiyai valiyuṟuttavē, cāyuṅkālam, cāyuntaram eṉṉum moḻip poruṭ kāraṇam tōṉṟum vaṭivukaḷai vēṇṭu meṉṟē kāṭṭātu viṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
sāyam eṉṉum colliṟku vaṭavar poruntap poyttalākak kāṭṭum vēr muṭittaṟ poruḷai uṇarttum sō eṉṉum col.  atuvum cāy eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirinta cā eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ tiripē.
(2)  tā.
iccol (ilavacamākak) koṭuttal eṉṉum poruḷil, vaṭamoḻiyil tā (da) eṉṟum, ilattīṉil tō (do) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. itaṉ tamiḻp poruḷ ottōṉukkuk koṭuttal eṉpatu.
ītā koṭu eṉak kiḷakkum mūṉṟum
iraviṉ kiḷavi ākiṭaṉ uṭaiya.
avaṟṟuḷ,
īyeṉ kiḷavi iḻintōṉ kūṟṟē.
tā eṉ kiḷavi oppōṉ kūṟṟē.
koṭu eṉa kiḷavi uyarntōṉ kūṟṟē.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam (927, 28, 29, 30).
putup perukku nīraik kuṟikkum veḷḷam eṉṉum col taṉ ciṟappup poruḷaiyiḻantu nīr eṉṉum potup poruḷil malaiyāḷattilum, viṭai collutalaik kuṟikkum ceppu eṉṉuñ col taṉ ciṟappup poruḷaiyiḻantu collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷil teluṅkilum, vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē; ottōṉukkuk koṭuttalaik kuṟikkum tā eṉṉum collun taṉ ciṟappup poruḷaiyiḻantu koṭuttal eṉṉum potup poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉṟaṟika. toṉṟu toṭṭu iruvakai vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅki varutalum, aṭippaṭaiyaic cērnta eḷiya collāyiruttal, ciṟappup poruḷ koṇṭu ēṉaiyiru tūya teṉ coṟkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaimaiyum, tā eṉpatu tamiḻc collē eṉpataṟkut takka cāṉṟukaḷām.  taravu, tarukai, taraku, tattam, tāṉam ākiya coṟkaḷellām tā eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tirintavaiyē. iṟutiyiraṇṭum vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkuvataṉālēyē vaṭacoṟ pōl tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, tā eṉṉum viṉaiccol paṭarkkaiyiṭattil varāteṉṟu vilakkappaṭṭiruppatum, atu tamiḻccollē yeṉpatai uṇarttum.
tarucol varucol āyiru kiḷaviyum
taṉmai muṉṉilai āyī riṭatta.  (tol. 512)
(3)  muttu
iccol uruṇṭaiyāṉatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.  itaṉoṭu toṭarpuṭaiya coṟkaḷ muttai, muṇṭu, muṇṭai, muṭṭu, muṭṭai, muṭṭi, muṭṭāṉ mutaliyaṉa.
muṇṭu = uruṇṭa kaṭṭai. muṇṭai - muṭṭai.
muṇṭai viḷaipaḻam (patiṟṟup 60.6)
muṇṭam = uruṇṭa kaṭṭai, kai kāl talaiyillā uṭampu.
muttai = cōṟṟuruṇṭai.  muttai - mottai.
muṭṭu = naṭattiṟkuriya uruṇṭa paṟai.
muṭṭi = kommaṭṭi.
muṭṭāṉ = tirunīṟṟiṟkuriya cāṇavuruṇṭai, mañcaṭkiḻaṅku.
mitukkam paḻattaik kuṟumuttam paḻam eṉpatu karūr vaḻakku.  uruṇṭu ciṟattirukkum vitaikaḷaiyellām mutteṉpatu potuvāṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
ulakil muttu viḷaiyum palaviṭaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pāṇṭi nāṭṭu (maṉṉārkuṭā)k kaṭal.
muttu periyatāyiruppiṉ muttam eṉappaṭum.  am eṉpatu oru perumaip poruḷ īṟu (Augmentative suffix):
x.neh.: mati = nilā. matiyam = muḻunilā.
nilai = tērnilai pōṟ ciṟiya iṭam.  nilaiyam = pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyam pōṟ periya iṭam.
viḷakku = vīṭṭu viḷakkup pōṟ ciṟiyatu.  viḷakkam = kalaṅkarai viḷakkampōṟ periyatu.
muttam eṉṉum teṉcollai vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mukta eṉṟu tirittu, cippiyiṉiṉṟu viṭutalai (mukta) peṟṟateṉṟu vērp poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟi vaṭa collākak kāṭṭa muyalvatu, kuṟumputtaṉamāṉa kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology) ākum.
muttu eṉpatu, tamiḻil ciṟitāy uruṇṭu tiraṇṭirukkum palvēṟu poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikkum.
muttu: 1. kiḷiñcilil viḷaiyum maṇivakai.
muṟimēṟi mutta muṟuval  (kuṟal.1113)	
2.	uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa vitai.
e.ṭu. āmaṇakku vitai. muttirukkuṅ kompacaikkum
(taṉippā. 1.3.2).
3.	uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa āṭṭakkāy.
4.	periyammaik koppuḷam.
5.	kaṇṇīrttuḷi parumuttuṟaiyum (cīvaka.1318)
6. 	paṉi muttu nīrttuḷi. nīrc cāntaṭainta mū uy  (paripā.10. 13).
uruṇṭu tiraṇṭirukkum eṇṇeyuḷḷa vitaikaḷ mutteṉṟē peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu.	āmaṇakkumuttu ( muttukkoṭṭai, koṭṭai muttu), 
	kurukku muttu, vēppamuttu.
muttuccampā, muttuccōḷam eṉpaṉa uruṇṭu tiraṇṭu muttuppōli ruppaṉa.  uruṇṭu tiraṇṭuḷḷa kōraikkiḻaṅku muttakkācu eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu.
muttukkuḷippu varalāṟṟiṟkeṭṭāta toṉṟu toṭṭup pāṇṭi nāṭṭu maṉṉārkuṭāk kaṭalil naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. muttūrkkūṟṟam eṉṟu or āḷnilappirivum paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭiliruntu kiṟittuviṟku muṉ pāṇṭināṭṭu muttu mēṉāṭukaṭku ērāḷamāy ēṟṟumatiyāṉatum, urōm nakarap perumāṭṭiyar aḷavaṟṟa poṉṉai ataṟkuc cela viṭṭatum, varalāṟaṟinta uṇmaiyākum.  pāṇṭiya kavāṭam eṉṉum parumuttavakai cāṇakkiyariṉ artta cāttiram eṉṉum poruḷnūliṟ ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  toṇ 
(nava) maṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa muttu veṇṇiṟamuḷḷa teṉpatu, cīrkeḻu veṇmuttam (pālaikkali,9) eṉṉum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkālum, vāytiṟantāl muttutirntu viṭumā? eṉṉum ulakavaḻakkālum aṟiyappaṭum.
iṉi, vēta āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟkuḷ (intiyāviṟkuḷ) kāl vaippataṟku īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muntiya pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, āriyam eṉṉum pērē tōṉṟuvataṟku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muntiya ekiptu moḻiyilum, maṟukka 
muṭiyāta tamiḻccoṟkaḷ aṭippaṭaiyāyuḷḷaṉa.  ākavē mēlaiyulakil (tōrā. ki.mu. 5,000) mutaṉmutalāka nākarika maṭainta ekiptu nāṭṭu moḻiyil, oru col, irucol alla,  pala coṟkaḷ, avaiyum aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ, tamiḻā yiruntatē tamiḻiṉ toṉmaikkut talaiciṟanta ilakkiyac cāṉṟākum.
ekipatiyat tolvaralāṟ ṟārāycciyāḷarāṉa villiyarcu tūvarṭṭu (Villiers Stuart) eṉpavar, airōppiyariṉ muṉṉōruṭa perumpālār āciyāviṉiṉṟu pāpel mantepu (Babel Mandeb) eṉṉum nīriṇaippu (Straits) vaḻiyāka āppirikkak karaiyēṟi ettiyōppiyāvil cilakālan taṅkiyapiṉ ekipatu nāṭṭu nīlāṟṟu veḷinilattiṟkup paraviṉareṉṟum, piṉpu aṅkiruntu naṇṇilak kaṭal (Mediterranean Sea) kaṭantu airōppāviṟ kuṭiyēṟi āriyarāy māṟiṉāreṉṟum, ekipatiyar paṉaiyōlai pōl eḻutu karuviyākap payaṉpaṭuttiya pāppiracu (Papyrus) eṉṉum tāḷ allatu tōkaiyuḷḷa nāṇal mutalil apiciṉiyāvilēyē iyaṟkaiyāy viḷaintateṉṟum, ekipatiyariṉ tēviyal (Sacred) uyirikaḷum 3 -ām 4 -ām 5 -ām āḷkuṭi (Dynasty) aracariṉ kallaṟaic cuvarkaḷil varaiyap peṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷum apiciṉiyāviṟkum teṉṉāppirikkā 
viṟkumē yuriyavaiyeṉṟum ekipatiyak kuṟunila aracukaḷai yellām mutaṉmutalāka oṉṟāka iṇaittu ōraracāṭci niṟuviya meṉecu (Menes)ntªjD« teṟkattiyāṉēyeṉṟum, ekipatiya moḻi airōppiya moḻikaṭkellām mūlamāṉa acainilai (Monosyllabic) moḻiyeṉṟum, atu camaṟkirutat tiṟku 3,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭateṉṟum tam ōr ekipatiya araciyiṉ paḷḷipaṭaip paṭamāṭam (The Funeral Tent of An Egyptian Queen) eṉṉum varalāṟṟārāycci nūliṉ iṟutiyil, moḻinūl (Philology) eṉṉum talaippil, takka cāṉṟu kāṭṭit tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāyk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
itiṉiṉṟu, kaṭal kōḷukkut tappiyum vēṟṟu nāṭu paraviyum ceṉṟa mutupaṇṭaik kumari māntaruḷ vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟa oru peru vakuppār, cōmālināṭu (Somaliland) apiciṉiyā (Abyssinia) eṉṉum ettiyōppiyā, ekipatu ākiya nāṭukaḷil muṟaiyē paravi, piṉpu airōppāviṟkuc ceṉṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.  ituvē, āttirēliyāviṟkum teṉṉāppirik kāviṟkum iṭaippaṭṭirunta kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭamēṟkāc celvārkku ēṟṟa iyaṟkaiyāṉa vaḻi eṉpatai ñālappaṭam (Atlas)  nōkkik kaṇṭukoḷka.

tamiḻiṉa oṟṟumai
iṅkilāntil, aipīriyar, kelattiyar (Celts), pittiyar (Piets), kāṭṭiyar (Scots), āṅkilar, cākacaṉiyar (Saxons), cūtiyar (Jutes),njÅa® (Danes), nārmaṉiyar (Normans) mutaliya oṉpāṉ allatu paṉṉiru ciṟṟiṉaṅkaḷ kalanta kalavaip pēriṉamāyirup pavar iṟṟai āṅkilēyar. ataṉāl, mati nuṭpamuḷḷa makkaḷ piṟantu, nīrāviyum neyyāviyum miṉvicaiyum koṇṭi yaṅkum palvēṟu poṟikaḷaiyum ūrtikaḷaiyum puṉaintu, iṟṟai aṟiviyalkaḷait tōṟṟuvit tirukkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭilē, ōrēyiṉam āriyac cūḻcciyāl nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa akamaṇap pirivukaḷākac citaikkappaṭṭu viṭṭataṉāl, iṉṟum tamiḻar

ceṉṟeṟi ciralum paṉṟiyum paṇaiyum  (cilap.15:207-14) 
nūṟṟuvaraik kolli, taḷḷiveṭṭi, kaḷiṟṟuppoṟi, viḻuṅkum pāmpu, kaḻukuppoṟi, pulippoṟi, kuṭappāmpu, cakaṭappoṟi, takarppoṟi, arinūṟpoṟi mutaliyaṉavumāṉa, muṉṉōr ceyta matiṟpoṟikaḷaiyuñ ceyyat teriyātu, 
māṭṭu vaṇṭiyum kutirai vaṇṭiyum nara vaṇṭiyum toṭar vaṇṭik kūṇṭumē ceyyum nilaiyiluḷḷaṉar.
iṅkilāntiluḷḷa makkaḷ peyarellām peyaraḷavil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉavē yaṉṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟpōl cemparlēṉ mutaliyār, carccil ceṭṭiyār, attilīk kavuṇṭar, īṭaṉ nāṭār, vilcaṉpiḷḷai eṉṟu paruḷaṟṟavālkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkuvatillai .(iṅṅaṉamē ēṉai nāṭukaḷilum).
aṉṟiyum, aṅku makkaḷ vakuppai viṉaviṉ, toḻilaṭip paṭaiyil uḻavar vaṇikar āciriyar kaṇakkar eṉṟu collu kiṉṟaṉarēyaṉṟi, iṅkuppōl āciriyaṉaik kammāḷaṉ (āccāri) eṉṟum, kaṇakkaṉai vaṇikaṉ (ceṭṭiyār) eṉṟum, kaṭṭaṭavēlai ceypavaṉaip paṭaittalaivaṉ (mutaliyār) eṉṟum, itayaṟkāraṉaippaṭakaṉ (pāṇaṉ) eṉṟum, orēn valai bcaypavaraippalvēṟu btāḻiṟkulattārākavum, ilvāḻvāṉaittuṟavi (aiyar)eṉṟum, pakuttaṟiviṉṟi cbcālvatillai. iṉi, māṇavaṉiṉ aṟiyāmaiyaip pōkki avaṉukku uṇmaiyāṉa ceytikaḷai aṟivikkak kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷa āciriyaṉ,  māntaṉūlum (Anthropology) uṭalnūlum (Physiology) kaṟṟavaṉā yiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku māṟāka, piṟappaṭippaṭaiyil avaṉai ōr iṉamākavum (Species) taṉṉai maṟṟōriṉamākavum kūṟuvatum, iṅṅaṉamē kalviyatikāriyum kalvittuṟai iyakkunarum kalviyamaiccarum palkalaikkaḻakat tuṇaik kaṇkāṇakaruṅ kūṟuvatum, āciriyaṉ māṇavaṉaip piṟappil tāḻntavaṉeṉṟum āciriyaṉaip piṟappil tāḻntavaṉeṉṟum koḷvatum, ettuṇaik kēṭāṉa ceyal! ittakaik kalviyāl oru nāṭu muṉṉēṟa muṭiyumō?
piṟavik kulap piriviṉaiyāl viḷaiyum peruṅ kēṭukaḷ
(1)	ulakuḷḷa aḷavum oṟṟumai yiṉmai.
(2)	iṉa muṉṉēṟṟa miṉmai.
(3)	tamiḻ moḻi yilakkiya vaḷarcci yiṉmai.
(4)	tamiḻap paṇpāṭ ṭaḻivu.
(5)	aṭimaittaṉamum tāḻvum toṭarkai.
(6)	mativiḷakkamum uṭal valimaiyumuḷḷa makappēṟiṉmai.
(7)	ayalārkkum takutiyillārkkum patavip pēṟu.
(8)	pakuttaṟivaip payaṉ paṭuttāmai.
oruvar ettuṇai yuyariṉum, tammai nāṭār eṉṟu collik koḷḷiṉ, kula ēṇiyil avarukkuriya paṭiyil tāṉ avarai vaippar.  ataṉāl avar veḷḷāḷaroṭu uṟavāṭa muṭiyātu.  aṅṅaṉamē, veḷḷāḷarum ettuṇai uyarvā yiruppiṉum, avar pirāmaṇarukkut tāḻntavarākavē karutappaṭuvar.  ātalāl, āriyak kula ēṇiyait tūḷtūḷākkic cuṭṭerittal vēṇṭum. oru kulattāriṭai oru naṟpaḻakka miruppiṉ, ataip piṟarum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum; tīppaḻakka miruppiṉ atai viṭṭoḻikka vēṇṭum. nākarikam, tuppuravu, oḻukkam, kalvi, celvam, atikāram, matinuṭpam mutaliyavaṟṟālēyē uṇmaiyāṉa uyarvuṇṭākum.  nāṉ kārkātta veḷḷāḷaṉ eṉṟum, nāṉ paṭaittalaik kavuṇṭaṉ eṉṟum, colvatāl maṭṭum uyarvuṇṭākātu.  turukkiyait tirutti muṉṉēṟṟiya kamāl pācā pōl, oruvar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkut tēvai.
tamiḻukkut tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ceyyavēṇṭiyavai
1.	ceṉṉai mānilattiṟkut tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu peyariṭṭu moḻi yiyaṟ kalainākarikat taṉṉāṭci (Linguistic and Cultural Autonomy) amaittal.
2.	taṉittamiḻp paṟṟum tamiḻilakkiyavaṟivum uḷḷa āciriya raiyē kalviyamaiccarāka amarttutal.
3.	taṉittamiḻppaṟṟum tamiḻaṟivum uḷḷavarāy ōyvupeṟṟuḷḷa, kalvittuṟaiyiyakkunar, peruṅkallūri mutalvar, talaimaip pērāciriyar ākiyōraiyē, tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ tuṇaikkaṇkāṇakarāka (Vice-Chancellors) amarttutal.
4.	iruvakai vaḻakkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷaiyum aṟintavarum, vaṇṇaṉai moḻiyiyalaiyaṉṟi varaṉmuṟai moḻi nūlaiyē kaṭaippiṭippavarum, vaṭamoḻiyintit tākkuta liṉiṉṟu tamiḻaikkāppavarum, uṇmaiyai yuraikkum tiṇmaiyuḷḷavarumāṉa tamiḻp pērāciriyaraiyē, ceṉṉai aṇṇāmalai maturaiyākiya muppalkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷilum tamiḻt tuṟait talaivarāka amarttutal.
5.	tamiḻukku māṟāka vēlai ceyyum tamiḻāciriyar, talaimai yāciriyar, mutalvar, tuṇaikkaṇkāṇakar ākiyōraip pataviyiṉiṉṟu nīkkutal.
6.	intiyāl tamiḻ keṭuvatu tiṇṇamātalāl, tamiḻ āṅkilam ākiya irumoḻit tiṭṭattaiyē, kalvi, āḷviṉai (Administration), vaḻakkut tīrppu ākiya muttuṟaiyilum ellā maṭṭattilum kaiyāḷutal.
7.	tamiḻppaṟṟaṟṟa pirāmaṇat tamiḻppaṇṭitar koṇṭāṉ mārait tuṇaikkoṇṭu tokutta ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻakarāti eṉṉum kalavaimoḻi akara mutaliyai uṭaṉē tiruttutal.
8.	tamiḻnāṭṭu uṇmaivaralāṟṟai eḻutuvittup pāṭamāk kalum, tamiḻppakaivarāl eḻutappaṭṭa poy varalāṟṟaip puṟakkaṇittalum.
.9. 	centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyai muḻu niṟaivāka uruvākkutal.
10.	cemmaiyāṉa āṅkilat tamiḻakaramutali yoṉṟu tokup pittalum, kalaiyaṟiviyal kammiyakkuṟiyīṭukaḷait tūya tamiḻil moḻipeyarttalum.
11.	pērāyakkaṭciyāḷar tokutta kalaikkaḷañciya naṭaiyaiyum, atiluḷḷa tamiḻaippaṟṟiya tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷaiyum, tiruttutal.
12.	acciṟku varāta tamiḻccoṟkaḷaiyum tamiḻ ēṭṭup pottakaṅ kaḷaiyum takka aṟiñaraikkoṇṭu tokup pittal.
13.	kalveṭṭut tokuppum veḷiyīṭum takka tamiḻaṟiñaraik koṇṭu ceyvittal.
14.	pāṭappottakaṅkaḷait tūya tamiḻil eḻutuvittalum, tūyatamiḻ nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭum kaḻakaṅkaḷai ūkkutalum.
15.	tūya tamiḻiṟ pēcum māṇavarkkum tūya tamiḻiṟ ciṟanta nūliyaṟṟum aṟiñarkkum paricaḷittal.
.16.	āṭpeyar, iṭappeyar, poruṭpeyar, paṭṭappeyar, ciṟappuppeyar ākiya ellāppeyaraiyum tūya tamiḻākkal.
17.	ilakkaṇam, icai, nāṭakam, kaṇiyam, maruttuvam mutaliya paḻantamiḻ aṟiviyalkaḷaiyum kalaikaḷaiyum muṉpōl tūymaippaṭuttutal.
18.	araciṉar aṟivippukkaḷum viḷamparaṅkaḷum eḻuttup piḻaiyum ilakkaṇap piḻaiyumiṉṟi veḷivarac ceytal.
19.	tamiḻaiyum patiṉeṇ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷaiyum oruṅkē kaṟpikkum oru kallūri niṟuvutal.
20.tamiḻppaṟṟuḷḷavaraiyē, caṭṭacapai pārāḷumaṉṟa vēṭ pāḷaraḷākat terinteṭukkavum tērnteṭukkavum ceytal.
21.	vaṇṇaṉai aṭippaṭaiyiṉṟi varalāṟṟaṭippaṭaiyilēyē moḻi nūlai vaḷarttal.
22.	kōyilvaḻipāṭum, iruvakaic caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻil naṭaipeṟacceytal.
23.	tamiḻukkāka uyiriḻantōr kuṭumpattiṟku vāḻkkai naṭaip poruḷ aḷittal.
24.	tamiḻppaṟṟāṟ pataviyiḻantōraip piṉṉōkkiya valimai yoṭu (Retrospective effect) mīṇṭum pataviyil iruttutal.
25.	varuvāyaṟṟa peruntamiḻp pulavarkku utavic campaḷam aḷittal.
26.	tamiḻuṇarcciyaip parappip potumakkaṭkut tamiḻaṟivu pukaṭṭum taṉittamiḻ itaḻkaṭkup poruḷutavi ceytal.
27.	mēṉāṭṭārait tuṇaikkoṇṭu, teṉmāvāriyil āḻa mūḻkip paḻamporuḷeṭut tārāya ēṟpāṭu ceytal.
28.	alukkiṟkum aḷapeṭaikkum pōtiya iṭaiveḷi viṭṭu, immiyum picakātu tiyākarācaiyar kīrttaṉaikaḷ pōṉṟē iṉpamāyp pāṭakkūṭiya uyariya meṭṭup pāṭal kaḷai aṟivum oḻukkamum taḻuviya potup poruḷkaḷ paṟṟi, iyaṟṟittarum icaivāṇarkkum pāvalarkkum ciṟanta paricaḷittal.
29.	ūrkāvalar paṭaittuṟaiyiṉar uṭaṟpayiṟciyum meyk kāṭṭum (Parade) tamiḻ ēvaṟ coṟkaḷaikkoṇṭu naṭappittal.
30.	kūṭṭaṅkaḷilum mānāṭukaḷilum iṟutiyil ulaka vāḻttup pāṭṭu tamiḻiṟ pāṭal.
31.	maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, (P.T.) cīnivācaiyaṅkār, (V.R) irāmac cantiratīṭcitar ākiya mūvarkkum ceṉṉaiyil paṭimai niṟuvutalum, ammūvar muḻuvuruvappaṭaṅkaḷaiyum mutaṉmai yāṉa araciyal aluvalakaṅkaḷilum potuk kūṭaṅkaḷilum māṭṭivaittalum.
32.	ceyyuṭkē ciṟappākavuriya ilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷai nīkkiviṭṭu, urainaṭai yilakkaṇattai maṭṭum virivākavum viḷakka mākavum eḻutip potuttamiḻ māṇavarkkup pāṭamāka vaittal.
33.	ikarattiṉ neṭilaik kuṟittaṟku, iṭaikkālattil āriyarāṟ pukuttappaṭṭa, vāyiṟkālpōṉṟa kiranta vari vaṭivai nīkki viṭṭu, iṟutiyil cuḻikoṇṭa paḻaiya ikara vaṭivaiyē kaiyāḷutal.
34.	iṅkilāntil 1875ām āṇṭiṟ ceytavāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭil ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkakkalviyaic caṭṭa vāyilāyp pukuttutal.
35.	aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻaka moḻiyiyal tuṟait toṭakka viḻāttalaivar par. caṭṭarciyiṉ acciṭṭa (tamiḻukku māṟāṉa) talaimaiyuraiyai, akkaḻakattiṉiṉṟu akaṟṟutal.
36.	par. caṭṭarciyum par. kattirēyum intiya ñāla nūliyal varalāṟu - mutaṉmaṭalattil (Gazetteer of India - Vol.I) tamiḻaip paṟṟi varaintuḷḷa tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷait tiruttutal.
37.	tiruvaiyāṟṟu aracar kallūriyil, camaṟkiruta mutalvariṉ kīḻirukkum tamiḻttuṟaiyaip pirittut tamiḻ mutalvariṉ kīḻ amaittal.
tamiḻukku veḷināṭṭiṟ ceyyavēṇṭiyavai
1.  uṇmaiyāṉa tamiḻ varalāṟṟai āṅkilattil veḷiyiṭṭu ulaka muḻutum parapputal.
2. veḷināṭukaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, nūlkaḷ, kalaikaḷ, paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ, vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai ārāyumāṟu, takka ārāycciyāḷaraik kāṉā, ekiptu, piraṉīcu malaināṭu mekcikkō (Mexico), kāmpōtiyā, caiyām, malēyā, cālittīvu mutaliya nāṭukaṭku aṉupputal.
3.	veḷināṭukaḷuṭaṉ pōṉṟē, intiya naṭuvaṇaracōṭum piṟa intiya mānilaṅkaḷōṭum, eḻuttuppōkkuvarattu āṅkilattilēyē naṭattutal.
4.  	ovvoru perunāṭṭilum oru tamiḻp pērāciriyar patavi ēṟpaṭacceytal.
5.  	tamiḻmoḻi nākarikap paṇpāṭṭai eṭutturaikkum oru viṭaimukar kuḻuvai (Delegation), ulakañ cuṟṟivaracceytal.
6.	tamiḻar peruntokaiyiṉarāyuḷḷa ilaṅkai pōṉṟa veḷi nāṭukaḷil, intiyat tūtāṇmaikkuḻuvil, tamiḻp paṟṟum tamiḻaṟivumuḷḷa tūyatamiḻar oruvarum irukkac ceytal..
7.	ākkacuppōrṭu (Oxford) āṅkila akaramutaliyilum pirit tāṉiyak kalaikkaḷañciyattilum, (Encyclopaedia Biritanica) tamiḻaip paṟṟiya tavaṟāṉa kuṟippukkaḷaiyum uṭaṉē tiruttacceytal.
tamiḻē ulakamutal tāymoḻi
cāṉṟukaḷ:-
1.	tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiyamai.
2.	paḻantamiḻ tiraviṭamoḻikaṭkut tāyāyiruttal.
3.	āriya moḻikaḷākak karutappaṭum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai tamiḻāyiruttal.
4.	mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ pala tamiḻāyiruttal
5.	irumutu kuravaraik kuṟikkum ammai appaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tirintum tiriyātum perumpāṟ perumoḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkutal.
6.	ellā moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum oṉṟiraṇṭēṉum tamiḻc colliruttal.
7.	āriyamoḻikaḷiluḷḷa cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷellām tamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyiruttal.
8.	tamiḻilakkaṇa amaitikaḷ pala moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟutal.
9.	pala moḻikaṭku allatu moḻikkuṭumpaṅkaṭkuc ciṟappākac collappeṟum ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷiṉ mūlanilai, tamiḻil iruttal.
tayir - tati (dadhi) - i.vē.
tai - tayir. o.nō: ai - ayir (nuṇmai), mai - mayir (kariyatu), vai - vayir (kūrmai).  pai - payir (paciyatu).
taittal = kuttutal, muṭkuttutal.
kāṉavēl muṭṭaikkuṅ kāṭu  (poyyāmoḻippulavar).
pāliṟku uṟaimōriṭutalai uṟaikuttutal, pirai kuttutal eṉpatu vaḻakku.
tati - tahī (i.)  (va.va: 172).
taraṅkam - taraṅka = alai.
tuḷaṅkutal = 1. acaital.
tuḷaṅkimil nallēṟṟiṉam  (culit.106).
2. nilaikalaṅkutal.
kaṭimaran tuḷaṅkiya kāvum  (puṟam.23.).
tuḷaṅku - taḷaṅku - tayaṅku. tayaṅkutal = acaital.
tayaṅkiya kaḷiṟṟiṉmēl  (kalit. 31:10).
taḷaṅku - taraṅku = acaintiyaṅkum alai.
taraṅkāṭun taṭanīr  (tēvā.461 : 1).
taraṅku - taraṅkam =1. alai.
nīrttaraṅka neṭuṅ kaṅkai (periyapu. taṭuttāṭ. 165).
2. icaiyalai.
oṇṭaraṅka vicaipāṭu maḷiyaracē  (tēvā. 87: 1).
o.nō: araṅku - araṅkam.  alaital = acaital.
alai = alaiyum nīrttirai.
ampōtaraṅkam = karainōkkivarum nīralaipōl varavarac ciṟuttuvarum kalippāvuṟuppu.
ampōtaraṅkam aṟupatiṟṟaṭittē  (to. 1408).
tolkāppiyam vaḻinūlātalāl, āriya varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa iṭaikkaḻakak kālattilēyē ampōtaraṅka vottāḻicaik kali vaḻakkiliruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭavar taram + ka (paya) eṉṟu pakuttu, kuṟukkē celvatu eṉṟu mūlap poruḷuraippar.  taram eṉpataṉ mūlam trū. itu turuvu eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟirivu.  ka eṉpatu ē eṉpataṉ kāṉmuḷai. 
ē - yā - jā - kā - ka.  (va.va. 172, 173.)
tarukkam - tarkka.
tarukkutal = 1. cerukkutal.
taṉṉai viyantu tarukkalum  (tirikaṭu. 38).
2. mikuttuk kūṟutal.
taṉṉoṭu mavaḷoṭun tarukkiya puṇarttu  (tol.996).
3.  pōrūkkaṅ koḷḷutal.
vempōrt tarukkiṉār maintar  (cīvaka. 1679).
4. tākkutal, iṭittal.
taṉmaruppāl veṇpiṇṭi cērat tarukki (patiṉo.tiruvīṅkōy.40).
5. takarttal.
marakkala miyaṅkavēṇṭi ... tarukkiyaviṭattu  (kamparā.mīṭcip 171). (va.va:)
tarukku = 1. cerukku. 2. etirkkum valimai.
etirceyun tarukkilāmaiyiṉ  (kamparā. tāṭakai. 40).
3. coṟpōr.  tarukkiṉāṟ camaṇceytu  (tiv. periyati.2.1:7).
tarukku - tarukkam =1. coṟpōr.
poruvaru tarukkañ ceyya  (kantapu. tatīciyut. 157).
2.  tarukka nūl.
tamiḻt tarukka nūl eḻu poruṭ pākupāṭṭai aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭatu.  tarkka paripāṣai eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷa akattiyat tarukka nūṟpākkaḷaik kāṇka.  tamiḻt tarukka nūlaiyē vaṭavar vaicēṭikam eṉa vakuttaṉar.  (va.va: 174 -175).
talaikkaṭṭu
kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum maṭṭum uḷḷa kuṭumpam; kuṭumpam kaṇavaṉum maṉaivi makkaḷum cērnta kūṭṭam; kuṭumpu pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērnta kūṭṭam; il allatu kuṭi pala talai muṟaiyākat toṭarntu varum peruṅ kuṭumpu: marapu toṭarntu varum kuṭi vaḻi; kulam pala kuṭikaḷ cērnta tokuti (e.ṭu) paḷḷiyar, kavaṇṭar, akampaṭiyar, kuṭiyāṉavar mutaliya pala kuṭikaḷ cērnta tokuti vēḷāṇkulam; iṉam orēvakaiyāṉa moḻipēcum pala kulattokuti (Nalion) varaṇam niṟaṅ kāraṇamāka ēṟpaṭṭa māperum kulam (Race).  (col: 47)
talaikkaḻakam
muttamiḻum oruṅkē kaṟṟa pulavar nūṟṟukkaṇakkiṉar tōṉṟiya taṉāl, paḻaiya ilakkiyattai ārāyavum putiya ilakkiyattai iyaṟṟavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅkarai mēlirunta maturai yeṉṉum taṉ talainakaril oru kaḻakam niṟuviṉāṉ. ataṉ uṟuppiṉar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉ patiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṉpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉṟum, avarāṟ pāṭap paṭṭaṉa mutunāraiyum mutukurukum kaḷariyāviraiyum pala paripāṭalum piṟavumeṉṟum, akkaḻakattai naṭatti vanta pāṇṭiyar kāyciṉavaḻuti mutal kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāka eṇpat toṉ patiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruṭ pāvaraṅkēṟiṉār eḻuvareṉṟum, akkaḻakam irunta kālanīṭci nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṉpatāṇ ṭeṉṟum, iṟaiya ṉārakapporuḷ uraiyiṟ collap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
akkaḻaka vuṟuppiṉar peyarkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. akattiyar iṭaikkaḻakak kulaiviṟkuppiṉ vaṭanāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta āriyar, mutaliru kaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarum tūya tamiḻarāvar.  civaṉ peyarum murukaṉ peyaruṅ koṇṭa iruvar talaikkaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarā yiruntirukkalām.  murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar ki.mu. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭup pārata kālattavar.  nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉ eṉpatu, vaṭaticait talaivaṉaik kuṟikkum tolkataik kaṭṭuppeyar.
talaik kaḻakam ki.mu. 10,000 pōl tōṉṟiṟṟu. ak kālattu makkaḷ ikkālattāriṉum mika nīṇṭu vāḻntirupparātalāl, carācari āḷukku 50 āṇṭu vaittukkoḷḷiṉ, 89 pāṇṭi yarukkum 4450 āṇṭākum. mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kālanīṭci 4440 āṇṭu. itu muṟṟum poruttamāṉatum nikaḻntirukkak kūṭiyatumākum.
talaikkaḻakattiṟku aḷavai nūlāyirunta māpiṇṭa nūṟ peyarum maṟaintu viṭṭatu. akattiyam oru māpiṇṭamāyiṉum, iṭaikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatātaliṉ, talaikkaḻaka nūlā yiruntirukka muṭiyātu.  eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum mukkūṟ ṟilakkaṇamuṅ koṇṭa iyaṟṟamiḻ nūl piṇṭam; iyalicai nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻilakkaṇamuṅ koṇṭa nūl māpiṇṭam. ovvoru tamiḻum ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum irupāṟpaṭṭatu. muttamiḻkkum ilakkaṇam orē nūlāyirukkum; āyiṉ, ilakkiyam vevvēṟu vaṉappuk kaḷākavum paṉuvalkaḷākavum irukkum. iyaṟṟamiḻikkaṇap poruṭkūṟṟil yāppum uvamai yeṉṉum aṇiyum aṭaṅkum.
talaikkaḻakam varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmaiyatātalāl, ataip paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉaiyiṟ cila viḷattaṅkaḷ ki.pi. 6-ām allatu 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil varaiyappaṭṭavuraiyil, tavaṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatu iyaṟkaiyē.
talaikkaḻakam toṭaṅkiṉapiṉ, ulakiyaṟ kalaikaḷum aṟiviyal kaḷum vaḷarntu vantatupōṉṟē, mataviyal ārāycciyum āḻntu vaḷarntatu.  civa neṟikkum tirumāl neṟikkum mēṟpaṭṭa, kaṭavuḷ neṟiyeṉṉum potu neṟiyum kaṇ ṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. potu makkaḷ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattaiyum, pulamakkaḷ peruntēva matattaiyum, tuṟaviyar kaṭavuḷ neṟiyaiyum, kaṭaippiṭittaṉar.
āyiṉ, kāḷi pōrveṟṟit teyvamākavum ammai nōyt teyvamākavuṅ karutappaṭṭataṉāl, nāḷaṭaivil aintiṇaip potut teyvamāṉāḷ.  tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭumākiya teṉṉilattiṉ teṉkōṭiyilirunta perumalait toṭarukkum, vaṭakōṭiyilirunta pērāṟṟiṟkum, kumari yeṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarē iṭappeṟṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ matikkulattāṉātalāl, avaṉ mutal talainakar avaṉ kula mutalvaṉ peyarai yoṭṭi maturai yeṉappaṭṭatu.  mati - matirai - maturai. o.nō: kuti - kutirai.  kutittal tāṇṭutal.
vaikai maturai piṟkālatta tātalāl, maruta muṉṟuṟai eṉṉum toṭariṉiṉṟu maturaip peyar vanta teṉpatu poruntātu.  paḵṟuḷi maturaip peyarē vaikai maturaikkum piṟkālattil iṭappaṭṭatu.
talaikkaḻakak kāla nākarikamum paṇpāṭum
āriyar teṉṉāṭu varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻakat taṉit tamiḻ nūlkaḷaṉaittum aḻiyuṇṭu pōyiṉum, moḻiyamaip piṉiṉṟum mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṉiṉṟum kaṭaikkaḻakap pāṭal kaḷilum paṉuvalkaḷilumuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷiṉiṉṟum, talaikkaḻakak kāla nākarikap paṇpāṭṭai oru vāṟuṇaralām.
moḻi
aḵṟiṇaiyiṉiṉṟu makkaḷaip pirittuk kāṭṭuvatum, oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu aḷavukōlum, moḻiyē.  moḻiyiṉālēyē māntaṉ naraṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.  naralutal = 1. olittal.  āṭukaḻai naralum cēṭcimai  (puṟam. 120). 2. kattutal. veṇkuruku naralavīcum nuṇpaḵṟu valaiya (akam. 14) 3. pēcutal.
naral - naraṉ = māntaṉ. vaṟitē nilaiyāta vimmaṇṇulakiṉ naraṉāka vakuttaṉai  (tēvā. 934:2).
naraṉ - naram = māntappiṟavi.  narattilum piṟattirāta (tiv. tiruccan. 29).
vāṉaram (vālnaram) = vālaiyuṭaiya māntaṉ pōṉṟa vilaṅku. vāṉara mukaṉ  (cīvaka. 1168).
naraṉ - va. nara. vāṉaram  (va. vānara.)
nara eṉṉuñ colliṟku vaṭamoḻiyil vērillai.  vāṉara eṉṉuñcollai nara ēva eṉṟu pirittu, naraṉaip pōṉṟatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
moḻikku aṭuttapaṭiyāka, māntaṉiṉ ciṟappāṟṟal muṉṉutal.  muṉṉu tal karututal.  muṉṉuvāṉ (vāṉīṟṟu ni. kā. vi. e.) - muṉṉāṉ (ma.) AS munan (to think).
muṉ - muṉṉam =1. karuttu.  muṉṉa mukatti ṉuṇarntu (puṟam. 3). 2. maṉam. (tivā.).
muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam = uḷḷam.
maṉam - va. maṉa. ṭu. mens. ME mynd, E mind.
muṉ - maṉ = karutum āṟṟaluḷḷa māntaṉ.
maṉpatu = makkaṭ kūṭṭam, maṉpatu maṟukkat tuṉpaṅ kaḷaivōṉ (paripā. 15:52).
maṉpatu - maṉpatai = makkaṭkūṭṭam.
maṉpatai kākkuniṉ puraimai  (puṟam. 210).
maṉ - va. manu. OS, OHG man, E man.
moḻiyiluṅ karuttilum aṉṟum iṉṟum eṉṟum ciṟantavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum, moḻiyai vaḷarppavar potu makkaḷ; ilakkiyattai vaḷarppavar pulamakkaḷ. moḻikaḷ iyaṉ moḻiyum tirimoḻiyum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. tāṉē tōṉṟiyatu iyaṉmoḻi; oṉṟiṉiṉṟu tirintatu tirimoḻi.  āku peyarkaḷ irumaṭiyum mummaṭiyun tiriyum.  tiraviṭam tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tirimoḻi; āriyam tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu tirinta irumaṭit tirimoḻi.
kumarināṭu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam maṭṭumaṉṟi māntaṉ piṟantakamu mātalāl, tamiḻē immiyum ēṉaimoḻi kalavāta muḻut tūya iyaṉmoḻiyākum.
irutiṇai aimpāl īreṇ mūviṭaṅkaḷākiya kiḷaviyilak kaṇamum, nuṇpirivukaḷaiyuṭaiya eṇ vēṟṟumaip peyarilak kaṇamum, nannāṉku vakaippaṭṭa mukkāla viṉaiyilakkaṇamum; vaḷamai, cemmai, marapu, takuti, tūymai mutaliya coṟṟiṟaṅ kaḷum; curukkam, teḷivu, matippuṟavu, iṭakkaraṭakkal, maṅkalam mutaliya coṟṟoṭaramaitikaḷum koṇṭu; patiṉeṇ tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkut tāyum patiṉāṟāriyap pirivukaṭku mūlamumā yamaintatu tamiḻ.
irutiṇai
ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum (uyiriyum) eṉa mūvakaiyāka vakuttālum, taṉakkum piṟarkkum nallatun tīyatumākiya iraṇṭaiyum pakuttaṟiyum paṇpaiyē ciṟappākak koṇṭu, aḵtuḷḷatai uyarnta vakuppeṉṟum aḵtillatait tāḻnta vakuppeṉṟum tuṇintu, ataṟkēṟpa, uyarnta vakuppaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭkē āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālīṟum, tāḻnta vakuppaik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭ kellām orumai paṉmai yeṉṉum īreṇṇīṟumē koṭuttu, moḻiyai vaḷarttavar tamiḻap potumakkaḷē. makkaḷum tēvarum uyarnta vakuppu; maṟṟavai tāḻnta vakuppu.
orumai :	murukaṉ vantāṉ, vaḷḷi ceṉṟāḷ.  uyartiṇai.
			kāḷai uḻukiṟatu, āvu kaṟakkiṉṟatu, kal kuttukiṟatu, 
				atu paṟakkiṉṟatu. - aḵṟiṇai.
			paṉmaiyīṟum iruvakuppiṟkum vevvēṟām.
paṉmai : 	aracar vāḻntaṉar (vāḻntār),
			āciriyaṉmār pēciṉār. - uyartiṇai
			kāḷaikaḷ uḻukiṉṟaṉa, māṭukaḷ mēykiṉṟaṉa, 
				avai iṉikkiṉṟaṉa, ilaikaḷ acaikiṉṟaṉa.  - 
				aḵṟiṇai.
makkaḷ, kurukkaḷ, avarkaḷ vantārkaḷ, eṉpaṉa vaḻuvatai tiyākak koḷḷappeṟum.
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampal eṉṉum muṟaiyil, potumakkaḷ vakutta vakuppukaṭkē ilakkaṇa nūlār uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai yeṉap peyar koṭuttaṉar.  iṉṟum, kallā māntar ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷai aṟiyā viṭiṉum, irutiṇai muṟaippaṭiyē pēcuvar. uyiraiyum pālaiyumēyaṉṟip pakut taṟivai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu vēṟemmoḻiyārum poruḷkaḷaip pakuttilar. teluṅkil makat, amakat eṉṟatu tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟip piṟkālattilēyē.
vaḷamai
nālvakaiyaṭai: vāḻaiyilai, neltāḷ, karumputtōkai, paṉaiyōlai.
palvakaip piñcu : mā vaṭu, palā mūcu, vāḻaik kaccal, teṉṉaṅ kurumpai, pākku nuḻāy.
kāyppu māṟiyapiṉ tōṉṟum piñcu nuru (noru).
yāṉaippeyarkaḷ: āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kari, kaṟaiyaṭi, kaimmalai, kaimmā, tūṅkal, tumpi, tōl, nālvāy, pakaṭu, puḻaikkai - pūṭkai, perumā, poṅkaṭi, matamā, maṟamali, mātiram, moy, vaḻuvai, vāraṇam, vēḻam mutaliyaṉa.
mukattiṟ cempuḷḷi yuḷḷatu cinturam allatu pukarmukam. murukaṉ ūrti piṇimukam.  āṇyāṉai kaḷiṟu; peṇyāṉai piṭi. ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta kutiraiyiṉattaiyum, pattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vakaiyākap pirittaṉar.
tamiḻ moḻi, taṉakkuriya vīṭu eṉṉuñ collai maṭṭumaṉṟi, il eṉṉun teluṅkuc collaiyum, maṉaiyeṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collaiyum, camaṟkirutattiṟkum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa kuṭi eṉṉuñ collaiyum, taṉṉakattuk koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟu, kālṭuvelār tamiḻc col vaḷattaip pārāṭṭik kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
cemmai
eḻuttukkaḷaiyum coṟkaḷaiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum, oliyum poruḷum tirikkātum citaikkātum ilakkaṇa neṟiyiṟ pēcuvatu cemmai.  cemmai tavaṟiyatu koṭumai.  cemmaiyāṉa tamiḻ centamiḻ. koṭumaiyāṉa tamiḻ koṭun tamiḻ. tamiḻai eṉṟuñ centamiḻākavē vaḻaṅkavēṇṭumeṉpatu, toṉṉūlāciriyar iṭṭa nilaiyāṉa varampu.  avvarampi ṉālēyē, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka āriyar ettuṇaiyō kēṭu ceytiruppiṉum, tamiḻ iṉṉum aḻiyātu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
ikkālattup pērāciriyar cilar ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum koccai vaḻakkiṟkum vēṟupāṭaṟiyātu, vaccirukkōm (vaittiruk kiṟōm) eṉpatai ulakavaḻakkākak koḷvar.
nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭaintum
kalanta mayakkam ulaka mātaliṉ
irutiṇai aimpāl iyalneṟi vaḻā amait
tirivil colloṭu taḻāal vēṇṭum.  (tol.1589).
vaḻakkeṉap paṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟē 
nikaḻcci avarkaṭ ṭākalāṉa.  ( 1592).
eṉpavaṟṟaik kaṇṭum avar uṇarvatillai.  potumakkaḷuḷ oru cārārāṉa kīḻmakkaḷ pēccaiyun taḻuvavēṇṭumeṉiṉ, avar oḻukkattaiyum piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākum. itu uyarntōk kuṭampāṭaṉṟu. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟkum ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭiruppiṉum, iraṇṭum centamiḻ vaḻakkēyeṉṟum, cemmai tamiḻiṉ uyirnāṭip paṇpeṉṟum, tamiḻukku vaḻu nilaiyāṉavai tiraviṭa moḻikaṭku vaḻānilaiyāku meṉṟum, ataṉālēyē avai taṉimoḻikaḷāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
marapu
epporuḷ eccoliṉ evvā ṟuyarntōr
ceppiṉar appaṭic cepputal marapē.  (naṉ. 388).
iḷamai, āṇpāl, peṇpāl, vilaṅkuk kāvalar, uṟuppukkaḷ, viṉaikaḷ, kaḻiporuḷ mutaliya palvakaip poruḷumpaṟṟi, paṇṭai mēṉmakkaḷ eccoṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉarō accoṟ kaḷaiyē vaḻaṅkutal marapām.
ūṇviṉaikaḷ
uṟital,
uṟiñcutal	-	nīrpporuḷaiyum nekiḻccip poruḷaiyum 
					kāṟṟāl vāykkuḷ iḻuttal.
kuṭittal 	- 	nīrpporuḷai iyalpāka uṭkoḷḷutal.
parukutal 	- 	nīrkkalattiṟ paṟpaṭak kuṭittal.
aruntutal 	-	ciṟitu ciṟitākak kuṭittal.
maṇṭutal 	-	maṇṭiyuṭpaṭak kuṭittal
māntutal 	- 	pēraḷavākak kuṭittal
capputal 	-	oṉṟai mellātu nāviṟkum aṇṇattiṟkum iṭaiyiliṭṭu nerukki ,ataṉcāṟṟai mellamella uṟiñcutal; allatu apporuḷaic ciṟitu ciṟitākak  karaittal.
cuvaittal 	- 	oṉṟai meṉṟu ataṉ cuvaiyai nukartal.
cavaittal 	- 	melliya poruḷai mellutal, kuḻantai tāyp 
		pālaic capputal
cūmputal,
cūpputal  	-	tittippuk kuccum mūḷaiyelumpum viralum pōṉṟa 
		vaṟṟai vāyiliṭṭuc capputal
tiṉṉutal 	- 	paḻamum palakāramum pōṉṟa ciṟṟuṇṭiyai meṉṟu 
		uṭkoḷḷutal.
uṇṇutal 	- 	kavaḷaṅ kavaḷamākac cōṟṟai uṭkoḷḷal.
cāppiṭutal 	- 	kuḻampum cāṟum mōrum (allatu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu) 
		kalanta cōṟṟaik kavaḷaṅ kavaḷamākak kaṟivakaikaḷuṭaṉ (allatu avaiyiṉṟi) uṭkoḷḷutal.
maṭuttal,
vāymaṭuttal -	kampaṅkañciyum kēḻvarakuk kūḻum pōṉṟavaṟṟaik 
		kaṭṭikaṭṭiyākak kūṭṭil toṭṭuṇṇutal, kavaḷaṅkavaḷamākap piṟar ūṭṭutal.
acaittal,
acaivu 
ceytal - vilaṅku acaiyiṭuvatupōl alakaiyacaittu meṉṟu 
		uṭkoḷḷutal.
ayilutal -  kuḻantai aḷaintu cōṟuṇṇutal.
ayil - ayiṉ - ayiṉi = uṇavu.
tāli kaḷaintaṉṟu milaṉē pālviṭ
ṭayiṉiyu miṉṟayiṉ ṟaṉavē ... (puṟam. 77)
o.nō: paḷ - (pay) - payil. payilutal paḻakutal.
kapputal - mokki viraintu uṭkoḷḷutal.
mokkutal = vāyniṟaiyak koṇṭumellutal 
kaittala niṟaikaṉi yappamo ṭavalpori

micaital - viruntiṉarai yuṇpittu miñciyatai
yuṇṇutal.
vittu miṭal vēṇṭuṅ kollō viruntōmpi
miccil micaivāṉ pulam.  (kuṟaḷ 85).
mocittal - palar kūṭi yuṇṇutal.
viḻaviṉ ṟāyiṉum paṭupatam piḻaiyātu
maiyūṉ mocitta vokkaloṭu (puṟam. 96)
moykoṇ mākkaḷ mocikkavūṇ
curantaṉaḷ  (maṇi. 19:136).
moyttal tiraḷutal. moy - moyi - moci.
mocital moyttal.
kaṭuntē ṟuṟukiḷai mocintaṉa tuñcum  (patiṟṟup.71:6).
ārtal - vayiṟu nirampa vuṇṇutal.
viḻuṅkutal - mellātum cuvai pārātum orē taṭavaiyil viraintu vāyvaḻi vayiṟṟukkuḷ iṭutal.
uṭkoḷḷutal - evvakaiyilēṉum vayiṟu cērppittal.  itu ellā ūṇviṉaikaṭkum potuvām.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiṟ pulavar palar marapum (Idiom) takutiyum (Pro-priety) pōṟṟāmaiyāl, palacoṟkaḷ tam ciṟappup poruḷai iḻantuviṭṭaṉa.
iṉṟu, kuḷampi (kāppi) cāppiṭutal, curuṭṭuk kuṭittal, kocuvalai, tēṭkaṭi, eṉpaṉa marapuvaḻuvām.  ivai, kuḷampi kuṭittal, curuṭṭup piṭittal allatu pukaittal, ulaṅku valai allatu nuḷampuvalai, tēṭkoṭṭu eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. campaḷattai (salary) ūtiyam (profit, gain) eṉpatum vaḻuvām.  ilakkaṇappulakai nirampātavar nūlāciriyarum pottaka ākiriyarum āvataṉālum marapu keṭukiṉṟatu.
tūymai
cemmai pōṉṟē tūymaiyum tamiḻiṉ uyirnāṭip paṇpām.
veḷināṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷkaṭ kellām, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu nōkki uṭaṉuṭaṉ tamiḻppeyarkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: aṭaikkāy (pākku), aṇṭimā (muntiri, Cashew) uruḷaik kiḻaṅku, oṭṭakam, karumpu, kaḻutai, kuccuk kiḻaṅku (āḻvaḷḷik kiḻaṅka, ēḻilaikkiḻaṅka, kompukkiḻaṅku, cavarikkaṭṭai, maravaḷḷikkiḻaṅku), kutirai, centāḻai, (Pine-apple), pukaiyilai, pukaivaṇṭi, pērīntu, mitivaṇṭi, miḷakāy, muntiri (koṭi muntiri, grape), vāṉkōḻi.
kāḷ kāḷ eṉṟu kattuvatu kaḻutai.
tamiḻiṉ iyalpai aṟiyātār cilar,
kuraṅkiṉ ēṟṟiṉaik kaṭuvaṉ eṉṟalum
marampayil kūkaiyaik kōṭṭāṉ eṉṟalum
cevvāyk kiḷiyait tattai eṉṟalum
vevy verukiṉaip pūcai eṉṟalum
kutiraiyuḷ āṇiṉaic cēval eṉṟalum
iruḷniṟap paṉṟiyai ēṉam eṉṟalum
erumaiyuḷ āṇiṉaik kaṇṭi eṉṟalum
muṭiya vanta avvaḻak kuṇmaiyiṉ
kaṭiya lākā kaṭaṉaṟinta tōrkkē.  (tol. 1568).
eṉpataip piṟaḻavuṇarntu, tamiḻil varaituṟaiyiṉṟip piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkat tolkāppiyam iṭantatantu viṭṭatākak kūṟuvar.  innūṟḷapāviluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷē yeṉpataiyum, avaṟṟuṭ cila ciṟitē poruḷ tirintavai yeṉpataiyum, avar aṟintilar.
kaṭuvaṉ eṉpatu kaṇavaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripu. kaṇavaṉ eṉṉuñcol aḵṟiṇaiyilum vaḻaṅkum.
vaṅkāk kaṭanta ceṅkāṟ pēṭai
eḻāluṟa vīḻnteṉak kaṇavaṟkā ṇātu  (kuṟun. 151).
eṉpatai nōkkuka.  kōṭṭāṉ marakkompil allatu pontil vāḻvatu.  tattai ilaikaḷilum ōlaikaḷilum totti niṟpatu.  tottu - tottai - tattai. tottai - tōtta (inti).  pūcai eṉpatu vīṭṭup pūṉaiyiṉ peyar.  cēval eṉpatu paṟavai yāṇiṉ peyar. cē eṉpatu vilaṅkāṇiṉ peyar. e-ṭu: cēṅkaṉṟu = āṇkaṉṟu. cē - cēvu - cēval. ēṉam eṉpatu kariyatu eṉṉum poruḷuḷḷatu.  ēṉal =karuntiṉai. ēṉai - yāṉai. kaṇṭi kaṭiyatu (kaṭumaiyāṉatu).
āyiṉum, tolkāppiyarkku irupatu nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya naṉṉūlār, tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaik kāvātu, ellā vaṭacoṟ kaḷaiyum tamiḻil vaḻaṅka iṭantantatu pōṉṟē,
paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum
vaḻuvala kāla vakaiyi ṉāṉē. (462).
eṉṟu vaḻuppaṭak kūṟiviṭṭār.  itu taḻuvat takkataṉṟu.
curukkam
curuṅkac collal oru paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟākap paṇṭait tamiḻarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
payaṉilcol pārāṭṭu vāṉai makaṉeṉal
makkaṭ pataṭi yeṉal.  (kuṟal. 196). 
palacollak kāmuṟuvar maṉṟa mācaṟṟa 
cilacollal tēṟṟā tavar  ( 649)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
oruvaṉ kūlakkaṭaikkucceṉṟu, kaṭaikkāraṉiṭam pācippayaṟu (paccaippayaṟu) uḷḷatā eṉṟu viṉaviṉ, kaṭaikkāraṉ aḵtillāviṭattu, uḻuntallatillaiyeṉṟu ta;ṉaṉiṭamuḷḷa vēṟoru payaṟṟai allatu carakkaippaṟṟic colla vēṇṭumeṉṟum, viṉaviya poruḷ maṭṭumē yirukkumāyiṉ, atai ippayaṟalla tillaiyeṉṟu cuṭṭikkūṟa vēṇṭu meṉṟum, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ mēṟkoṇṭu pala viṉākkaḷum viṭaikaḷum vīṇāka eḻāvāṟu taṭukkumeṉṟum, paṇṭai yilakkaṇa nūlār vaṇikarkkuc coṉṉatu, maṟṟellāt toḻilāḷarkkum poruntum potu vāypāṭākum.
epporu ḷāyiṉum alla tilleṉiṉ
apporu ḷallāp piṟituporuḷ kūṟal.
apporuḷ kūṟiṉ cuṭṭik kūṟal.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam  (col. 35.36).
teḷivu
curuṅkac collal ciṟanta paṇpāyiṉum, viḷakkamiṉṟic colvatu payaṉpaṭātu kuṟṟamākumātaliṉ, māppūttatu eṉṟu palaporuḷorucolliṉ ciṟappu viṉai kuṟiyātu, mā vīḻntatu eṉṟu potuviṉai kuṟippatu kūṭāteṉṟum, atai māmaram vīḻntatu, vilaṅkumā vīḻntatu eṉṟu teḷivu paṭuttik kūṟavēṇṭumeṉṟum; oru poruḷiṉ iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa iyalaiyum ceyalaiyuṅ kūṟumpōtu, ataṟkuk karaṇiyattaiyum kuṟippākavō veḷippaṭaiyākavō kuṟittal vēṇṭumeṉṟum; toṉṉūlāciriyar neṟiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
oṉṟuviṉai maruṅkiṉ oṉṟit tōṉṟum
viṉaivēṟu paṭāap palaporu ḷorucol
niṉaiyuṅ kālaik kiḷantāṅ kiyalum.
kuṟittōṉ kūṟṟan terittumoḻi kiḷavi.
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.  (col.54, 55).
ilakkiyam
icai, nāṭakam, kaṇitam, kaṇiyam, ēraṇam, mantiram, maṟai, paṭṭāṅku, maṭai, maruttuvam, aṟam, araciyal, mal, pōr, nilam, nīr mutaliya palvēṟu nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya ciṟappilakkiyamum; aṟuvakaip pāvāṉa taṉinilaic ceyyuḷum, eṇvakai vaṉappāṉa toṭarnilaic ceyyuḷum, ākiya potuvilakki yamum; mūlamum uraiyum ceyyuḷākavē yiruntaṉa.  iṅṅaṉam vēṟemmoḻiyilum iruntatillai, irukkappōvatu millai. 
ilakkiyam muḻutum ceyyuḷākavē iruntataṉāl, ilakkiya vaḻakku ceyyuḷvaḻakkeṉappaṭṭatu. ellāppulavarum pāvalarāyiruntataṉāl, pāvalar eṉappaṭātu pulavar eṉṟē peyar peṟṟaṉar. avar ikkālattup pāvalarpōl, ēṭum eḻutukōlum eṭuttu ōriṭattamarntu eṇṇiyeṇṇi aṭittuntiruttiyum ceyyuḷiyaṟṟātu, urainaṭaiyiṟ pēcuvatu pōl, eṅkum eṉṟum epporuḷum paṟṟik kaṭuttup pāṭiyavarāvar. āciriyarum aṟivuṟuttuvōrum kaṇiyarum ākiya kalvit toḻilāḷar maṭṭumaṉṟi; uḻavar, vaṇikar, maruttuvar, kollar mutaliya palvakaip piṟatoḻilāḷarum, kuṟiñci nilattuk kuṟavarum, pālainilattuk kaḷḷar maṟavarum, mullainilattu āyarum, neytal nilattup paravarum ākiya varuḷḷum cilar pāvalarāyiruntaṉar.  ataṉālēyē, talaik kaḻakattup pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar uḷḷiṭṭu, nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉar.
ilakkaṇam
tamiḻil ilakkaṇa mutaṉūl iyaṟṟiyavar. muṟṟattuṟantu muḻumuṉi varāṉa oru meypporuḷaṟiñar.
viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉūl ākum.  (tol.1594)
muṉaital veṟuttal.  muṉai - muṉaivu - muṉaivaṉ. muṉitar veṟuttal. muṉi - muṉivu - muṉivaṉ = ulakai veṟuttup paṟṟait tuṟantavaṉ.
eḻuttu
mutaṉūlāciriyar oru ciṟanta meyp poruḷaṟiñarāyirunta taṉālēyē, uyirum meyyum uyirmeyyum (uyiriyum) pōṉṟu eḻuttolikaḷ mūvakaippaṭṭiruttalaikkaṇṭu, mupporuṭ peyarkaḷaiyē eḻuttolikaṭkum uvamaiyāku peyarāka iṭṭirukkiṉṟār.  tāṉāka iyaṅkum uyiraip pōṉṟu tāṉāka volikkum uyireḻuttum; uyiriṉ cērkkai yiṉṟittāṉāka volikkāta meyyeḻuttum; uyiroṭu cērnta vuṭampu ataṉāl iyakkappaṭṭu ataṉoṭu oṉṟi ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟum uyirmey pōṉṟu, uyireḻuttoṭu cērnta meyyeḻuttu olikkappaṭṭu ataṉoṭu oṉṟi ataṉiṉum muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟum uyirmey yeḻuttum; iruttalaik kāṇka.  uyirmey uyiraiyuṭaiya mey.  pirāṇi eṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻakkiṉāl uyirmey eṉṉuñ colliṉ poruḷ maṟaintatu.
oliyiṉ nilaimaiyaiyē uruvattiluṅ kāṭṭuvataṟku, uyir meykku meyyuruvamum uyirkkuṟiyuñ cērnta taṉivaṭivamait tār.  ivvamaippu mutaṉmutal tamiḻilēyē ēṟpaṭṭatu.  uyireḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttukkaḷum cērnta tokutikkuk kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, avaṟṟōṭu uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷum cērnta tokutikku neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum peyar.  ivai uṟavukkuṟiyīṭukaḷātalāl (Relative Terms), oruṅkē tōṉṟiṉa vaiyākum.
mēlaināṭukaḷ uṭpaṭap piṟanāṭṭu vaṇṇamālaikaḷel lām (Alphabets)FW§fz¡nf.  cappāṉilum ettiyōppiyā vilum uḷḷa acaiyeḻuttukkaḷum (Syllabaries), meyyiṉmai yāl neṭuṅkaṇakkākā.
mēṉāṭṭuk kuṟuṅkaṇakkellām oḻuṅkiṉṟi uyirum meyyuṅ kalantiruppatāl, tamiḻk kuṟuṅkaṇakkuppōl piṟappai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa muṟaiyaik (Order) kāṭṭuvatillai. colliṉ mūviṭattilum enta eḻuttum varalāmātalāl, mutaṉilai iṭainilai iṟutinilai eṉṉum iṭavarampum mēlai moḻiyeḻuttukkaṭkillai. coṟkaḷ nīṇṭakālamāka mēṉmēlun tirintu tirintu urumāṟi, turup piṭitta iruppukkaruvikaḷpōl uṟuppup pirikkamuṭiyātirup patāl, pakucollamaippum coṟpuṇarcciyum ākiya eḻuttiṉ puṟattilakkaṇaṅkaḷum perumpālum mēlaimoḻikaṭ killai.  ākavē, pattu vakaiyakamum iruvakaip puṟamum ākiya paṉṉīreḻut tilakkaṇaṅkaḷum nirampiya moḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē.
eḻuttilak kaṇamē yīrā ṟavaitām
eṇpeyar muṟaipiṟap puruvam aḷavē
mutalī ṟiṭainilai pōli yakamām
kiḷaviyum puṇarcciyuṅ kiḷappiṉ puṟamām.
māttirai eṉpatum teṉcollē. kumarināṭṭār potuvāka eḵkuc ceviyarāyiruntamaiyiṉ, kaṇṇimai yaḷavāṉa oru māttiraiyiṉ araiyaḷavōṭu kālaḷavaiyum eḻuttoli kaḷiṟ kaṇṭaṟintaṉar.
tamiḻil eḻuttut tōṉṟiya kālam ki.mu. 10,000. vēta āriyar nāvalan tēyattiṟkuṭ pukunta kālam ki.mu. 1500. avarukku eḻuttillai. avar vētam nīṇṭa kālamāka eḻutāk kiḷaviyākavē yiruntatu.  teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ, tamiḻeḻuttaip piṉpaṟṟik kiranta eḻuttai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. ataṉpiṉ, kiranta eḻuttai aṭip paṭaiyākak koṇṭu, vaṭanāṭṭil, ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkum 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭaiyil, tēvanākari tōṉṟiṟṟu. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkaiyum eḻuttilak kaṇattaiyum taḻuvi, eḻuttumuṟaiyum uyirmeyt taṉivaṭivum coṟ puṇarcciyum, mutaṟkaṇ camaṟkirutattilum piṉṉar ēṉai yintiya moḻikaḷilum, ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
ki.mu.3-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttu tamiḻakattup pukuntatu.  ataṉiṉṟē vaṭṭeḻuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. āriyar tam tamiḻoḻipput tiṭṭattai aṭip paṭaiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkiyatāl, mūvēntarum āriya aṭimai yarāyp pōṉa piṟkālattil, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kalveṭṭil vaṭṭeḻuttup pukuntirukkiṉṟatu. tamiḻukkum vaṭṭeḻut tiṟkum yātoru toṭarpumillai.  iṉṟuḷḷa tamiḻeḻuttut toṉṟu toṭṭu varuvatē.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.  (98)
eṉṟu 13-ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟatal kāṇka.  ekara okaramey puḷḷi nīṅkiyatum, ēkāra ōkāramey iraṭṭaiccuḻik kompu peṟṟatum, ēkāravuyir kīḻiḻuppuk koṇṭatumē, piṟkālattu vēṟupāṭām.
col
coṟkaḷai mutaṉilai, īṟu, puṇarcci, cāriyai, iṭainilai, tiripu eṉṉum āṟuṟuppākap pakuttum; iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticaiccol eṉa mūvakaiyāka vakuttum; moḻinūṟku aṭikōliyatu tamiḻē.
poruḷ
tamiḻilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷ naṭaiyiliruntamaiyāl, collilakkaṇattai yaṭuttuc coṟṟoṭarilakkaṇattaik kūṟāviṭiṉum, ceyyuḷilakkaṇattaik kūṟi ataṉpiṉ poruḷi lakkaṇattaik kūṟiyirukkalām. āyiṉ, mutaṉūlāciriyar meypporuḷaṟiñarā yiruntataṉāl, moḻinaṭai etuvāyiṉum poruḷē ataṉ uḷḷīṭeṉṟum, taṉiccoṟkum poruḷuṇ ṭeṉṟum, kaṇṭu, ataṉaiyē colliṟkaṭuttuk kūṟiyatōṭu, ataṟkē ciṟappuk koṭuttuc ceyyuḷai ataṉuḷḷaṭakki, mūṉṟām atikārattaip poruḷatikārameṉap peyariṭṭu iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūliṉ muṭimaṇiyākkiṉār. tiruk kōviliṉ mukamaṇṭapamum iṭaimaṇṭapamum uṇṇā ḻikaiyum pōla, eḻuttum collum poruḷum muṟaiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ciṟantaṉa vākum.
piṟkālattup pāṇṭiya ṉoruvaṉ, eḻuttatikāramum col latikāramum vallāraimaṭṭum talaippaṭṭup poruḷatikāram vallārait talaippaṭātapōtu, puṭaipaṭak kavaṉṟu, eṉṉai? eḻuttuñ collum ārāyvatu poruḷatikārattiṉ poruṭ ṭaṉṟē! poruḷatikāram peṟēmeṉiṉ ivai peṟṟum peṟṟilēm. eṉṟu varuntiyatāka,iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai kūṟiyiruppatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.  itaṉāl, tamiḻaṉ perumaiyaiyum tamiḻiṉ perumaiyaiyum oruṅkē uṇaralām.  ēṉaimoḻiyilakkaṇa nūlārellām eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉṉum nāloṭu niṉṟuviṭa, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlār maṭṭum yāppiṉ poruḷukkum ilakkaṇam vakuttatu, kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻariṉ oppuyarvaṟṟa akakkaraṇa vaḷarcciyaik kāṭṭum. poruḷilak kaṇam pōlap paṇṭait tamiḻaṉiṉ pulamai nuṇukkattaik kāṭṭum cāṉṟu vēṟetuvumillai.
ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum akampuṟam eṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, avvoṉṟaiyum evvēḻu tiṇaiyāka vakuttiruk kiṉṟār mutaṉūlār.
kūlikkāraṉmutal kōmakaṉ varai ennilaiyarāyiṉum et toḻilarāyiṉum, makkaḷaiyellām āṇṭu naṭattum kuṇam iraṇṭu.  avai kātalum maṟamum. makkaḷellārum āṭavarum peṇṭirumākap paṭaikkappaṭṭiruppatāl, kātal vāḻkkai māperumpālarkku iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum.  poruḷillārkku ivvulaka millaiyātalāl, oru toḻilai mēṟkoḷvatu ellārkkum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. ettoḻililum pōṭṭi toṉṟu toṭṭuc ciṟṟaḷavākavō pēraḷavākavō iruntē vantirukkiṉṟatu.  etirikaḷai veṉṟu vāḻkkai yait tiṟampaṭa naṭatta maṟamum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
iṉi, orē peṇṇaip palar maṇakka virumpupōtu, kātalilēyē maṟamuṅ kalakkiṉṟatu.
ivviru kuṇaṅkaḷuḷḷum allatu kuṇavāḻkkaiyuḷḷum, uḷḷattiṟku mika neruṅkiyatu kātalē.  ātalāl atai akam eṉṟār.  akamallātatu puṟamātaliṉ maṟattaip puṟam eṉṟār.  iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭaiyum vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟiṉum, avai akappakaiyum puṟappakaiyum pōlap pirintu nillātu, akaṅkaiyum puṟaṅkaiyumpōla oṉṟiyē niṟkum.
tamiḻaṉukkut tamiḻ akam; tiraviṭam akappuṟam; āriyam puṟam; cēmiyam puṟappuṟam.  ivvakaik kūṟṟiṉiṉṟu, akam puṟam eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷai oruvāṟuṇaralām.
aracaṉ ellārkkun talaimaiyākavum potuvākavum ellāraiyun taṉṉuḷaṭakkiyum niṟpatāl, aracaṉukkuc coṉṉatu aṉaivarkkuñ coṉṉatāku meṉṟum, kātal vāḻkkaiyilum maṟavāḻkkaiyilum talaiciṟanta nukarcciyum paṭṭaṟivum aracaṉukkē yuṇṭeṉṟuṅ kaṇṭu, ōr iḷavaraca ṉaiyē kātalaṉākavum ōr iḷavaraciyaiyē kātaliyākavuṅ koṇṭu, akapporuḷilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉat tamiḻukkē ciṟanta marapākum.  āyiṉum, itu nāṭaka vaḻakkamum ulakiyal vaḻakkamum kalantatātalāl, ciṟupāṉmai potumakkaḷ kātal vāḻkkaiyum āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappeṟum.
akapporuḷ eṉṉum kātal allatu maṇavāḻkkai, kaikkiḷai (orutalaikkāmam), aintiṇai (irutalaikkāmam), peruntiṇai (poruntākkāmam) eṉa mūvakaiyāka vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  avaṟṟuḷ, ellāvakaiyilum nallateṉa oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭa irutalaikkāmam puṇartal, pirital. iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉṉum aintu uripporuḷākap pirikkappaṭṭum; avai muṟaiyē kuṟiñci pālai mullai neytal marutam ākiya nilaṅkaṭku urimaiyākkappaṭṭum; uḷḷaṉa. ippirippum nilavurimaip paṭuttamum, mutaṉūlā ciriyariṉ neṭuṅkālak kūrṅkavaṉippaiyum nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattaiyum teṟṟeṉak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
talaimaik kuṭimakaṉ
makkaḷ vāḻkkaikku ettuṇaiyō pala poruḷkaḷ vēṇṭiyiruppiṉum, avai yellāvaṟṟuḷḷum iṉṟiyamai yātatu uṇavu oṉṟē.  ataṉāl, uṇavai viḷaivikkum uḻavaṉ ellāruḷḷum uyarntavaṉākiṉṟāṉ. itupaṟṟiyē, tiruvaḷḷuvar tam oppuyarvaṟṟa ulakap potu nūlil, kuṭi ceyalvakaiyiṉ piṉ uḻavatikārattai vaittār.
uṇavaḷippavaṉ
uḻuvār ulakattārk kāṇiyaḵ, tāṟṟā(tu)
eḻuvārai yellām poṟuttu.  (kuṟaḷ.1032)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāl, uḻavar ulakamākiya tērukku akcāṇiyāvar eṉṟuraittār teyvap pulamait tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār. accāṇi yillāta tēr muccāṇum ōṭātu. ceyaṟkai viḷaiviṉṟi, makkaḷiṉam perutta ulakam oru nāḷum naṭaipeṟātu.  uṇavu viḷaittalai ataṉ arumai paṟṟi aḷittal eṉṟatu uyarpu naviṟci.
viruntōmpi vēḷāṇmai ceypavaṉ
viruntu, putumai, putitāy vanta ayalārkku aṉpuṭaṉ uṇavaḷippatē viruntōmpal. vēḷāṇmaiyāvatu piṟarai virumpic ceyyum palvakai yutavi.  ivai yiraṇṭum tāmākavē uṇavai viḷaippavarum talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāyp piṟaraip pēṇip paṇpaṭṭu varupavarumāṉa uḻavar kuṭiyiṉarkkē iyalum.
purappōr koṟṟattāṉ
aracaṉ uṭpaṭa aṉaivarkkum uṇavu viḷaivittum, āḷ viṉaikku vēṇṭum āṟiloru kaṭamai yiṟuttum, pakaiyaracaṉ paṭaiyeṭuttuvariṉ porutu veṉṟum, ūkkamuḷḷa tam vēntaṉ piṟanāṭukaḷai veṉṟu putuviṟal tāyam peṟa utaviyum, aracaṉ veṟṟipeṟak kāraṇamāyiruppavar vēḷāṇ peru makkaḷē.
itaṉāl,
palakuṭai nīḻalun taṅkuṭaikkīḻk kāṇpar
alakuṭai nīḻa lavar.  (kuṟaḷ..1034)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar uḻavarai uyarttuk kūṟiṉar.
uḻaippāḷi
kaṭuṅkōṭaiyilum puyalmāriyilum iravum pakalum uṭalai varutti uḻaippavar uḻavarē. 
uḻuvā rulakattiṟ kāṇi eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil aḵtāṟṟātu eṉṉun toṭarum. cuḻaṉṟu mērp piṉṉa tulakam eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil uḻantum eṉṉum collum, uḻavut toḻiliṉ uḻaippuk kaṭumaiyaik kuṟikkumkuṟippukaḷām.  payirceytalaip pāṭupaṭutal eṉṉum nellaiyuḻavar vaḻakkum iṅkuk karutattakkatu.
taṉṉurimaiyāḷaṉ
taṉ nilattil uḻutu payir ceypavaṉ taṉakkut tāṉē talaivaṉā taliṉ, piṟa toḻilāḷarpōl piṟarmuṉ kaikaṭṭi niṟka vēṇṭiyatum oruvarkku añcavēṇṭiyatum illai.
uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvār maṟ ṟellām
toḻutuṇṭu piṉcel pavar  (kuṟaḷ. 1033)
eṉṟu tamiḻmaṟai kūṟutal kāṇka.
mutaṟṟoḻilāḷi
ulakil mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiyatu uḻavuttoḻilē.  ataṉāl, piṟa toḻilāḷarum ovvoru vakai uḻavarākavē kūṟappaṭuvar.
viṟ paṭaiñarai villēruḻavar eṉṟum, nūṟ pulavaraic col lēruḻavareṉṟum, kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar. kaḷavut toḻilaik kaḷavēr vāḻkkai eṉṟār cīttalaic cāttaṉār. vāḷ maṟavaṉai vāḷuḻavaṉ eṉṟatu tivākaram.
paḻutaṟṟa toḻilāṉ
uḻavut toḻilil oruvakaik kuṟṟamumillai.
āṟṟaṅ karaiyiṉ maramum aracaṟiya
vīṟṟirunta vāḻvum viḻumaṉṟē - ēṟṟam
uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvataṟ koppillai kaṇṭīr
paḻutuṇṭu vēḷōr paṇikku,
eṉṟār aruntamiḻ mūtāṭṭiyār auvaiyār.
uṇmai iṅṅaṉa miruppavum, uḻavut toḻilil pūcci puḻukkaḷum ceṭi koṭikaḷum kollappaṭuvatāl, atai iḻintateṉṟu uyarntōr kaiviṭṭaṉar. eṉṟu āriyap pōli aṟanūlkaḷ kūṟum.

paṇṭai nāḷil, ciṟṟūr vāṇarāyiṉum, pērūr vāṇarāyiṉum, kollaṉ, taccaṉ, kaṟṟaccaṉ, kaṉṉāṉ, taṭṭāṉ, kottaṉ, kuyavaṉ, vaṇṇāṉ, mañcikaṉ (nāvitaṉ), vāṇiyaṉ, pāṇaṉ, paṟaiyaṉ mutaliya patiṉeṇ toḻilāḷarum kuṭimakkaḷ eṉappaṭṭu, uḻavarkkup pakkattuṇaiyāyiruntu tattam toḻilaic ceytu, katiraṭikkaḷattil avvakkālattuk kūlattaik kūliyākap peṟṟu vāḻntamai. ak kālattuk 
kūṭṭaravu (camutāya) amaippil uḻavarē talaimaiyā yiruntatai uṇarttum,
cuḻaṉṟum ērppiṉṉa tulakam ataṉāl
uḻantum uḻavē talai.  (kuṟaḷ.1031)
eṉṉum kuṟaṭkaruttum itaṉālēyē viḷaṅkum.
poṅkal eṉpatu, uḻavar paṇṭikai. aṟuvaṭaiyāṉa piṉ, putiri eṉṉum putu nellaic camaittu naṉṟiyaṟivuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvattiṟkup paṭaittuṇpatē poṅkal.  nīrvaḷam mikunta pāṅkaril muppū viḷaiyum. (pūveṉpatu veḷḷāmai) nīr vaḷa millā iṭaṅkaḷil māri nīrttēkkattāl oru veḷḷāmai tāṉ viḷaikka muṭiyum.  ākavē, mārkaḻi allatu tai māta aṟuvaṭaiyē nāṭeṅkum nikaḻum. ātalāl, taip poṅkal perum poṅkal eṉappeṟum.
cenneṟ paccariciyaip perumpālun taviṭu pōkkāmal cari nīr vaittuc camaittu, paruppukkuḻampuṭaṉ uṇpatu poṅkal marapu. paruppuk kēṭkum paccaricic cōṟu, ceruppuk kēṭkum cittirai māta veyil! eṉpatu paḻamoḻi, poṅkaṟ pāṉaiyil ulai poṅki vantavuṭaṉ ciṟitu vaḻiya viṭuvatu poṅkal aṭaiyāḷamākak karutap peṟum. ituvē uḻutuṇpār poṅkal.
akkāraṭalai (carukkaraip poṅkal), akkāravaṭicil (caruk karai neyppoṅkal), neyppoṅkal, miḷakup poṅkal, veṇ poṅkal mutaliya cuvaimikka poṅkal vakaikaḷel lām uḻuvit tuṇpārum celvarumāṉa piṟar poṅkal.  pāl poṅka vaippatu iṭaiyar poṅkal. callik kaṭṭeṉṟum mañcu viraṭṭeṉṟum māṭuviṭutal eṉṟum naṭai peṟṟu varuvatu paṇṭai mullai nila vaḻakkam. (ēṟutaḻuval).

talaivaṉ oḻukkam
ekkūṭṭattaiyum naṭattum talaivaṉukku oḻukkam iṉṟi yamaiyāta tākum. ikkaṭamaiyai ikkālattār kavaṉippatillai; uṇarvatumillai. talaivaṉ cemmaiyāyiruntāltāṉ avaṉaip piṉpaṟṟuvōrum cemmaiyāyiruppar. piṟar oṉṟaic ceyyavēṇṭumeṉṟu collukiṟavaṉ allatu virumpukiṟavaṉ tāṉum ataic ceyya vēṇṭum.  collu 
kiṟavaṉ muntic ceytāltāṉ kēṭpōrum pintic ceyvar. colva teḷitu; ceyvatu aritu. colvataic ceytu kāṭṭiṉāl tāṉ col valiyuṟum. kēṭpōrum kaikkoḷvar. 
periyōraip piṉpaṟṟutalē ciṟiyōr iyalpātaliṉ, periyōrum talaivarum takāc ceyalai nīkkit takka ceyalaiyē ceytal vēṇṭum.
talaivaṉukkuriya peyarkaḷuḷ cemmal eṉpatu oṉṟu.  itu cem(paṇpuc coṟkaḷai mai vikuti koṭuttuk kūṟuvatu ilakkaṇavāciriyar vaḻakku. avvikutiyiṉṟic collai maṭṭum kūṟuvatu moḻi nūlāciriyar vaḻakku eṉavaṟika). eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirintatu.  cemmaiyāy oḻukupavaṉ cemmal. iccollāl, ettalaivaṉukkum oḻukkam iṉṟiyamaiyātatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.  oḻukkattil. ciṟiyōr periyōraip piṉ paṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttup paṟṟiyē, piṟarkku ollum vakaiyil cellum vāyellām naṉmai ceytaṟku, oppuravu (allatu) oppuravoḻukutal eṉṟu peyar. tavam(1):
tavam - tapa
uṟṟanōy nōṉṟal uyirkkuṟukaṇ ceyyāmai
aṟṟē tavattiṟ kuru.  (kuṟaḷ. 261).
uṟṟa nōyuḷ, veyiliṉ vemmaiyum pacitākattāluṇṭākum uṭampiṉ vemmaiyum aṭaṅkum.  (va.va: 174).
tavam (2):

taḻuvutoṭarum taḻāt toṭarum
vēṟṟumai yaimmuta lāṟā malvaḻi
toḻilpaṇ puvamai yummai yaṉmoḻi
eḻuvāy viḷiyī reccamuṟ ṟiṭaiyuri
taḻuvu toṭaraṭuk keṉavī rēḻē   (naṉ.152)
itaṉ uraiyil, vai.mu. caṭakōparāmānujācāriyārum, nr.kiUZzkhrhÇahU«,
ivai taḻuvutoṭar eṉavē, av viruvaḻiyilum, taḻāt toṭa rum cila uḷa eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. nīrkkuṭam eṉpatu nīraiyuṭaiya kuṭam eṉa viritalāl, nīr eṉpatu uṭaiya eṉpatait taḻuvik kuṭam eṉpataip poruḷāl taḻuvāmal toṭarntataṉāl, itu taḻāttoṭarākiya vēṟṟumait tokai.............marattaic cāttaṉ veṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatil, marattai eṉpatu veṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatait taḻuvic cāttaṉ eṉpataip poruḷāl taḻuvāmal toṭarntataṉāl, itu taḻāttoṭarākiya vēṟṟumai viri.  curaiyāḻa ammi mitappa eṉpatu curaimitappa ammi āḻa eṉak kūṭṭappaṭutalāl, curai eṉpatu āḻa eṉpataiyum ammi eṉpatu mitappa eṉpataiyum poruḷāl taḻuvāmal toṭarn taṉa.  ivai taḻāttoṭa rākiya alvaḻippuṇarcci.  taḻāt toṭarāvatu nilaimoḻi yāṉatu varumoḻiyōṭu poruṭ poruttamuṟat taḻuvāta toṭar. poruṭ porutta muṟat taḻuviya toṭar taḻuvu toṭar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
taḻuvutoṭar, taḻāttoṭar eṉpaṉa ilakkaṇap poruttamuṟat taḻuvukiṉṟa toṭar. ïy¡fz¥ bghU¤jKw¤ jGthj bjhl® v‹nw bghUŸgL k‹¿., poruṭ poruttamuṟat taḻuvukiṉṟa toṭar, poruṭ poruttamuṟat taḻuvāta toṭar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭā.
cūttirattuṭ kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉāṉku toṭarkaḷuḷ, mutaṟ patiṉmūṉṟum taḻuvutoṭarum, kaṭaiyoṉṟum taḻāttoṭaru mākum eṉpataik kuṟittaṟkē, aṭukkut toṭarkku muṉ taḻuvutoṭar eṉpataik kūṟip pirittaṉar. ēṉaip patiṉmūṉṟum taḻuvutoṭar eṉavē, iṟutiyoṉṟum ataṟketirākiya taḻāttoṭareṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum. meymmayakkuc cūttirattil oṉṟai uṭaṉilai eṉṟatiṉāl iṉṉoṉṟu vēṟṟunilai yeṉṟaṟiyappaṭṭāṟpōla. itu cūttiramātaliṉ coṟcuruṅ kaṟku iṅṅaṉam yākkappaṭṭatu.
vēṟṟumaittokai mutaliya āṟu tokaikaḷum, eḻuvāyt toṭar mutaliya eṭṭut toṭarkaḷum, nilaimoḻiyum varu moyiyum ilakkaṇattiṟ campantappaṭṭiruppatum, aṭukkut toṭar oṉṟumaṭṭum campantappaṭātu nilaimoḻi varu moḻikaḷ taṉittaṉi niṟpatum eṭuttukkāṭṭu vāyilāyk kaṇṭukoḷka.
e-ṭu.	cōṟuṇṭāṉ - vēṟṟumait tokai - taḻuvutoṭar
		irāmaṉ vantāṉ - eḻuvāyt toṭar - taḻuvutoṭar
		pāmpu pāmpu - taḻāttoṭar
nīrkkuṭam eṉpatu nīraiyuṭaiya kuṭam eṉṟu viriyumpōtu, nīrai eṉpatu uṭaiya eṉpataiyum, uṭaiya eṉpatu kuṭam eṉpataiyum taḻuvukiṉṟamai kāṇka. nīrkkuṭam eṉṟu tokaiyāy niṟkum pōtum nīr eṉpatu (tokkuniṟkum) uṭaiya eṉṉuñ collaiyē avāy nilaiyāl taḻuvum.  allākkāl, vantēṉ eṉṉum tokai vākkiyattil nāṉ eṉṉum eḻuvāy avāynilaiyāl varuvikkappaṭāmai kāṇka. cāttaṉ marattai veṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatē iyalpāṉa tamiḻurai muṟaiyātaliṉ, marattaic cāttaṉ eṉpatu oru payaṉ nōkkiya muṟai māṟṟākum.  cāttaṉ marattai eṉpatil cāttaṉ eṉpatu eḻuvāyum, marattai eṉpatu eḻuvāyiṉ toḻilai yaṭaikiṉṟa ceyappaṭuporuḷāyum eṅṅaṉam iyaiyumō, aṅṅaṉamē marattaic cāttaṉ eṉpatilum iyaiyum.
vanta cāttaṉ makaṉ eṉṉun toṭaril vanta eṉpatu makaṉait taḻuvumāyiṉ, aḵtu iṭaippiṟavaral eṉap potuviyalil vēṟilakkaṇamākak kūṟappaṭutaliṉ īṇṭaik kēlātākum.  ēṟpiṉ, potuviyaliṉ kūṟum iṭaippiṟavaral kūṟiyatu kūṟalāyk kuṟṟan taṅkum.
curai yāḻa ammi mitappa eṉpatu ceyyuṭkuriya moḻi māṟṟup poruḷkōḷātaliṉ, atuvum īṇṭaik kēlātu.
āṅkilattilum taḻuvutoṭar taḻāttoṭarkaṭku ilakkaṇap porutta muṇmai yiṉmaikaḷaiyē ilakkaṇamākak koḷvar.
Compound words (toṭar moḻikaḷ) are subdivided into two classes:
I.  Unrelated (taḻāttoṭar) or those in which the simple words are not connected together by any grammatical relation. (These have been also called juxta-positional).
II. Related, (jGî bjhl®) or those in which there is some grammatical relation between the component words.(These have been also called syntactical.)   (Nesfield: Book IV)
patiṉāṉku toṭarkaḷilum taḻuvutoṭarum taḻāttoṭarum uṇṭeṉṟu naṉṉūlār koṇṭiruppiṉ, atai oru taṉic cūttirattil viḷaṅka vaittiruppārēyaṉṟi, ittuṇai uyt tuṇarviṟkum mayakkattiṟkum iṭan tantirār eṉka. (cen tamiḻc celvi maṭaṅkal 1936)
taḻaiyuṭai
taḻaikoṇṭu cēṟal, cantaṉattaḻai takāteṉṟu maṟuttal, taḻai etirtal, taḻai ēṟpittal, taḻai viruppuraittal mutaliya akapporuḷ tuṟaikaḷ makkaḷ kuṟiñci nilaiyil taḻaiyuṭai aṇintiruntataik kāṭṭum.
taḻaiyuṭaiyiṉ piṉ maravuriyāṭai aṇiyappaṭṭatu.  maravurikkuc cīrai eṉṟu peyar.  appeyarē cīlai, cēlai eṉat tirintu iṉṟu paruttiyāṭaiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  (col. 19)

itaṟku vaṭavar kāṭṭum tru eṉṉum mūlam poruntāmai kāṇka. tru = citaṟu.

(2) 	vaṭṭattāṟu,
(3) 	vāl tāṟu,
eṉa mūvakai.  moṭṭait tāṟāvatu, cuṟṟilum vēṭṭiyait tiraittu, muḻaṅkāṟkuk kīḻ mikutiyāy allatu oru ciṟitum vēṭṭi toṅkātapaṭi muṉpuṟa muntiyai allatu aṭip pākattai irukāṟkumiṭaiyē pāyccip piṉṉāl iṟukac cerukutal. vaṭṭattāṟāvatu: oru puṟa muntiyaik kaṇukkāl varai irukālum maṟaiyat toṅkaviṭṭu, maṟṟoru puṟamuntiyai irukāṟkumiṭaiyē pāyccip piṉṉāl iṟukac cerukutal. vāltāṟāvatu: vaṭṭattāṟṟoṭu piṉṉāl vāl viṭṭuk kaṭṭutalām.  itu oṟṟai vāltāṟu: iraṭṭai vāltāṟu eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.  muṉpakkattiliruntu oru kīḻ muntiyai maṭṭum piṉṉāṟ koṇṭupōy iṭakkāṟ pakkamēṉum valakkāṟ pakkamēṉum naṭuvilēṉum vālviṭṭuc cerukuvatu oṟṟai vāltāṟu eṉpatām.  iru kīḻ muntiyaiyum muṉpōṟ koṇṭu pōy valakkāṟ pakkam oṉṟum iṭakkāṟ pakkamoṉṟumāka iruvāl viṭṭuc cerukuvatu iraṭṭai vāltāṟu eṉpatām.  moṭṭait tāṟṟukku nāṉku muḻamum, vaṭṭat tāṟṟukku aintu muḻamum, vāl tāṟṟukku āṟu muḻamum, āṭai vēṇṭum. ivai, muṟaiyē, oṉṟiṉoṉṟu matippāṉavai.  ātalāl, kallāp potumakkaḷum kallāp perumakkaḷum kaṟṟa perumakkaḷum ivaṟṟai, muṟaiyē, kaiyāḷvar.  itu toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkam.
kallāp potumakkaḷ perumpālum uḻaippāḷikaḷātaliṉ,  matippu vēṇṭāmaiyōṭu uḻaippu vacatiyum moṭṭait tāṟṟaiyē avarkkuriyatākkum.  ūkkamāy viṉaiceyyum uḻaippāḷikaḷ, ciṟappāka uḻavar, moṭṭaittāṟ ṟuṭaiyum mikaiyeṉak karuti nīrccīlai (kōvaṇam) yoṉṟē yaṇintu viṉaiceyvar.
evvuḻaippu viṉaiyaiyum ūkkamāyc ceyya muṉaiyiṉ, araiyāṭaiyai iṟukak kaṭṭikkoḷvatu māntar iyalpu.  ataṉāl,
kuṭiceyval eṉṉum oruvaṟkut teyvam
maṭitaṟṟut tāṉmun tuṟum.  (kuṟaḷ. 1023)
eṉṟu, teyvattiṉ mēlum taṟṟuṭuttalai ēṟṟik kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar.  oru viṉaiyai muṉaintu ceyvataṟku araiyil āṭaiyai iṟukkikkaṭṭiṉāl maṭṭum pōtātu: kīḻp pāycciyuṅ kaṭṭa vēṇṭum.
taṟṟuṭuttaleṉiṉum tāṟu pāyccik kaṭṭuta leṉiṉum oṉṟē.  taṟutal - iṟukak kaṭṭutal allatu uṭuttutal. taṟṟuṭuppatu tāṟu. (mutaṉilai nīṇṭa toḻilāku peyar). taṟu-tāṟu. x.neh.: iṟu - īṟu, uṟu - ūṟu.
taṟukkaṇittal:
(1)	uṇavupporuḷ iṟukutal,
(2)	catai allatu puṇ kāyttuppōtal,
(3)	paḻaṅ kaṉṟippōtal.
vaḻi naṭaikkukkūṭa, cuṟṟik kaṭṭuvatiṉum tāṟupāyccik kaṭṭutalē vacatiyāṉatu: allākkāl, kāl taṭṭum, veṭṭum: tuṇi pitirum, kiḻiyum.
ki.mu.3000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ mēṉāṭukaḷil tuṇi neyyappaṭa villai yeṉṟum,intiyāviṉiṉṟē ēṟṟumatiyāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum, vayavar cāṉ mārcal (Sir John Marshall) kūṟukiṉṟār.  itaṟkuc cāṉṟāka, paruttiyum pañcum tuṇiyum paṟṟiya tamiḻp peyarkaḷ iṉṟum mēṉāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

tāṇṭakam
26 eḻuttiṟku mēṟpaṭāta 4 aṭikaḷaik koṇṭa pāviṉam maṇṭilam eṉṟum, attokaikku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷai yuṭaiya 4 aṭikaḷaik koṇṭa pāviṉam tāṇṭakam eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.  maṇṭila (virutta) eḻuttaḷavait tāṇṭic celvatu tāṇṭakam. tirunāvukkaracar tāṇṭakaṅkaḷuḷ māperumpālaṉa maṇṭila aḷavē koṇṭiruppatāl,

tāmarai
tāmarai - tāmarasa
tum = cem. tum - tumpu - tuppu = civappu, pavaḻam, arakku.
tumpu - tumparam = civappu, civappāṉa attippaḻam.
tum - (tumar) - tuvar = civappu, pavaḻam, kāvi, tuvarai, tuvarppu (kācukkaṭṭi). tuvarttal = civattal, tuvarppuc cuvaiyātal.
tuvar - tuvarai = cempayaṟu, ceppukkōṭṭai nakar.
tuvar - tukir = pavaḻam
tumar - tamar - tāmaram = cempu. tāmaram - tāmarai = cemmuḷari, muḷari.
tāmarasa eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭivait tāma+rasa eṉṟu pakuttup pakal muḷari eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar.  āyiṉ, tāma eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlamākak koḷḷum tama eṉṉuñ coṟkō iruḷ allatu iravu eṉṟutāṉ poruḷ.  iṉi, ikkuḻaṟupaṭaiyaip perukkaṟkut tam eṉpatai aṭi mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar.  ataṟkut tiṇaṟutal allatu tikkumuk kāṭutal eṉpatu poruḷ iṅṅaṉam vaṭavar tiṇaṟit tiṇṭāṭuvatellām, vēṇṭumeṉṟē meymmaiyaip puṟakkaṇittup poymmaiyai mēṟkoḷvataṉ viḷaivē.
tāmaram - tāmpara 
tumparam -utumpara, uṭumpara (a.vē.).  (va.va:176).
tāmarai: tum - tumar - tuvar = civappu, tuvar - tuvarai = cempayaṟu. tumar - tamar (tāmarai) - tāmarai = cenniṟa nīrppūvakai.  iccol iṉṟu taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiḻantu, avvakaiyiṉ iruniṟa malarkkum potuppeyarāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.  tāmarai - tāmarasa(va.)   (ti.ma:743).
tāymoḻik karaṇam
tēva moḻiyeṉa oṉṟiṉmaiyālum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyatum āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamum āṉatiṉālum, āriyak karaṇam tamiḻaṉukku māṉakkēṭṭai viḷaippataṉālum, pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu varumuṉ ellāt tamiḻar maṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻilēyē naṭaipeṟṟu vantatiṉālum, ovvoru tamiḻaṉum tamiḻilēyē karaṇam ceyvittal vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇaṉ tamiḻaṉpaṉākit tamiḻiṟ karaṇam ceyya icaiyiṉ atai ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām.  tamiḻiṟ karaṇam ceyviyātavaṉ tamiḻaṉākāṉ.
ovvoru tūya tamiḻaṉum, taṉ vīṭṭuk karaṇattai maṭṭu maṉṟip piṟar vīṭṭuk karaṇattaiyum tamiḻiṟ ceyvikka muyaṟci ceytal vēṇṭum.  vaṭamoḻiyil naṭakkum karaṇattiṟkuc cellutal kūṭātu.  (ta.ti.57).

nāyum palakaiyum varūuṅ kālai

ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi akaram nilaiyum  (. 374)
eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷāl (cūttiraṅkaḷāl) aṟiyalām.
paramapatam, ciluvaittāyam eṉa vaḻaṅkum kuṟukkuk kaṭṭat tāyam, ākiya iraṇṭum, perumpāṉmaiyāka vaḻaṅkum peṇṭir tāya viḷaiyāṭṭukkaḷām.
tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻaratē
oru kātalaṉ taṉ kātalikku, allatu oru maṇamakaṉ taṉ maṇamakaḷukku, allatu kaṇavaṉ taṉ maṉaivikku, kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvataṉ vāyilāy, avaḷait taṉ vāḻkkaittuṇaivi eṉṟu piṟarkkuk kāṭṭuvatu, tamiḻakattut toṉṟutoṭṭa vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col maṇattal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu, tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam paṟṟiyē. X® ïisahid mšyJ ïisahis neh¡», ‘ Ú ahiu¡ f£l¥ngh»whŒ? eṉṟu kēṭpatu ulaka vaḻakku.  maṇamakkaḷ iruvaruḷḷum tāli kaṭṭuvatu maṇamakaṉum avaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭuvatu maṇamakaḷumā yiruppiṉum, kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col maṇattal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkattalaippaṭṭapiṉ, atu iruvarkkum potuvāṉa collāyiṟṟu.
mutaṟkālattil, makkaḷ kuṭumpappiriviṉṟik kulaṅkulamāy allatu tokuti tokutiyāy vāḻntuvanta nilaiyil, oru kulattup peṇṭir, perumpālum kulattalaivaṉ maṉaiviyar nīṅkalāka, akkulattu āṭavar aṉaivarkkum potu maṉaivi yarākavē iruntu vantaṉar. piṉpu nākarikan tōṉṟit taṉimaṉai viyar ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, oruvaṉ taṉ maṉaiviyai allatu maṉaivi yarai vēṟākap pirittu vaittaṟku tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu.  īkaiyariya iḻaiyaṇi makaḷir eṉpatu  (puṟam 127), tamiḻappeṇṭir tāliyaṇiyum vaḻakkattaik kuṟikkum caṅkanūṟ cāṉṟām.
muntukālattil, ciṟappākak kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻntu vantapōtu, ilaiyaiyum pūvaiyum āṭaiyaṇiyāka aṇivatu tamiḻar vaḻakkam.  nālvakaiyilaiyuḷḷum ōlaiyeṉpatu uṟutiyāṉatātaliṉ, paṉaṅkuruttāl kātaṇi kaḻuttaṇi mutaliya palvēṟu aṇikaḷai avar ceytu koṇṭaṉar.  cēraṉ paṉam pūmālaiyai aṭaiyāḷa mālaiyākak koṇṭirunta tiṉālum, ōlaiyaik kuṟikkum tōṭu eṉṉum col iṉṟum kātaṇippeyarāy vaḻaṅkutalālum, kātil ōlaiyaṇivatāl ōlaippaḷḷi yeṉṟa peyar peṟṟa oru paḷḷi vakuppār  iṉṟiruttalālum, tāḻttappaṭṭa cila kulattup peṇṭir ōlaiyaiyē kātil aṇivatālum, paḻaṅkālattil ōlaiyaṇi kaḷaik kātilum kaḻuttilum tamiḻap peṇṭir aṇintaṉar eṉpatil, eḷḷaḷavum viyappiṟkiṭamiṉṟām.  kātiṟpōṉṟē kaḻuttilum paṉaṅkuruttaṇi aṇiyappaṭṭamai.
